=========== Conqueror-2-001.html — MAIN MENU ===========

MAIN MENU

MAIN MENU

=========== Conqueror-2-002.html — 1.  Conqueror Main Menu ===========

1. Conqueror Main Menu

1. Conqueror Main Menu

All areas of Conqueror can be accessed by the Main Menu, which contains modules and sub-modules. Conqueror Main Menu can be divided as follows: central main menu, TopNav and external frame. The central part of the screen contains five main modules whose order cannot be changed or modified. Click on any module to open the corresponding sub-module. E.g. The operator wants to sell an item, POS (Point of Sale) > Income and then select an option from those available.

The TopNav and the external frame of the Main Menu are always visible while using Conqueror. The upper bar displays general information such as the time and date, help functions, shifts, and the operator's name currently logged in. On the left side of the screen, multiple modules can be kept open while and called back if necessary, as described in the Task Holding section. There is no limit to the number of modules that can be left open, as a scroll bar appears as soon as the number of modules open exceeds seven. Quick buttons are located on the right-side; operators can add up to 8 programmable buttons (plus Bowling Mode, which is a fixed Quick button), personalizing the system for either user or terminal, making frequently used modules easily accessible.

MAIN MENU > Conqueror Main Menu

=========== Conqueror-2-003.html — 2.  Main Menu ===========

2. Main Menu

2. Main Menu

2.1 Quick Access 

The most frequently used functions can be added to the Home
> Quick Access
module. To add a module to the Quick Access
menu, right-click on the module and click on Add Module to Quick Access
. It is also possible to customize the icon by choosing the Add Module to Quick Access and Customize
option; use this option to rename it and add a new picture from those available. Operators can add up to 54 Quick Access items; each Quick Access module contains max nine items meaning that it will be possible to add up to six Quick Access modules. At any time, operators can modify the order of the items within the Quick Access module(s). Click on the module you want to move, select Change Quick Access item order and use the dialog window to move the items from one Quick Access tab to another. The first nine items will be inside the first Quick Access module. 

To delete a Quick Access item, right-click and choose the Remove Module from Quick Access
option. To apply Quick Access options as user preference instead of terminal setting, see Diversified User Settings.

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Quick Access

=========== Conqueror-2-004.html — 2.2  Front Desk ===========

2.2 Front Desk

2.2 Front Desk

The Front Desk
section contains modules fundamental to manage aspects of the Bowling Center utilized daily, related to customers, such as Lane Status, through which the operator interacts with the lanes.

In this section, the operator has control over modules regarding leagues, tournaments, Time Games, Time Tracking, lockers, frequent bowlers (members of the Bowling Center), Shifts and the ability to send messages and images to lanes.

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Front Desk

=========== Conqueror-2-005.html — 2.3  POS ===========

2.3 POS

2.3 POS

The Point of Sale section contains all modules dealing with sales, deposits and refunds. POS can also be accessed through other Conqueror modules, such as Lane Status and the Booking System.

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > POS

=========== Conqueror-2-006.html — 2.4  Back Office ===========

2.4 Back Office

2.4 Back Office

The Back-Office
section is utilized by employees working with bookings, reports (the general administration/management of a Center), Shifts, system logs or the Trouble Call System (dealing with technical work on the lanes).

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Back Office

=========== Conqueror-2-007.html — 2.5  Setup ===========

2.5 Setup

2.5 Setup

This section deals with all Conqueror related settings, from setting up individual terminals, to adapting the Conqueror system to suit the whole Bowling Center. Technical settings as well as settings regarding prices of items sold in the Center and tax rates used can all be adjusted through the various modules in the Setup
section.

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-008.html — 2.6  Utilities ===========

2.6 Utilities

2.6 Utilities

Conqueror provides utility programs to perform backup tasks and to resolve emergency situations that block activity in the Bowling Center, by restoring activity. Most of the contents herein are utilized only by the technician when installing Conqueror or when upgrading the system.

Windows Access

Utilities > Windows

Through this module, the Windows applications Calculator
, Notepad
, Web Browser
, Send E-mail
, Control Panel
, Printer Setup
and DOS Prompt
can be accessed by simply pressing the appropriate option instead of closing Conqueror to use them.

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Utilities

=========== Conqueror-2-009.html — 2.7  Search Bar ===========

2.7 Search Bar

2.7 Search Bar

The search bar allows Bowling Center operators to quickly find menu items. It starts searching when there are at least three letters in the input field.

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Search Bar

=========== Conqueror-2-010.html — 2.8  Sticky Notes ===========

2.8 Sticky Notes

2.8 Sticky Notes

Sticky notes gadget is a notes application included in Conqueror. It is displayed only when the operator is on Home > Quick Access menu. Sticky Notes can be removed but cannot be moved and pinned anywhere else on the screen; therefore, they will always be on the right-side of the main screen. The notes stretch with the text, once the sticky note reaches the bottom of the screen, it will no longer be possible to add text. Even if the operator disables this function, the number of items on the main menu will not be affected by it and continue to be nine. 

MAIN MENU > Main Menu > Sticky Notes

=========== Conqueror-2-011.html — 3.  TopNav and External Frame ===========

3. TopNav and External Frame

3. TopNav and External Frame

The external frame of the Main Menu window displays general information regardless of the user's position within the program. It is possible to personalize the frame for a specific user or terminal, through Terminal Setup.

MAIN MENU > TopNav and External Frame

=========== Conqueror-2-012.html — 3.1  TopNav bar ===========

3.1 TopNav bar

3.1 TopNav bar

Version in use

The version currently in use is visible at the top of the window.

Cloud Connection

Help on-line

Help on-line provides the user with all available information on specific subject matter through accurately organized links to relevant sections, an index, search section,, and favorites list.

The contextual help guide (Tooltips) connects the user to the exact part of the manual on the relative subject, displaying a panel that occupies only part of the screen, providing clear and complete indications.

Tooltips

The yellow question mark is Conqueror's contextual help guide. Click on the yellow question mark and then on the item for which help details are required. Having done so, it is possible to navigate within the tooltip area by scrolling. Using the arrows at the base of the page or by clicking on the links (underlined words), the operator can move out of the help section currently in use to another relevant section. To change the size of this help guide, the borders can be adjusted by using the drag and drop method.

Online User Manual

Clicking on the green question mark opens the Conqueror User Manual pertaining to the part of Conqueror currently in use. Here it is possible to navigate within the Conqueror User Manual independently of the Conqueror module being utilized.

Navigating the On-line User Manual

Help online is easily navigated through the Contents tree on the left. The main window of the Help on Line manual can be explored by scrolling, using the arrows at the base of the screen, or through the links (pressing on an underlined word takes the operator to the relevant place in the manual).

Through the Contents
tab, the various sections of the Conqueror help manual are divided into a tree format for easy identification and can be opened section by section, clicking on the title of interest.
Clicking on the active links takes the operator to the specific area in the manual that deals with that subject matter.

On the left of the main Help online window are another three tabs that allow a more direct navigation of Conqueror Help online.

A list of keywords, buttons, and labels can be found under the Index
tab, whereby double-clicking on a word displays the paragraph containing that word.
To search for a specific section or keyword, press the Search
tab and enter a keyword in the search space, and press either Enter
on the keyboard or List Topics
. Any possible matches for the search criteria entered are displayed underneath. Select the desired section by double-clicking on it or by selecting it and pressing Display
.
In the Favorites
tab, it is possible to maintain a list of the most useful and most utilized sections of the instruction manual by first accessing the desired section through one of the aforementioned methods and then pressing Add
at the base of the tab section. To recall one of the existing favorite sections, select it and press Display
(follow the same procedure to Remove
items from the list).

To visualize the online user manual on a full screen, press Hide
to hide the tabs and trees on the left. Having done so, the button is replaced with Show
to restore the tree.

Pressing Print
proposes the option to print only the selected topic or the selected heading and all subtopics, choose the desired printout and press OK
.

Shift Check

The color of the button under the clock reveals the status of the Shift in progress (or others); the button is white if a Shift is open and red if not. Click on the button to open the Shift
window where Shifts can be opened, closed, and parked, and information on specific Shifts can be displayed or made into reports.

Urgent Tasks

Bowling Center Operators get a notification every time they have to validate a picture; there's a call from the virtual waiter or orders from lanes. A red dot with the number of notifications appears on the bell every time a notification pops up. Once opened, notifications are displayed from the most recent to the oldest one. 

Time and Date

The clock in the upper right-hand corner displays the system time and is used as a common reference for all Conqueror terminals connected, thus avoiding any synchronization problems. It is possible to choose a digital clock to be displayed in the Terminal Setup. The current date is under the clock.

User

The name of the operator currently logged in is displayed in the top-left corner of the window. Identify yourself every time you are using Conqueror or starting their Shift, or replacing a colleague. Users can use it to log in using a profile from those available or, in the alternative, can change the password. 

MAIN MENU > TopNav and External Frame > TopNav bar

=========== Conqueror-2-013.html — 3.2  External Frame ===========

3.2 External Frame

3.2 External Frame

Bowling Mode

This area indicates the current Bowling Day and which Bowling Mode is currently effective.

Click on this field to shortcut to the Bowling Mode
module.

Quick Buttons

It is possible to choose up to eight programmable displayed on the right-hand side of the screen, personalizing the system for either user or terminal in making frequently used modules easily accessible.

Task Holding

Main Menu and Return Tabs

There are two tabs at the bottom right-hand side of the screen.

Pressing the first tab takes the user back to the Main Menu, while the returning arrow allows the user to return to the previously viewed open screen.

Task Holding Tabs

Up to 7 different modules can be held open at once, which can then be recalled through the tabs on the far-left-hand side of the desktop.

Each tab has individual colors and icons for quick and easy recognition.

To task hold, press the M
tab while in the module that is to be kept open. This takes the operator back to the Main Menu without closing the other module.

MAIN MENU > TopNav and External Frame > External Frame

=========== Conqueror-2-014.html — 3.3  Stop Button ===========

3.3 Stop Button

3.3 Stop Button

The desktop STOP button is Conqueror's built-in safety mechanism to be pushed at any time to cut off all power to the pinsetters, stopping play immediately in emergency situations until the Reset
button is pressed.

A child running onto a lane could present an emergency, in which the operator would stop play in that lane by pressing STOP
. To have the STOP
button present during Conqueror use, select the Stop Button
option in the Terminal Setup. When the Close Stop Button
option in Terminal Setup is deselected, the STOP
button icon remains on the computer screen even after Conqueror has been closed. Selecting this option closes the STOP
button when closing Conqueror.

There is also a STOP button that functions in the same way on the lanes so that customers also have this safety option if they recognize a dangerous situation on the lanes.

MAIN MENU > TopNav and External Frame > Stop Button

=========== Conqueror-2-015.html — 4.  User Messages ===========

4. User Messages

4. User Messages

The Conqueror system can transmit useful information to the user and alert the user to a situation requiring input.

To communicate with the operator, the system proposes messages that, according to urgency, are viewed on-screen:

A reminder appears (upon log on or according to urgency) at the base of the screen informing users of any new/unviewed user messages regarding Conqueror. The operator then goes to the User Messages
module to view the User Messages.
If the message is of considerable importance, the module opens automatically, and an exclamation mark highlights the message.

In any case, all messages can be viewed in the User Messages
module by following the path Back Office > User Messages.

MAIN MENU > User Messages

=========== Conqueror-2-016.html — 4.1  Marking User Messages ===========

4.1 Marking User Messages

4.1 Marking User Messages

When the operator has seen the message and noted its content, clicking on the Done
button checks the message as having been viewed, whereby the time and date viewed are displayed in the upper section of the window under the message along with the user name.

If Security is deactivated, the system is unable to recognize the user, thus indicating Completed
.

To go back and unmark the message, select the message and re-click on Done
, where the information Unmarked
and the time and date of the action are added under the message.

MAIN MENU > User Messages > Marking User Messages

=========== Conqueror-2-017.html — 4.2  Deleting a User Message ===========

4.2 Deleting a User Message

4.2 Deleting a User Message

Press Delete
to permanently erase the selected message.

To perform this action, the Access to the System Log and Delete User Messages
privilege is required.

MAIN MENU > User Messages > Deleting a User Message

=========== Conqueror-2-018.html — 4.3  Table ===========

4.3 Table

4.3 Table

In the table (the lower part of the screen), messages are ordered chronologically according to important messages that have not been viewed, important messages that have been viewed, and all other messages.

If the message is checked, it has been dealt with,
if marked with an asterisk, the message is yet to be seen,
if the message is marked with an exclamation mark, it is of great importance.

MAIN MENU > User Messages > Table

=========== Conqueror-2-019.html — 5.  Utilities ===========

5. Utilities

5. Utilities

Conqueror provides utility programs to perform backup tasks and to resolve emergency situations that block activity in the Bowling Center by restoring activity.

Most of the contents herein are utilized only by the technician when installing Conqueror or when performing an upgrade of the system. However, if technical problems occur, the Bowling Center operator follows Technical Support instructions telephonically, utilizing the following modules.

MAIN MENU > Utilities

=========== Conqueror-2-020.html — 5.1  About Conqueror ===========

5.1 About Conqueror

5.1 About Conqueror

Utilities > About Conqueror

The About Conqueror
module displays details pertaining to the Bowling Center and technical information regarding Conqueror and the terminal on which Conqueror is installed.

The About
tab in this section contains Conqueror version information, while the System Information
tab contains operating system and hardware information of the terminal on which Conqueror is installed. This section is consulted when technical support is required - the technician might require the details contained here.

MAIN MENU > Utilities > About Conqueror

=========== Conqueror-2-021.html — 5.2  Database ===========

5.2 Database

5.2 Database

Database Backup

Utilities > Database Backup

The option to backup all current Conqueror data at any given time may be useful in order to send data to the Technical Support team for analyzing in problem situations, and where problems occur, the data from the backup can be recalled through performing a Restore.

Busy Centers that do not wish to have the system down for the time it takes to perform an automatic backup every day can perform them manually at a more convenient time.

To perform a backup manually, enter the Database Backup
module and press OK
.

If the Bowling Center is open 24 hours a day, database optimization may take too long, delaying system access,, and should therefore be deselected. Every now and then,, optimization must be carried out anyhow for the efficiency of the database.

For settings on Automatic Backups, see the Backup
tab in Center Setup.

Database Restore

Utilities > Database Restore

Through the Database Restore
module, it is possible to recall all Conqueror data copied in the last backup performed.

To restore a backup, all terminals using Conqueror will be shut down. Press Yes
to proceed, and a panel opens, requiring the user to choose the specific backup to be restored. Select the appropriate backup and press OK
.

Pressing Yes
in the confirmation panel that follows, reveals a window where the data to be restored can be selected from the following; databases, effects and advertising, messages, member photos and settings.

Press OK
to complete the restore procedure.

Note that this is an extremely risky operation in that it replaces all data with that from the last backup performed and therefore if the last backup was performed three days ago, information from the last three days will be lost.

Database Cleanup

Utilities > Database Cleanup

Select this option in order to eliminate all superfluous data from the database, as set in the Remove Data section in Center Setup > Backup Tab.

MAIN MENU > Utilities > Database

=========== Conqueror-2-022.html — 5.3  Marketing Kits ===========

5.3 Marketing Kits

5.3 Marketing Kits

Utilities > Marketing Kits

BES X Only

This option allows retrieving the Marketing Kits
related to the bundles licenses purchased by the Center.

Press on Marketing Kits
to choose the directory where saving the files and click OK
to start downloading.

MAIN MENU > Utilities > Marketing Kits

=========== Conqueror-2-023.html — 5.4  Technical Support ===========

5.4 Technical Support

5.4 Technical Support

Utilities > Technical Support

This is an external problem-solving module to be utilized together with telephone assistance and can be opened independently from Conqueror in emergencies through the desktop icon.

Having called the technical support team, the Tech Support technician may require that the operator open the Technical Support 
Module and select the necessary options.

This can be done by selecting sections in the tree on the left and pressing + 
to reveal the sub-sections. According to the section selected in the tree, the right-hand column displays a brief description of the functioning of the section and its various options to be followed in order to resolve the problem.

Note that many of the options here require that the operator enter a password, as these functions can cause substantial damage if performed incorrectly (passwords are obtained by calling Technical Support).

Having entered the password, follow the technician's advice on the correct options to select from the lower right-hand panel of the screen.

The toolbar at the base of the screen contains a key icon on the right which, when pressed, increases security allowing only those with the relevant password to perform certain procedures.

Next to the key icon is the field informing of the password required - there are three; User
, Advanced
and Technician
, depending on the level of access required.

On the left hand side of the toolbar is an update as to how the operation is progressing, e.g. "Restore complete".

MAIN MENU > Utilities > Technical Support

=========== Conqueror-2-024.html — 6.  Keyboard Shortcuts ===========

6. Keyboard Shortcuts

6. Keyboard Shortcuts

The following keyboard shortcuts permit the operator to move rapidly within the Conqueror environment.

F1

Use the F1
command to open the User Manual regarding the window currently being visualized.

Shift + F1

Displays Tooltip information on the item selected.

F10

This command returns the user to the Main Menu of Conqueror whereby the window currently being viewed is parked and can be recalled through the appropriate tab.

Ctrl + F1-F9

Opens the respective Quick Button on the right side of the screen.

Ctrl + 0-9

Opens the various tasks held on the left hand side of the screen; therefore, pressing Ctrl
and 2
would open the second parked window.

Tab

Pressing Tab
selects the next field or button to that currently selected on screen.

Shift + Tab

By pressing the Shift
and Tab
keys simultaneously, the operator selects the previous field or button to that currently selected on screen.

Esc

Pressing the Esc
key closes the window currently being viewed.

Shift + Esc (or Alt + F4)

Closes Conqueror.

Windows Key

This option reduces Conqueror to an icon in the tool bar at the base of the screen, opening the Windows Start Menu.

Alt + Tab

To access other open applications.

MAIN MENU > Keyboard Shortcuts

=========== Conqueror-2-025.html — POINT OF SALE ===========

POINT OF SALE

POINT OF SALE

=========== Conqueror-2-026.html — 1.  Overview ===========

1. Overview

1. Overview

Through the Point of Sale
module, the operator can sell all the items that are generally sold in a Bowling Center (e.g. games/time, drinks, food, etc.). Selling items and collecting payments are among the most important operations for a Bowling Center. Through its immediate and simple layout, the Conqueror System allows safer, easier, quicker and more effective sales operations and financial transactions.

The POS module can be accessed through the Conqueror Main Menu and through other areas such as Lane Control, Booking System and Lockers. No matter how the module is accessed, the layout and user interface remain the same so that the operator can also use this module in other contexts like sales purposes, purchasing goods that the Center may require, managing deposits, giving refunds to customers, etc.

Some of the most important POS functions can be accessed through the Main Menu, for example:

Income
, for the retail sale of the items organized in the main categories of a Bowling Center (Snack Bar, Pro Shop, bowling games, etc.).
Tabs

Expenses
, allowing the operator to pay a supplier using money from the cash register.
Deposits
, allowing management and collection of deposits.
Refunds
, allowing the correction of any sales errors and refunding of deposits, etc.
Tips

Reprint Receipts
, to print a receipt again in cases of receipt loss or printing issues.
Open Cash Register
, allowing the cash register to be opened without having to perform a transaction.

When entering the POS from different areas for sales purposes (Lane Control, Booking System, etc.), the POS module adapts to the new context and to the items to be sold, ensuring the quickest and easiest use in that particular area.

POINT OF SALE > Overview

=========== Conqueror-2-027.html — 2.  Sales ===========

2. Sales

2. Sales

The Sales window allows the selling of items according to how they have been organized in the Category Setup module. The main POS window is divided in main sections: the selection area on the right, the current bill on the left and the control panel comprising the options at the base of the screen.

When opened, the Sales window displays the first price key category, and when clicking on a sub-category, all price keys belonging to it are displayed. Each category is given a different color, according to their settings.

POINT OF SALE > Sales

=========== Conqueror-2-028.html — 2.1  Selling Items ===========

2.1 Selling Items

2.1 Selling Items

First click on the category, then on the item to be sold: the item selected is displayed within the table in the left-hand area of the screen, indicating the name of the Product
, its Quantity
and its Total
price.

As selections are made, the Total
located straight under the items is automatically updated, displaying the total amount.

Selling Multiple Items

The system allows the sale of more than one item of the same kind in two quick and easy ways, giving the operator the opportunity to choose the most suitable one.

To sell more than one item of the same kind:

Click on the item twice to sell 2 items, click on it three times to sell 3 items, etc. In this case every click is a row in the list.
Press the desired numeric key, then select the item to be multiplied. If the quantity is greater than 7, click on ?x 
and enter the quantity of the item. In this case a single row is displayed in the list.

To modify the quantity relating to an item, click on the related row, press Change Quantity
and enter the new quantity.

Adding Modifiers

Modifiers are displayed when a price key with Automatic modifiers is selected: click on the desired modifier and press OK
.

To assign a modifier to a price key to which no automatic modifier has been assigned, press Modifiers
, click on the desired modifier and then press OK
.

The modifier is added by default. If a modifier has to be removed from a price key, press Without
before pressing OK
.

By default the modifier is Optional
: i.e. during sales the operator has to press Modifiers
to access the choice of the modifier. In this case the modifier window must be opened by the operator when selling the price key.

Selecting Menu Choices

If the package selected has an open choice menu, click on Choose Menu
to specify the customer's choice. If for example, the package includes 1 generic soft drink, click here to specify the particular soft drink from coke, sparkling water, lemonade, etc.

An item (e.g. coke) could already be selected when opening the menu choices: this means it was previously set as Default Choice
in the Price Setup, because it is the one chosen more frequently.

Only enabling the Receive Payment without Specifying Package Items option in Center Setup allows payment without specifying each individual item in the package deal.

Inserting a Note

Allows writing a note that can be displayed under the selected line. In the window that opens it is possible to specify whether to print it in receipt and/or in reports.

Deleting a Row

Select the bill row to be removed and press here to delete it (price key or note).

A price key cannot be deleted if it was previously saved in a tab, but it can be voided.

Voiding a Price Key

Voids the selling of the selected price key.

When voiding a package in the POS window, a single row is displayed instead of displaying a row for each package item in order to decrease the space that voided items occupy in the bill.

Voiding a transaction row related to the beverage dispenser is considered a Suspect Action and is therefore reported in the Shift Reports.

Applying Discounts

Manual discounts can be applied to an entire bill (including taxes) or to individual price keys, by selecting them before choosing the discount. Click on Discount
, whereby the available discounts that can be applied within that particular time zone are displayed. Click on one of the discounts and press OK
to apply that discount to the bill.

When a discount is applied manually to a member with a regular discount, the higher of the two discounts applies. Example: if an operator decides to apply a 20% discount to a family's total bill, the 20% discount will apply to all family members except the mother, whose 30% discount still applies as it is higher than the other discount.

When applying a manual discount in the Sales window, the chosen mode (Apply to Selected Items
or Apply to Entire Bill
) is now set as default for the further manual discount applications on that terminal.

The relevant privilege is required to perform manual discounts with the Authorization Required option.

Clearing a Bill

Press Undo
to clear the table.

Assigning a Transaction to a Member

A bill can be assigned to a specific member in order to:

record financial data relating to that specific member (print reports and allow the member to collect award points),
sell Members only items,
allow the member to pay with QCash,
give them their member discount
allow them to pay with their Member Account and print them an invoice etc.

To link a bill to a member, swipe the FB card or press Search
: this opens a search window where members can be found by inserting their name or ID.

Adding a Tip

Click on Tips
to open the Tip & Gratuity Management.

Creating a New Tab

The current bill can become a tab by simply clicking on Save
: the tab creation window pops up and is automatically associated to the operator currently logged in, and by default is called "Terminal n - hh:mm"; enter a meaningful name (so it can be easily identified) and press Enter
on the keyboard or press OK
.

If the tab holder is a member, click on the Member
field and select the FB.

To operate with a tab belonging to another operator, the Access to Tabs Pertaining to other Operators
privilege is necessary.

Saving Items within a Tab

To save the current items within an existing tab, click on Add to Tab
, and in the window that opens:

double click on the relevant tab

or

select it and press Choose
.

The tab is automatically opened in the Sales window, completed with the newly added items (the items saved earlier are displayed in blue, while the new ones are in black).

Managing Tabs

Click on Tabs
to directly access the complete Tab Management.

Proceeding with Payment

Payment
opens the Payment window, which allows various financial operations: a bill can be paid, paid with receipt, split into two or more bills and different Payment Modes can be managed.

Undoing a Payment and Reprinting a Receipt

Click on Reprint
to directly access the POS > Reprint Receipts module, where it is possible to:

choose and print a particular receipt,
undo a payment in order to change the payment mode after the receipt has been issued,
reopen a bill closed by mistake.

Only operators provided with the Reprint Receipts
privilege have the possibility to proceed.

POINT OF SALE > Sales > Selling Items

=========== Conqueror-2-029.html — 2.2  Recharging Prepaid Cards ===========

2.2 Recharging Prepaid Cards

2.2 Recharging Prepaid Cards

CoinTech, Embed, Intercard, Sacoa

The prepaid card button in the POS main window opens a little window where it is possible to activate cards and to recharge them.

To activate a new card, simply swipe it and press Activation
- Note that Sacoa cards are not activated by Conqueror
.

To recharge the prepaid card, swipe it, enter the recharge amount and press Recharge
: the recharge is automatically added to the current open tab.

One or more recharges can be added to the tab, allowing the customer to pay for them together with the other price keys in a single bill, e.g. if a family wants to recharge 2 cards, it is possible to pay for them together with a credit card.

In the Shift Reports recharge payments are listed in the Trust
(Deposit) department.

To check if the card is active and the amount available on it, simply swipe it in the POS or payment window.

POINT OF SALE > Sales > Recharging Prepaid Cards

=========== Conqueror-2-030.html — 2.3  Selling Beverages through Dispenser ===========

2.3 Selling Beverages through Dispenser

2.3 Selling Beverages through Dispenser

To sell a beverage through the dispenser, open the Sales window in a Food & Beverage department and enter the related code on the dispenser keypad: the beverage is automatically added to the bill currently open.

To pour beverages through the gun system and manage these sales in Conqueror Pro, it is necessary to:

set the device and its port in the Terminal Setup,
associate codes to the beverage price keys,
configure the dispenser accordingly (carried out by a qualified technician).

In case of dispenser damage or disconnection it is possible to use the price keys as regular price keys.

A single Berg dispenser can be shared by two (or more) different Conqueror Pro terminals by connecting a specific Berg switch to the Berg keypad and to the Conqueror terminals. The operator can use it to send the entered Berg code to all the connected Conqueror Pro terminals.

In the Price Key Setup it is necessary to:

duplicate the price keys that are used by more than one Berg dispenser,
assign different codes to the duplicated price keys,
specify, for each price key, which code is valid on which terminal.

Voiding a transaction row related to the beverage dispenser is considered a Suspect Action and is therefore reported in the Shift Reports.

POINT OF SALE > Sales > Selling Beverages through Dispenser

=========== Conqueror-2-031.html — 2.4  Sending Orders to Kitchen Printer ===========

2.4 Sending Orders to Kitchen Printer

2.4 Sending Orders to Kitchen Printer

For the price key sub-departments pertaining to the F&B main department it is possible to set a specific printer (or printer profile), so that orders are sent to it, e.g. the kitchen item department has to be associated to the kitchen printer so that kitchen orders will be printed on the printer set here.

The price key sent to the kitchen printer are those set as Kitchen Printable in the Price Key Setup.

The printers available in the scroll down menu are the printers installed on the Conqueror Server computer.

POINT OF SALE > Sales > Sending Orders to Kitchen Printer

=========== Conqueror-2-032.html — 3.  Tabs ===========

3. Tabs

3. Tabs

POS > Tabs

Enable the Use Tabs Screen as POS Main Window
option in the POS Setup in order to display the Tab Management screen as main screen of the POS Module.

The tabs currently open are represented by the boxes in the central part of the window.

Only one click on the tab is necessary to display its details (description, operator, bill details). A second click opens it in the Sales window.

To operate on the tab, double click on it or select it and press Choose
: the Sales window is accessed and it is possible to add, modify and delete items (the items saved before are displayed in blue, while the new ones are in black).

To operate with a tab belonging to another operator, the Access to Tabs Pertaining to other Operators
privilege is necessary.

Creating a Tab Manually

A tab can be created manually:

in the Sales window after item selection by clicking on Save

in this window by pressing New
: in this case the operator is automatically sent to the Sales window for the sales.

In both cases the tab is associated to the operator who is currently logged in and by default is called "Terminal n - hh:mm", but it is advisable to enter a meaningful name to the tab so it can be easily identified.

If the tab holder is a member, click on the Member
field and select the FB.

By clicking on OK
the Sales window where items can be assigned opens immediately.

Sorting Tabs

To make the tab search faster, use the Sorting by
scroll down menu and choose from Description
, Date-Ascending
, Date-Descending
: the tabs are automatically arranged according to the chosen criteria.

Works in both the open and closed tabs screen.

Deleting a Tab

To delete a tab, select it and press Delete
.

Note that a tab can be deleted only if empty.

Tabs Automatically Created

Tabs are automatically created when opening a lane or when setting a reservation as Arrived (or Ready).

Lane tabs can be displayed by pressing Lanes
on the right-hand side of the window: each lane is represented by a box that can be selected.
Reservation tabs can be displayed by pressing Booking
on the right-hand side of the window: incoming reservations and current reservations are represented by boxes that can be selected.

From the POS workstation it is possible to sell items to a reservation without opening the Reservation Sheet or the Lane Control window: simply click on the Booking
or Lanes
button, select the desired reservation or lane and press Choose
.

Incoming Reservation Tabs

Incoming Reservations can be displayed and managed.

To create a tab for an incoming reservation click on Next Bookings
, select the desired incoming reservation and press Choose
to add items directly to the tab.

If the incoming reservation is a Provisional or Confirmed reservation, when creating a tab for it the reservation is automatically set as Arrived and the tab is displayed in the Tab window.

Merging Tabs

To merge 2 or more tabs:

open the first tab to be merged,
press Add to Tab
and select the second tab to be merged,
press Choose
and the POS items are automatically moved/added to the first tab.
By pressing Save
the inclusive tab is closed, with all the items grouped in it.

This procedure can be repeated on more tabs, even on tabs related to reservations or lanes, e.g. customer drinks or meals can be linked to their lane when they start to bowl.

To restore the original tabs, press Unmerge
: the tab is automatically split in two separated tabs.

Credit Card and Seamless Transactions

Thanks to tab management it is possible to provide customers with a single bill for their entire visit including bowling, restaurant and other items purchased at the snack bar, on the lanes or in any area of the Bowling Center: the operator swipes the customer's credit card at the beginning of the evening and all customer purchases will be added to their tab. When they leave the Center the total tab amount will be automatically charged on the credit card previously swiped.

The name of the tab automatically includes the name of the card holder, the terminal number, the date and the time of tab opening.

All tabs linked to a specific credit card will automatically be merged in the Payment window.

Printing the Bill

Press Bill
to print the bill to give to the customer before collecting the payment.

This bill is not the receipt. The receipt is printed after payment and it contains payment modes, change, taxes, discounts, etc.

POINT OF SALE > Tabs

=========== Conqueror-2-033.html — 4.  Gratuities and Tips ===========

4. Gratuities and Tips

4. Gratuities and Tips

Usually the waiter takes the bill to the customer, who gives their credit card to the waiter. The waiter swipes the credit card and gives the customer their receipt. At this point, the customer writes the amount of the tip on the receipt. The waiter goes back to the cash register and inserts the amount of the tip in the Tips
plugin.

Credit card payments and all the data related to them (ID
, Date
, Amount
, Tip
, Total
, Tip to
) are displayed in the table. Select the bill to which the tip has to be associated, insert the Tip Amount
and if desired assign it to a different operator (if not currently logged in) by using the Tip
scroll down menu.

The Apply
button makes the payment effective and the connection of the credit card transaction starts.

By default the table displays payments already made by credit card for which no tip has been given. To see transactions for which a tip has already been given, enable the Display all Transactions
option.

Details relating to the transaction selected in the table are found in the box at the bottom of the window.

Only operators provided with the Manage Tips
privilege have the possibility to proceed.

In the Sales window, enter a tip and print a receipt for the customer, displaying the bill amount, the tip applied, and the transaction total.

Automatic Gratuities

It is possible to pre-define which prices can be subject to a gratuity, which ones always require a gratuity and which are never to be considered (e.g. excluding alcoholic drinks where not allowed).

First define a default gratuity percentage (which can be optionally modified or amended at payment time). 

The gratuity is automatically calculated when the bill includes at least a price key set as Always Include in Gratuity. The gratuity calculation is based on all the price keys in the bill set as Always Consider
or Optionally Consider
. The price keys set as Never Consider
are not included in the gratuity calculation.

Collecting a Payment

From the payment window, press Tips
to add a gratuity to the bill.

The gratuity is calculated on the basis of the percentage previously defined in Center Setup but can be manually modified by operators having the relevant user privilege.

The collected gratuity is not saved in the tab but is assigned to the waiter associated to the bill, if present, otherwise it is assigned to the operator currently logged in. In the case of a credit card payment, the gratuity is managed as a credit card tip.

In the Tips
module it is possible to change a collected gratuity, in particular it is possible to modify the tip receiver in any case and the amount in the case of the following payment methods: credit cards, CoinTech and all the undefined payment methods (e.g. cash).

Only operators with the Change Automatic Gratuity Percentage
can modify the gratuity percentage in the payment window.

Shift Reports

Gratuities and tips are properly tracked, displayed in a dedicated department in the Trust section of the End of Shift Reports and exported to QuickBooks Desktop.

Any transaction involving gratuities and tips is included in a receipt as for a normal trust price key and therefore it is displayed in a dedicated department in the shift report section displaying the trust items. So gratuity collection, additional tip collection and tip payouts are displayed in the shift report and included when calculating the amount present in the cash drawer. These transactions are also included in the other economic reports: Historical, Statistical and Frequent Bowler reports.

POINT OF SALE > Gratuities and Tips

=========== Conqueror-2-034.html — 5.  Payments ===========

5. Payments

5. Payments

5.1 Payment Window

The Payment
window provides a handy and versatile tool that allows various financial operations: a transaction can be paid, paid with receipt, split into two or more transactions and different Payment Modes can be managed.

This window contains a table with a different row for each item sold and the fields above the table are:

Total
that indicates the total amount of the current bill including taxes and discounts; by clicking on this box the Tax Details
window opens, displaying the net total, the different taxes and the receipt total.
Total Collected
that indicates the amount already collected.
Still Due
that indicates the amount still due if the customer has only paid a part of the total amount using Special Payments; this amount can be paid using another special payment, or cash.
Amount Tendered
that indicates the amount the customer is paying; by entering an amount here, the Change Due
is automatically calculated.
Points
that indicates how many points are to be assigned to an FB. Click on this box to select the member to whom the points collected through the Players Club feature will be assigned.
Tax-exempt ID
that opens a window where the customer's tax-exempt ID can be entered. When a transaction is marked as Tax-exempt
, all add-on taxes are removed, and the balance adjusted.
Payment Modes
that indicates the amount paid through Special Payment.

The Pay
button allows immediate payment collection, without printing a receipt; to collect the payment and print the receipt, press Receipt
. Should the printout fail for any reason, to reprint the receipt, enter POS > Reprint Receipts.

During the payment phase, it is also possible to link the current bill to another one previously saved by clicking on the Link Tab
: select the tab to be paid together with the current one, and press Choose
.

A bill can be split into two or more different bills, allowing the customer to pay at different stages, or allowing different customers to pay part of the whole amount (e.g. if members of a group decide to have separate bills). To split the bill in parts press Split
.

POINT OF SALE > Payments > Payment Window

=========== Conqueror-2-035.html — 5.2  Special Payments ===========

5.2 Special Payments

5.2 Special Payments

This module allows extremely flexible management of any of the payment modes a Bowling Center might accept, according to what has been set previously in the Denominations module.

It is possible to combine various payment modes and to collect money from different customers if desired, allowing customers the freedom to choose the payment solution that best fits their needs. A customer may choose to pay part of the payment due using QCash, part by Credit Card, and part Cash, etc.

Note that for Player Club Cash payments and for Member Accounts it is not possible to combine different payment types.

On the right-hand side of the screen there are boxes representing the different payment modes previously set (QCash, Credit Card, Check, Voucher, Prepaid Cards, etc.), while the small table contains the description and the amount of the different Special Payments.

Simply click on the desired Special Payment
, set the amount and click on OK
.

Paying with QCash

Select the QCash
box, which opens the Member Search window: swipe the member's card or search and select the member in the archive.

The QCash Payment
window opens, displaying data relating to the member previously selected. This data is set through Front desk > Frequent Bowlers and cannot be modified from this window.

On the right-hand side of the screen the amount to be paid with QCash can be defined (by default, the total amount due is suggested).

Search
allows the operator to change the member through the Member Search window, while OK
closes the window, calculating the Special Payment
and the Total Due
.

Paying by Credit Card

Swipe the credit card, confirm or edit the Amount
to be paid and press OK
.

Selecting the credit card box opens the small Credit Card
window, where the Amount
to be paid by credit card can be entered.

The two fields Masked Card Number
and Expiry Date
are automatically completed with the relevant information cannot be completed manually.

Paying by Debit Card

Swipe the debit card, confirm or edit the Amount
to be paid and press OK
.

A message reminds the customer to enter the PIN code using the PinPad device.

If swiping the credit card without first choosing the Debit Card Special Payment, the credit card payment is selected by default, but can be changed into a Debit Card payment by clicking on the To Debit Card
button.

Paying with Player Club Cash

Select the Player Club Cash
box, which opens the Member Search window: swipe the member's card or search and select the member in the archive. Selecting the member automatically completes the Payment Mode
table with the relevant information.

Note that for Player Club Cash payments it is not possible to combine different payment types.

Paying with a Prepaid Credit Card

Swipe the Prepaid Card, confirm or amend the Amount
to be paid and press OK
.

Mixed payments are allowed, i.e. it is possible to pay part of the bill with the prepaid card and the rest with credit card, cash, free coupons, etc.

Partial Approval and Real Time Reversal
- For Mercury, PCCharge and PAYwarePC, 'partial approval' and 'real time reversal' features are supported. In practice: if on the prepaid credit card the credit is not sufficient to pay the total amount due, the transaction is successfully processed and the remaining total due must be paid with another Payment Method. Otherwise, the transaction can be immediately voided.

Paying with Member Account

Select the Member Account box, which opens the Member Search window: swipe the member's card or search and select the member in the archive. Selecting the member automatically completes the Payment Mode
table with the relevant information.

Press Pay
without paying to add the receipt to the member Account: the receipt is immediately displayed in the Invoices
module, where it can be managed.

Note that in Member Account it is not possible to combine different payment types.

POINT OF SALE > Payments > Special Payments

=========== Conqueror-2-036.html — 5.3  Paying Separately ===========

5.3 Paying Separately

5.3 Paying Separately

A tab can be split into two or more different tabs, allowing the customer to pay at different stages, or allowing different customers to pay part of the whole amount (e.g. if members of a group decide to have separate bills).

To split a tab, press Split
that opens a screen where the items to be split can be selected and moved from the table on the left to the one on the right, using the arrows
in the middle.

After confirming the choice by pressing OK
, the Payment
screen opens, allowing the customer to pay their part of the total amount.

After the payment has been received, the splitting screen reopens and the tab can be split again.

Press Exit
when split operations are completed, or press Save
to save the remaining items in the tab.

POINT OF SALE > Payments > Paying Separately

=========== Conqueror-2-037.html — 5.4  Dividing the Bill in Equal Portions ===========

5.4 Dividing the Bill in Equal Portions

5.4 Dividing the Bill in Equal Portions

In the payment window, it is possible to enter the number of paying customers in order to calculate the due for each person, allowing the operator to collect the partial payments in succession.

Press on Divide
and enter the number of paying customers: the portion amount appears, allowing the operator to collect the payment from the different customers.

POINT OF SALE > Payments > Dividing the Bill in Equal Portions

5.5 Reprinting a Receipt

POS > Reprint Receipts

First select the correct Date
and Terminal
followed by the desired transaction: the original transaction is immediately displayed on the left-hand side of the window, while the payment mode details are shown in the right-hand corner.

At this point, press Receipt
to reprint the receipt currently displayed.

Only operators provided with the Reprint Receipts
privilege have the possibility to proceed.

=========== Conqueror-2-039.html — 5.6  Undoing Payments ===========

5.6 Undoing Payments

5.6 Undoing Payments

POS > Reprint Receipts

From this window it is also possible to:

undo a payment in order to change the payment mode after the receipt has been issued,
reopen a bill closed by mistake.

Select the receipt to be undone and press Undo
: a new receipt is printed, voiding the payment one, and the tab is displayed in the Payment window, ready to be corrected and paid.

The Reprint Receipt
module is also accessible directly from the Sales window through the Reprint
button, but only by operators having the Undo Payment
User Privilege.

Restrictions

At present it is not possible to undo: credit card payments, debit card payments, payments made through Frequent Bowler accounts, payments involving packages with open choice menus, refunds.

=========== Conqueror-2-042.html — 7.  Deposits ===========

7. Deposits

7. Deposits

POS > Trusts

Effecting transactions as described below, assures accurate and precise management of all operations regarding deposits, enabling the Bowling Center to control and record all transactions of money and goods.

The Trust
module provided by Conqueror is a tool that allows the operator to perform and record all deposit operations. Deposits may be necessary in various situations:

As confirmation of a table reservation, tournament, etc. This deposit will be refunded upon full payment.
As insurance against damage that is refunded upon conclusion of games, etc. when it is clear that the customer has not caused any damage.

From this module, deposits can be managed in a simple way: they are received as deposits and refunded from the Refund module.

Effecting transactions as described below, assures accurate and precise management of all operations regarding deposits, enabling the Bowling Center to control and record all transactions of money and goods.

By clicking on the relevant item to be paid, a window appears where the Price
can be modified and the Quantity
of items selected. It is also possible to enter a reason for the deposit in the delegated space, and this Note
can then be printed in Receipts
and/or in Shift Reports
.

To assign the current transaction to a member, press Search
.

The Reset
button clears the table, while pressing OK
moves directly to the Payment window to carry out normal transactions (pay, stack, link).

Note that in the Booking System, deposits are managed differently.

POINT OF SALE > Deposits

=========== Conqueror-2-043.html — 8.  Refunds ===========

8. Refunds

8. Refunds

POS > Refunds

The Refund
module provided by Conqueror is a tool that allows the operator to perform and record all refund operations. Refunds may be necessary in various situations: if the item required is no longer available although it has already been paid for, or if the customer is for any reason not completely satisfied, or simply if the customer is to be refunded their deposit.

Click on the item to be refunded, modify the Price
if necessary (e.g. if only part of the price or a larger amount is to be refunded). If desired, a reason for the refund can be entered in the Note
space and it is also possible to choose to print this note in Receipts
and/or in Shift Reports
.

To assign the current refund to a member, press Search
; this also allows the refunding of Members Only items and discounted items.

The Undo
button clears the table, while the Payment
button allows moving directly to the Payment window to carry out usual transactions (Split, Link Tab, Receipt).

POINT OF SALE > Refunds

=========== Conqueror-2-044.html — 9.  Tax Exemption Handling ===========

9. Tax Exemption Handling

9. Tax Exemption Handling

In the payment window the operator can to mark an entire transaction as Tax-exempt
by specifying a Tax-exempt ID in the relevant field.

When a transaction is marked as Tax-exempt, all add-on taxes are removed and the balance is adjusted. The receipt shows the detail of Tax-exempt ID instead of the amount of add-on taxes.

It is possible to refund, undo and reprint a receipt with tax exemption.

Only the operators with the relevant privilege can perform tax exemption transactions. Employees without this new privilege are not able to insert a Tax-exempt ID in the payment window and in the FB data.

POINT OF SALE > Tax Exemption Handling

=========== Conqueror-2-045.html — 9.1  Payment Modes ===========

9.1 Payment Modes

9.1 Payment Modes

It is possible to pay a Tax-exempt transaction using all payment modes, including QCash and Member account.

When paying a tax exempt transaction with Player Club cash, the amount of points removed matches the total without add-on taxes.

POINT OF SALE > Tax Exemption Handling > Payment Modes

=========== Conqueror-2-046.html — 9.2  Tax-exempt ID Linked to FB ===========

9.2 Tax-exempt ID Linked to FB

9.2 Tax-exempt ID Linked to FB

For each FB it is possible to insert a Tax-exempt ID.

If a tab is linked to a FB with a Tax-exempt ID, when the FB pays the tab, the system automatically uses the Tax-exempt ID to calculate the tab payment total with tax exemption.

Reservations

If a reservation is linked to a FB that has a Tax-exempt ID, the amount of the deposit is automatically recalculated using the total without taxes.

When the FB pays the reservation, the system automatically uses the Tax-exempt ID to complete the reservation payment with tax exemption.

Web Reservations

When web user are registered with a Tax-exempt ID, the amount of the deposit of their web reservations is automatically calculated using the total without taxes and their Tax-exemption ID is used when paying the reservation.

The Tax-exempt ID is not saved into the tab, so in the case the operator doesn't proceed with payment, the tax-exemption ID is deleted; it has to be entered again when the customer pays the tab.

POINT OF SALE > Tax Exemption Handling > Tax-exempt ID Linked to FB

=========== Conqueror-2-047.html — 9.3  Reporting ===========

9.3 Reporting

9.3 Reporting

Summary Section in Shift Report

Staff operators can choose to add to the shift report a section that shows the details of receipts marked as Tax-exempt, similarly to refund and void sections.

Shift Report Grouped by Tax Exemption

When printing a shift report, a staff operator can choose to group the price keys sold with tax exemption or without tax exemption.

Historical Report Grouped by Tax Exemption

When printing an historical report, a staff operator can choose to group price keys sold with tax-exemption or without tax-exemption.

POINT OF SALE > Tax Exemption Handling > Reporting

=========== Conqueror-2-048.html — 9.4  Tax-exempt Exports ===========

9.4 Tax-exempt Exports

9.4 Tax-exempt Exports

If an export to Micros, R-Keeper or WinTic is configured, the add-on tax exemption does not modify the behavior of the system, because tax information is not actually exported.

No information related to tax exemption is available on exports to third party programs as RecTrac or Zonal.

Tax exemption is disabled when CleanCash export is enabled.

Export to Dassle (QCAD)

The operator can configure the tax-exempt suffix for Dassle. That suffix is appended to price key information during export to Dassle when the price key is sold with tax-exemption.

Export to QuickBooks Desktop

The operator can configure the tax-exempt suffix for QuickBooks Desktop. That suffix is appended to price key information during export to QuickBooks Desktop when the price key is sold with tax-exemption and the system is configured to export to QuickBooks Desktop without using account numbers.

In QuickBooks Desktop Setup, the operator can choose to define a second distinct account number for each price key. That second account number is used for price keys sold with tax exemption, when the system is configured to export the information to QuickBooks Desktop using account numbers.

POINT OF SALE > Tax Exemption Handling > Tax-exempt Exports

=========== Conqueror-2-049.html — 10.  Credit Card Transactions ===========

10. Credit Card Transactions

10. Credit Card Transactions

Back Office > Credit/Debit Cards

This module contains a summary of all credit card and debit card transactions performed throughout the day.

The Type
column shows the label Debit
or the Credit Card
provider name to identify the transaction type.

Web Transactions
and Local Transactions
are displayed separately. To switch from Local transactions to Web transactions, use the button Web/Local in the control panel. The control panel, the Close Batch feature and the Summary regard the table currently selected.

In the table it is possible to verify the status of all transactions, reprint receipts, filter the transactions displayed and void credit card transactions (if selecting a debit card transaction, the Void
button is disabled).

In this summary one transaction is displayed per each line and automatically updated as its status evolves.

The ID
(number associated to the transaction), the Date
the transaction was performed and the transaction Status
are specified for each line (for each transaction) as follows:

Executed
: the request has been sent to the bank (or to the international circuit), but the financial transaction is still open, so the operation can still be voided.
Voided
: the operator has voided the transaction due to an error.
Closed
: the bank has confirmed the operation, the transaction has been executed and therefore can no longer be voided.

Then, the Total
amount of the financial transaction paid by credit card is indicated, followed by the Tip
amount paid, the masked Card Number
, by the Operator
who performed the transaction and the Authorization Number
given by the provider.

If the transaction is voided, also the Void Date
will be specified, while the name of the operator responsible of the void operation will be indicated in the Voided By
column.

From...To...

This option allows the operator to view all transactions performed in the specific period of time entered.

Reprint

This option reprints a copy of the receipt pertaining to the selected transaction.

Void

This option allows voiding the selected credit card transaction.

Refresh

This option refreshes the list displaying all recent modifications, such as new transactions or changes of status made after screen opening or after the most recent refresh.

Web or Local

Web Transactions and Local transactions are displayed separately. To switch from Local transactions to Web transactions, use the button Web/Local in the control panel. The control panel, the Close Batch feature and the Summary regard the table currently selected.

POINT OF SALE > Credit Card Transactions

=========== Conqueror-2-050.html — 10.1  Batch Summary ===========

10.1 Batch Summary

10.1 Batch Summary

The amounts shown in the Batch Summary include Credit Cards and Debit Cards transactions.

Pressing Close Batch
button starts the completion procedure of financial transactions, i.e. it authorizes the debiting of the amount due. When it has been pressed, it is impossible to void transactions, which pass from the Executed
status to the Closed
status.

It is advisable to perform this task daily.

Web Transactions are not displayed together with the Local Transactions, but separately.

To switch from Local to Web or to Web to Local, use the new button Web/Local in the control panel.

The control panel, the Close Batch feature and the Summary regard the table currently selected.

POINT OF SALE > Credit Card Transactions > Batch Summary

=========== Conqueror-2-051.html — 11.  Payment Provider Setup ===========

11. Payment Provider Setup

11. Payment Provider Setup

License and Additional Hardware

A specific Conqueror Pro license (and/or additional hardware) is required to use a payment provider.

Payment Mode Activation

After configuring the Center's provider, it is necessary to activate and personalize the related Payment Mode
in the Denomination Setup: go to Setup > Price Setup > Denominations, click in the Special Payments
column, press New
, enter a Description
and select the relevant Payment Type
(Credit card, Debit card, etc.)

Provider Setup

Setup > Center Setup > Payments

These settings are set during the installation of the system.

It is not recommended that operators modify any of these settings without first having received instructions from a qualified technician of QubicaAMF Technical Support.

Enter the Local Setup
and the Web Setup
to specify the provider settings.

POINT OF SALE > Payment Provider Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-052.html — 11.1  Local Payment Providers ===========

11.1 Local Payment Providers

11.1 Local Payment Providers

PAYwareConnect Settings

Specify the Center Client ID
and the URL
.

Set the Pre-authorization Amount
: when a credit card is necessary as guarantee to open the tab of a customer, it is possible to specify for which minimum amount a check to the customer's bank has to be made. Example: if a customer wants to open a tab in the Center and 1$ is set, the bank's server will be queried to find out if the customer has at least 1$ on their account. A tab will then be opened and payment can be made. In this case, set the Pre-authorization Amount
to 1.

Now the PinPad Devices have to be installed in order to process payments. To install the center devices, go to Terminal Setup > Devices. In the PinPad Installed
section it is possible to remove a device previously set.

PAYwarePC Settings

Specify the Center Client ID
, User Name
and Password
, and the PAYwarePC Server Address
and Port
.

This payment mode can be used both on-line and in the local center.

If using PAYwarePC Debit Cards, a numerical PinPad is required to enter the personal identification number (PIN) needed to complete the transaction.

PCCharge Settings

Specify the Center Merchant ID
, the corresponding Processor
numbers and the PCCharge User Name
, Server Address
and Port
.

Mercury Settings

Specify the Center Merchant ID
and the Mercury Host List
.

Set the Pre-authorization Amount
: when a credit card is necessary as guarantee to open the tab of a customer, it is possible to specify for which minimum amount a check to the customer's bank has to be made. Example: if a customer wants to open a tab in the Center and 1$ is set, the bank's server will be queried to find out if the customer has at least 1$ on their account. A tab will then be opened and payment can be made. In this case, set the Pre-authorization Amount
to 1.

If using Mercury Debit Cards, a numerical PinPad is required to enter the personal identification number (PIN) needed to complete the transaction.

POINT OF SALE > Payment Provider Setup > Local Payment Providers

=========== Conqueror-2-054.html — 12.  POS Setup ===========

12. POS Setup

12. POS Setup

Setup > Price Setup > POS Setup

Tip Management

Enables all the features regarding tips, in particular the print of the line of the credit card receipt referred to tips.

Use Tabs Screen as POS Main Window

Enable this option to display the Tabs management screen as main screen of the POS Module.

Display Price Keys Needing Authorization

This option allows the manager to control the sale of particular items, e.g. some discounted price keys can be reserved only by managers, while others can be used also by operators managing reservations.

First set the price keys requiring this particular control as Authorization Required. Then, enable the Sell Authorization Required
privilege only to operators who may sell these special price keys.

There is a second level of authorization, setting price keys as Authorization Required - Type 2
and the Sell Authorization Required - Type 2 Price Key
user privilege.

Kitchen Printer Profiles

Configure printer profiles of 3 printers and the time ranges each printer will be used in: press New
, specify a profile name, select the first printer, set the time range this printer will be used in (Start Time
, End Time
) and then proceed in the same way for the second and the third printer.

The printers available in the scroll down menu are the printers installed on the Conqueror Server computer.

Then printer profile has to be associated to the relevant F&B sub-departments.

Click on the Font
icon in order to customize the layout of kitchen receipts. To remove the customization and go back to the default, disable the Custom Font
option.

Tab Assigned to the Last Operator

With this option enabled, tabs are assigned to the last operator having worked on it.

Organization Number

Swedish Market Only

Conqueror Pro can work with CleanCash, a fiscal control unit compliant with the regulations of the Swedish National Tax Board.

To use this interface it is necessary to enter the Organization Number
provided by CleanCash, and the receipt printer must be configured and properly connected.

POINT OF SALE > POS Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-055.html — 12.1  Accounting Software Interfaces ===========

12.1 Accounting Software Interfaces

12.1 Accounting Software Interfaces

Micros

Centers using Micros interface have to enable it in this window. For more details about the Micros parameters to be entered, please refer to the documentation provided at center installation.

WinTic

The WinTic Interface requires a specific Conqueror Pro license.

Conqueror Pro can work with this accounting software for the management of cash operations.

To use WinTic software it is necessary to:

Enable the Export to WinTic
, define which computer is the server for the WinTic
application, enter the Port
number and specify Connection Timeout
and Response Timeout
(both in milliseconds).
Associate WinTic codes to the price keys to be exported.
Configure the WinTic software accordingly (carried out by a qualified technician).

Cancelling a WinTic price key is considered a Suspect Action, and is therefore reported in the Shift Reports.

It is recommended to avoid rounding price keys in order to prevent total discrepancies between the Conqueror Pro and WinTic; in the Currency Setup, set 2 Decimal Digit
and the Rounding Unit
to 0.01.

Fixed discounts on single price keys are managed for both the Italian and the Romanian markets. For a technical limit of WinTic on rounding and subdivisions of the account part of SIAE, in the Italian market it is necessary to avoid using percentage discount on the single price keys. For a technical limit of WinTic on rounding, it is necessary to avoid using discounts on the transaction total (for both markets).

POINT OF SALE > POS Setup > Accounting Software Interfaces

12.2 Prepaid Cards

12.2 Prepaid Cards

CoinTech, Embed, Intercard, Sacoa

Enable Conqueror Pro to work with the third party prepaid card application, then define which computer is the server for this application and enter the port number.

If working with Embed, enter the same User Name
and Password
used previously on Embed server
.

If working with CoinTech, enter the specific Separator
, the Prefix Length
and the Suffix Length
.

These settings are set during the installation of the third party system. It is not recommended that operators modify any of these settings without first having received instructions from a qualified technician.
