=========== Conqueror-2-099.html — 12.  Bowler Options ===========

12. Bowler Options

12. Bowler Options

Bowler Options refer to the bowler's preferences regarding various aspects of the game.

Bowler Options can be accessed through various modules including Lane Control
, Waiting List
, Score
, Booking System
and the League
and Tournament
modules.

To insert bowler names, handicaps and preferences regarding the game, click on the player name in Lane Control, whereby the Bowler Options
window appears.

Name

The name of the player can be entered from the keyboard.

If the player is a member, swipe their membership card or search their details in the database using the Search
button.

To quickly enter an FB, type their FB ID and press Enter
from the keyboard.

Games/Time

Set the number of pre-assigned games or the amount of time to be sold with the default price key set through the Set Price
button.

Handicap

Enter the player's handicap.

Start Mode

When adding a bowler to an open lane through the Lane Control or Score modules, specify in the drop down menu, whether the bowler is to be added to This Game
, the Next Game
or directly to the current frame (Auto Fill
) inserting nil (Gutter score) for the points of previous non-played frames.

Delete

The Delete
button allows the cancellation of a player from the lane, as long as the lane has not yet been opened.

Birthday Photo

Press this button to access the birthday party photo settings.

Search

This option opens the Member Search module.

Info

This option opens the Member Data window where it is possible to view all information, data, photograph, etc. regarding that bowler.

Bowler Price

Set the bowler default price key to be utilized for the games or time pre-assigned through the Game/Time field.

OK

Save and exit by selecting this option.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Bowler Options

=========== Conqueror-2-100.html — 12.1  Bowler Type ===========

12.1 Bowler Type

12.1 Bowler Type

According to the settings, a symbol or letter is displayed next to the name of the bowler to identify the type of bowler in the Lane Control window.

Pacer

Check this option when the league bowler is to be set as Pacer.

Regular

When this option is selected, specify the following options;

Remove
- if the bowler is to be completely removed from the game and all successive games
Skip
- if the bowler has left for a brief period and is to recuperate all missed frames upon return
Left-handed
- BES Only
- launches the Tutor animation with bowling tips for left-handers.
If the Bumpers
are to be set for this bowler
If the Q-View
replay device is desired
If the player is Pre-bowling

Birthday Party
- Bowland Only
- enables a photograph of the birthday bowler to be sent to the lane monitor after the second bowl.
Special Game
- this option is checked by default, deselect if the bowler does not wish to participate in the Special Games set for the lane
QFlash Level
- enables the interactive laser bowling game combining accuracy with timing (and a Strike or Spare) - select Auto
for a more challenging game whereby the speed of the flashing beams increases as the score increases
No Tap
- if the player does not wish to participate in the No Tap Classic Game launched through Lane Options, select Off
in the scroll menu. The No Tap value can be modified in the scroll menu for that bowler.
Mad Game Level
- BES X Only
- Choose here between Easy
, Medium
, Hard
, Expert
as bowler level for Battle on the Lanes
and Bowlin'Hood
.

Blind

Specify whether to Remove
, Skip
or assign the bowler a Blind Score
.

Best Ball

The Best Ball game is a team game that constructs a virtual game with higher scores, by combining the scores of all of the players in the team; the system constructs this game using the players' best bowls.

For example, Bobby scores 7 and 1, while Frankie scores 6 and 2, therefore the Best Ball Bowler obtains a score of 7 and 2!

By selecting this option, this player is set as the Best Ball Bowler.

The Best Ball Bowler must be the last to bowl, for this function to work.

Best Frame

This option functions in the same way as the Best Ball Bowler option, but utilizes each of the players' best frames to construct a game of the best virtual players.

The Best Frame Bowler must be the last to bowl, for this function to work.

Vacant

Specify whether the bowler is to be removed, skipped or assigned a Vacant Score
.

Pre-bowled

Specify whether the player is to be removed or skipped, as well as the Current Game
score and that of the next 9 games.

This setting is used when a league or tournament player cannot participate on the date of play, whereby they play before the date, entering their scores manually in this section which will then be displayed frame by frame as the actual league/tournament game is played.

Player and Team Attribute

Select the suitable attributes in these scroll menus, at player level and at team level.

If an attribute has already been assigned to the team from Lane Control, it will be present here by default, but can be modified.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Bowler Options > Bowler Type

=========== Conqueror-2-101.html — 13.  Special Functions - BES &amp; Bowland ===========

13. Special Functions - BES & Bowland

13. Special Functions - BES & Bowland

Lane Status > Special Functions

Special Functions are a series of functions that can be applied to a lane or to a group of lanes.

To apply any of the Special Functions, first select the lane and then press Special Functions
to apply the desired function as follows.

Split/Merge

Divide a pair of lanes into two single lanes by selecting the pair and pressing the Split
button.

Join a single lane with its sister lane (if compatible) by selecting it and clicking on Merge
.

These functions are unavailable if lanes are opened in Time mode with the Charge Double Time
option checked in Center Setup.

Last

Press Last
to display the last photo of the pins taken by the camera on the lanes selected.

TV

Press here to access the settings of the monitor/TV connected to one or more pairs of lanes.

In the case of the Accuvision monitor, each monitor can be setup separately: both monitors on the pair of lanes plus the central one, when present. The data source will be different: lane-off picture/advertising, integrated single channel TV, multi-channel TV, AV1, AV2, component input and HDMI. If the multi-channel TV is selected, it is possible to choose a particular channel on tuner 1 or 2.

If the selection includes lanes with different settings, a question mark will be displayed on the monitor image.

Reopen

Reopens the lane with the same settings (options and players) of the last bowling session (the games played will be put together with those played previously).

Example: the game has finished, the lane is closed but the players change their minds and wish to continue playing.

Skip Boot Error

When the Lane Control Box has a boot error, it is possible to force its functioning by using this button (that is, open the lanes), thus ignoring the error.

When errors occur regarding TV PIP for example, that is not vital to the development of the game and therefore the customer wouldn't notice the malfunction, this button comes in handy. On the other hand, when an error occurs regarding the pinsetter, this option allows the lane to be opened, but the pinsetter commands will have to be entered manually on the keyboard, in the same manner as scores are inserted.

This button is useful during tournaments when continuous play is essential, even if it requires that tasks be performed manually.

Transfer Bowlers

BES, Bowland and Bowland-X Only

To transfer players from one lane to another available lane (whether open or closed), click on the Transfer Bowlers
button, choose the destination lane and press OK
, then select the player to be transferred and the amount of time (Time to Transfer
) if the session was opened in Time mode. If the lane was opened in Game mode, the remaining frames to be played are entered automatically.

Pogo Pin

Bowland and Bowland-X Only

Press here to activate the Pogo Pin game on the selected lanes.

Strike Challenge

BES, Bowland and Bowland-X Only

Press here to access the Strike Challenge setup and management.

Lucky Train

BES Only

Press here to activate the Lucky Train global game on the selected lanes.

Swap

Swap players in a pair of lanes, from one lane to another.

Keyboard

Opens a keyboard simulating that on the lanes, allowing the operator (instead of the players) to send commands to the lanes.

Volume

The Volume
option controls the general volume on the lanes, while the volume of Special Games
, TV
, animation/advertising in Lane Off
and for bowling sessions and animation in Lane On
can be controlled separately. The Mute
button turns the sound off completely which may be utilized when a loud-speaker message is to be transmitted, for example.

This panel is only accessible if the Sound option is checked in Center Setup.

The Lane Off
and Games
options are only available for Bowland Centers.

Monitor Image

Displays the image of the monitor of the selected lane.

Restore Last Game

BES Only

If the player goes to the following game by pressing the keyboard by mistake while the current game is not finished yet, they can ask the operator to restore the interrupted game.

This function is available only to the operators with the Restore Last Game through Special Functions
privilege.

Lane Lights

This function gives you the possibility to temporarily override light effects on a given lane set. You can use this function:

On Single lane / Group of lanes to set a color or an effect for testing or maintenance purposes; and celebrate an event, such as birthday parties.

Entire center / Selected areas of the center to override Global Mood sequences for a certain amount of time. 

To use these functions flag the following privileges: Access to Global Moods Setup and Access to Capping Light Setup
.

Lane lights consists of two tabs: Lane Effect and Global Mood. Select Lane Effect to modify Deck light and/or Bumper lights. You can switch the lights off, select a set color or in alterative choose an effect from the Light effect (deck + bumpers) list. Choose any effect from the list, you can modify it or run it as it is. To modify an effect use the options below: 

From
/To Color
: Define the color range that you want to rotate (From) and the color range you want to rotate (To). Double click on the color to open the color palette. 
Speed
: Modify Speed of the effect, open the drop-down menu and select your speed. 
Direction:
Modify the direction of the effect. Please note: this option may be available only for selected effects (e.g. Wave).

To run the effect click Play
, click Stop
to interrupt the effect and let the one set in the Bowling Mode run back again.

The Global Mood tab allows you to change the Global Mood of the center on a given lane set just for a limited amount of time. Use the drop-down menu to choose a Global mood previously set up; use the Lanes field on the right of the window to select on which lane set the effect must run on and then set the duration.

To run the effect click Play
, click Stop
to interrupt the effect and let the one set in the Bowling Mode run back again.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Special Functions - BES & Bowland

=========== Conqueror-2-102.html — 13.1  Pogo Pin ===========

13.1 Pogo Pin

13.1 Pogo Pin

Bowland and Bowland-X Only

Lane Status > Special Functions

Qupee animation jumps from lane to lane on the monitors and stops at a random player (or a pre-selected player) who has then won the game. The winner is advised on their lane monitor, while other players are displayed an arrow indicating the winner's lane.

The Access to Pogo Pin Special Function
privilege is necessary to activate this game.

Pogo Pin Setup

Select a group of open lanes for which player names have already been entered and then press Initialize
in the Pogo Pin
window to access the settings.

In the Pogo Pin Winner
box, the winner's name is displayed as is the lane on which the game was won. Pressing here, the operator can access the table of players available and choose the winner manually.

To choose the winner manually, the Change Pogo Pin Winner
privilege is necessary.

The Duration
of the animation can also be set here through Max Duration
, that is calculated on the basis of the number of lanes and the number of players.

The Volume
of the Pogo Pin game can be modified independently to the volume settings applied to the lanes.

Once the winner, duration and volume have been set, press Start
to send the Pogo Pin session to the lanes.

Press Cancel
to clear the display of the winner on the monitor.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Special Functions - BES & Bowland > Pogo Pin

=========== Conqueror-2-103.html — 13.2  Lucky Train ===========

13.2 Lucky Train

13.2 Lucky Train

BES Only

Lane Status > Special Functions

A bell rings: Lucky Train crosses all lane monitors and then stops, leaving a car in front of each lane. All the car doors open and the player on the lane where Qupee steps down from the car wins, while arrows on the other lane monitors point to the winner's lane.

The Access to Lucky Train Special Function
privilege is necessary to activate this game.

Lucky Train Setup

Press Lucky Train
in the Special Functions to access the settings.

Select a group of Lanes
on which the Lucky Train global game will be executed and choose a Winner
(a random player is proposed but it can be changed with another one).

Set the Duration
of the Lucky Train trip, i.e. the number of seconds between the moment it starts and the revelation of the winner. The suggested Range
is calculated on the basis of the number of lanes and the number of players.

The suggested Volume
of the Lucky Train game is by default the one applied to the lanes, but it can be modified.

Once the winner, the duration and the volume have been set, press Start
to start the Lucky Train on the lanes.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Special Functions - BES & Bowland > Lucky Train

=========== Conqueror-2-105.html — 15.  Lane Options - BES &amp; Bowland ===========

15. Lane Options - BES & Bowland

15. Lane Options - BES & Bowland

Lane Option Sets and Bowling Modes prepare the environment for faster and easier lane opening.

Lane Options refer to the options that control the lanes and lane monitors.

Lane Options can be grouped into pre-prepared sets in standard configuration for certain situations e.g. for children, for tournaments or for "glow" nights. These sets can be utilized when opening lanes, while the lane is already open or they can be associated a Bowling Mode and can be modified when being used.

A Bowling Mode is a comprehensive configuration of default values with Lane Opening Modes, Price Keys and sets of Lane Options grouped together to form the mode that can then be programmed to be activated in certain time zones and on certain days (e.g. for Saturday nights) or set manually when necessary.

Lane Options can be accessed by pressing Options
button in the Lane Control, in the Lane Status module and in the Reservation Details window (Booking System).

The module that opens displays a list on the left, containing the sets already created and the individual options, ready for selection in order to send them to the lanes through the Send to Lane
option.

To prepare a set of Lane Options, go to Setup > Bowling Setup > Lane Options. The module that opens is that same as that accessed through Lane Status or the Booking System, with the New
, Delete
and Save
buttons instead of the Send to Lane
option.

In the setup window it is possible to create, modify or eliminate Lane Options sets.

To create a Lane Options set press New
, enter an appropriate name, activate or deactivate the options constituting the set in the various tabs according to preference and press Save
. A brief Description
of the set can also be entered in the space under the Option Sets
list.

To modify an existing set, select the set from the Option Sets
list, make the desired changes in the tabs and save.

To eliminate a superfluous set, select it and press Delete
.

The above modifications can only be made if the set is not currently being utilized in a Bowling Mode.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Lane Options - BES & Bowland

15.2 Basic

15.2 Basic

Environment

where the pinsetter is to function normally

to deactivate the practice session.

, Low Game
, Odd/Even
or 3-6-9
) and setting the rules for them.

By choosing to send a BES Birthday Party
to the lanes, the Birthday Photo
button appears in the bottom right hand corner: press it to access the Birthday Party photo settings.

YOUTOONS

This option allows you to take bowlers inside a game. As soon as they take their picture, the game begins. YouToons merges those images into the graphical animations, turning bowlers into hilarious characters on the screen. You can choose your favorite YouToons game from the drop down menu located at the top of the YouToons window. You can add, modify or delete any YouToons section using the three buttons at the top right of the window. Use the three buttons at the bottom of the window to take pictures that will be merged into the graphical animations.

Check this option if Special Games are to be activated for all players. It is then possible to deactivate any players not wishing to participate, by deselecting the Special Games
option in their Bowler Options.

Deselect this option to deactivate Special Games on the lanes, activating only customers willing to participate.

Choose one of the previously set redemption configurations.

Set None
to disable the redemption ticket dispenser.

Activates the bumpers for the lane. Choose between Down
, Game Only
and Practice + Game
.

5 Frames Mode

It activates 5 frames game. 

Current Frame Format

Keyboard

This option enables the use of the on-lane keyboard.

It activates the Qubica Key
in Bowland Centers and the Modifications
tab in BES Centers (which contains the functions available from the lanes).

This option is available if the Keyboards have been previously enabled in Lane Setup.

Environment from Console

BES Only

Enable this option to allow the bowlers to choose the animation set from their console.

Animation

BES Only

Displays animation for 9 score, Strike, Spare, etc. on the lane monitors.

Sound

BES Only

Activates the audio on lanes.

Speed

This setting displays the speed of each ball throw on the lane monitor.

Spare Finder

BES Only

After the first bowl, the Spare Finder
displays the remaining pins and the bowl necessary to achieve a Spare.

Choose which Spare Finder to activate in the scroll down menu between:

a Static
picture,
an animation,
a Tutor
suggestion: in addition to a themed animation, an instruction is displayed, explaining how to throw the second bowl, e.g. "Hit pin 1 on the right side to send it to the 2 pin". This Spare Finder Tutor is also present in a Fast
version.
Statistics
can be displayed for each of the players, regarding the highest score, best score, the number of Strikes, Spares, Gutters, etc...

Swap Lanes

Select this option so that players bowling on lanes opened in pair mode exchange lanes after a set number of frames played (1,2,5 or never).

Swap Every Game

=========== Conqueror-2-107.html — 15.3  Advanced ===========

15.3 Advanced

15.3 Advanced

Pinsetter on with Sign-in Screen

Enables the starting of the pinsetter during the insertion of player names on the lane keyboard.

This option is deactivated by default with the pinsetter off during name insertion thus saving electricity: in which case the pinsetters are activated by pressing the Red Key
when the customers are ready to play (i.e. after they have changed their shoes and entered their names).

Begin Practice When First Ball is Thrown

If this option remains selected (as by default), practice time starts from the moment the first ball is bowled.

Deselect this option if the practice time is to start from the moment the lane is opened from the front desk.

This option is available only when the practice is measured in time and not by the number of bowls.

Warning Message

Enable here to display the Warning Message prior to lane opening.

When the message is displayed on the lane, bowlers have to acknowledge it to open the lane.

In BES X and Bowland-X Centers, this message is customizable and translatable in the Center Setup.

Print Score after Closure

Enable this option if the scores are to be printed automatically upon lane closure.

When opening lanes in the case of a tournament squad or a league week, a message warns that this option is enabled, so the operator can choose to confirm the printout or to disable it in order to save paper.

The score sheets are printed by the printer specified in the Terminal Setup.

Stop Game

BES only

Activates the Stop
key on the lane keyboard.

Start new game from sign-in

Allows players to start a new game from sign-in.

Start New Game

Allows players to start a new game by using the SuperTouch device even if the current game is not yet over.

Close Lane from console

Allows players to close the lane from the SuperTouch.

Score Correction

This enables players to correct their scores using the lane keyboard.

By enabling this option in a BES Center, the Delete Throw option is automatically activated as well.

By enabling this option in a Bowland Center, the Disable Delete Bowl option is automatically activated as well.

Delete Throw

BES and BES X Only

This enables players to delete the last two bowls using the lane keyboard.

This option is available only if the Score Correction option is enabled.

Your Lane Your Way

BES Only

This allows bowlers to choose/modify the on-lane environment.

Foul Input Check

This option is only visible if the Advanced Foul Device has not been set in the Center Setup.

Activates the electronic device that makes sure that the players don't exceed the foul line.

If the foul line is surpassed, the foul symbol is displayed in the player's score.

Light setting

This option is set by default on the Bowling Mode choice; this means that the options selected in the active Bowling Mode will be applied to that lane.

By clicking in the field, it will be open a window where deciding a custom color for the open lane light.

The Enable Event Light Effects
option activates colored LED flashes during the welcome and the warning messages and game events (Strike, Spare etc.).

Pressing OK
in the LED Color Effec
t box, the color selected will be displayed.

Picture Validation

BES X Only

Enable here the Picture Validation for the photos taken from the SuperTouch console on the lanes.

Speed Limit

Set the limit, above which the speed of the bowl is no longer displayed on the monitor and the counter must be reset at the front desk.

This deters players from competing for the fastest bowl, as throwing as fast as possible is dangerous and could cause damage.

Hyper Speed Limit

It sets Hyper Speed Limit range.

Advanced Foul Mode

This option is only visible if Advanced Foul Device has been set in the Center Setup.

Three different states can be managed from Conqueror:

Off

On
(if the foul line is surpassed, an alarm sounds and the foul symbol is displayed in the player's score)
Warning Only
(the foul line is passed, only the sound alert is activated, with no effect on the score)

Game Eligibility

Set the games played as For the Center Average
or not, or Official Games
. This option can be modified after the event has been played in the Game Printouts module.

Club

Choose the minimum final score (between 175, 200, 225, 250, 275, 300) for which to have animation displayed on the lane monitor.

When players reach the points set here, the monitor displays an animation like "Club 200"!

For five-pin bowling it is possible to choose between 200, 250, 300, 350, 400 and 450.

Slow Bowling

Select the number of minutes that bowling can lapse before the front desk is alerted and animation is displayed on the lane monitor requesting the bowlers to recommence play.

Walk Down Time

Set the amount of time it usually takes for bowlers from the moment their lane is opened at the front desk, to when they are present and ready for play on the lanes.

For example if a lane is opened for a group at 3:30 pm for an hour's play, and Walk Down Time
is set at 2 minutes, the hour of play will commence at 3:32 pm.

Choose between 1 and 14 minutes, Off
if not wishing to use the setting (such as when opening lanes through game mode), Unlimited
if not defining the amount of time, commencing the time from the moment the lane is opened.

Walk down time finishes as soon as the first ball is bowled.

Upper and Lower Monitor Mode

BES Only

Select the layout for the lane monitor grid from the following:

Default
- normal layout with each lane on its own monitor
12 Player Mode
- when there are more than six players to a lane, by setting this mode, the player rows shrink to allow for more players
Score Grid Share Mode
- if two lanes are open, the data of both lanes can be displayed on one screen, leaving the other free to watch television or advertisements and the like
Super Wide 
- Spread one lane's image across both monitors in a pair for a super-wide view. This permits the score display of one lane to be spread out over two monitors, that is, the first five frames on the left monitor and the second five on the right.
Super Wide Share 
- spread two lanes across the pair. This permits the score display of two lanes to be spread out over two monitors, that is, the first five frames on the left monitor and the second five on the right.

Pip TV
- this option displays the television in the right-hand corner of the monitor (not available for 3QT hardware)
.

Super Wide
- this permits the score display of one lane to be spread out over two monitors, that is, the first five frames on the left monitor and the second five on the right.
Super Wide Share
- this permits the score display of two lanes to be spread out over two monitors, that is, the first five frames on the left monitor and the second five on the right.

Welcome Screen

Option available only for SuperTouch, if the Touch screen/Keyboard option is enabled.

Click to open the Welcome Screen setup.

From the Preview for scroll down menu it is possible to choose the type of Welcome Screen between:

Open
Reservation
League/Tournament

When opening, the preview of the standard default Welcome Screen is displayed (blue background).

All the types contain the lane number on the left and the welcome courtesy sentence (title) on the top: Welcome to [center name].

On the right:

For the Open type, the player names are displayed. In the case of opening without specifying the player names, it will be displayed the number of players (e.g. "3 players"),
For the Reservation type, the reservation name is displayed in red with the number of players,
For the League/Tournament type, the league/tournament name is displayed along with the week number, the team is displayed in red with the player names below.

To customize the Welcome Screen, it is possible to:

modify the title, writing in the two lines on the bottom left (press the refresh button to update the preview);
choose an image by clicking in the Background list on the right; immediately the preview will be update; 

To add an image, click on the + button on the right of the Background list. To remove an image click on rubber button.

Please note that the perfect size for the picture is 1280*720.

Clicking on the OK button, the Welcome Screen defined will be saved and in the Advanced tab of Lane Options module the Welcome Screen box will display the name of the custom picture selected as background.

To remove the custom Welcome Screen defined click on "X" and the standard default style Welcome Screen will be displayed during lane opening.

When the lane associated to open bowling, reservation or league/tournament will be opened, the saved Welcome Screen will be displayed on the lane monitors and on the SuperTouch (if present).

15.4 Bowling and Lane Services

15.4 Bowling and Lane Services

BES Only

The Bowling Services
and Lane Services
menus allow bowlers to select various functions from the lane keyboard.

Bowling Services

The first menu, Bowling Services
, proposes:

Service Call
: allows bowlers to call the front desk operator when necessary. When this occurs, the Lane Status module displays the SOS flashing light on the relevant lane.

Statistics
: proposes statistics on the highest score, best score, the number of Strikes, Spares, Gutters, etc. for each of the players.

: allows the players to call for technical assistance through the Trouble Call System. When the call is registered, the players can see the progression of the work in progress through the animation on the monitors (the telephone ringing, QuPee working on the problem, etc.).

Like the grid on overhead monitors, the new one displays the list of the bowlers currently playing on the lane, whilst the type of score displayed in the "Total" column depends on the option previously set in the Lane Options module.

=========== Conqueror-2-109.html — 15.5  Modifications ===========

15.5 Modifications

15.5 Modifications

BES Only

This tab is enabled if the Keyboard
option is checked in the Basic
tab.

It permits the players to activate the following functions directly from the keyboard.

Correct Bowler

This activates the Correct Bowler
function on the lane keyboard, enabling the modification of player information.

Modify Bowler Name

Allows the modification of the name of the current player.

Modify Bowler Handicap

Allows the player to correct their handicap.

Change Line up

Allows the players to change the line-up of the current game.

Edit Blind Score

Allows players to edit a Blind player's score if corrections are necessary.

Skip/Unskip a Bowler

Allows the players to pause for a player that has to skip frames (if they are absent for a few minutes).

This pause cannot continue past the current game; therefore this option must be removed in order to finish the game.

Modify Bumper Status

Allows the deactivation of the bumpers.

Modify Bowler Gender

Allows to correct the gender of the player on lanes.

Modify Bowler Hand

Allows the player to correct their hand, between right-handed and left-hander.

Edit Prebowled Scores

Here the players can insert, modify or cancel the total scores of pre-bowled games.

Select Special Game Bowler

Permits the players to choose whether they wish to participate in the Special Games.

Substitute League Bowler

Allows the players to replace a league player with a substitute recognized by the system through their ID.

Edit No Tap Values

Allows the players to modify the values for the No Tap Classic Game.

Add/Remove Bowler

Enable various bowler modifications:

Add Regular
: a new team member can be added here.
Remove Bowler
: a league or team member can be removed here.
Add Pacer
: allows a Pacer to be set or another player to be indicated as a Pacer.
Add Best Ball Bowler
: allows the setting of a virtual Best Ball Bowler. The Best Ball Bowler's game is constructed by the system selecting the actual players' best bowls.
Add Best Frame Bowler
: allows the setting of a virtual Best Frame Bowler. The Best Frame Bowler's game is constructed by the system selecting the actual players' best frames.

FB Sign in

Enable to allow the members to sign-in from the lane console by entering their FB ID. Members who sign in by themselves from the lanes will be highlighted in the Lane Status so the operator can easily identify them.

Correct Team

This activates the Correct Team
function on the lane keyboard, enabling the modification of team and player information: Correct Team Name
and Correct Team Handicap
, allowing players to modify their team name and handicap.

Display Card Hands

Enables the Poker Hand
option on the keyboard, that allows the display of the cards received up to that moment.

Give/Remove a Card

This option enables the Deal Card
and Remove Card
options on the keyboard, allowing the request or removal of a card.

For example, an extra card could be requested when a pin that guaranteed a Strike, fell late and an undeserved card could be removed when a pin falls provoking an accidental Strike.

=========== Conqueror-2-111.html — 15.7  Score Style ===========

15.7 Score Style

15.7 Score Style

The following settings allow the modification/management of the Score Grid.

Display Consecutive Strikes

By checking this option, if consecutive Strikes have been achieved, the score of each Strike is displayed on the grid after the next two bowls.

If this option remains unchecked, consecutive Strike scores are displayed together as soon a non-strike bowl has been performed.

Display Incomplete Frames

Select to display the score after each bowl.

Unchecked, the score is displayed as usual, after the second bowl when the frame is complete.

Handicap per Frame

The player's handicap is included in the single frame score.

Incomplete Frames in Team Total

The team total increases bowl by bowl, without waiting for the end of the frame.

Display Game Total with Handicap

The handicap is added to the total game score.

Bowler Initials

To view only the bowler's initials in the grid.

Message in Pair Mode

Enables the Scrolling Messages to flow continuously from one screen to the next on 2 lanes opened in pair mode.

If not checked, the message appears separately on each monitor.

Change Grid Color with Game

Changes the grid color at each game.

Game 1 is green, game 2 yellow, 3 red, 4 green and so on.

Strike = X - Spare = /

Displays the Strikes and Spares as symbols in the grid.

Unchecked, they are displayed respectively as dark squares and triangles.

Reset Message

Displays a message alerting to the reset after the tenth frame.

Carry over Game Total

Here it is possible to have cumulative score of previous games together with the current game score which adapts accordingly as the game progresses.

Highlight Pre-paid

Select to highlight the lanes on which payment has already been received. Highlighted with the dollar sign in BES and with a red square in Bowland.

Frame/Time Counter

Displays the frame counter/minutes in the corner of the game grid where the number of the current game is usually displayed.

If the game is pre-assigned, the counter decreases, while if post-assigned, the counter increases.

Zoom to 5 Frames

Increases the size of the current player's score, only displaying the last 5 frames where 10 are usually displayed.

Zoom Frame Numbers

BES Only

This option is only active if the previous option has been set. It increases the size of the frame numbers, only displaying the last 5 frames where 10 are usually displayed.

Smart Best Game

If playing with Best Frame Bowler, this option does not allow the remaining players to bowl if the score of the current frame cannot be beaten, speeding up the game.

For example, the second player bowls a Strike and therefore the other 4 players don't bowl, as Strike is the highest score.

These players' frames are automatically completed with the Gutter score (zero).

All Bowlers on Screen

Bowland & Bowland-X Only

The space between lines is reduced as much as possible so all players can always be displayed on the lane monitor.

Carry over Best Game

BES Only

With this option enabled the Best Ball Bowler game is constructed utilizing the players' best ball/frames of all the games played within the event, and not only the ones in the current game.

Show Empty Frames

BES Only

Check this option to view the frames yet to be played on the monitor.

This is unchecked by default, allowing the background animation to be displayed.

Flip Screens in Pair Mode

When players change lanes in pair mode, this option enables slow animation simulating the exchange from one monitor to the other.

The alternative (unchecked) is a rapid update from screen to screen. With Bowland-X, however, arrows display the lanes that the players are to bowl on.

Display Bowler Total

BES Only

The display of the bowler total column on the lane monitors, can be selected from this scroll menu.

Display Team Total

BES Only

Here the display of the totals row on the lane monitors can be chosen.

Only complete games in totals

Only complete games are displayed in the total.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Lane Options - BES & Bowland > Score Style

=========== Conqueror-2-113.html — 15.9  Classic Games ===========

15.9 Classic Games

15.9 Classic Games

This title refers to alternative bowling games.

It is possible to identify the lanes on which these games are run through the colored icon in Lane Status.

Supplementary rules: when a Split Converted is scored, a Strike is attributed and when a Split is scored, a Spare is attributed.

3 6 9

Here the 3rd, 6th and 9th frames are not played as players are automatically attributed three Strikes.

With BES, Bowland and Bowland-X, the 3-6-9 default can be changed to any other frame, by using the buttons 1-9.

Even Better

One ball per frame at a full rack

In this game, the player must bowl an even number of pins, but not a strike.

If the player knocks down an even number of pins, his score is normally recorded.

If the player knocks down an odd number of pins or a strike or a gutter, the score will be zero.

The maximum score per game is 80.

Head Hunter

One ball per frame at a full rack - 10 frames

The object is to hit the head pin without getting a strike.

If you don't hit the head pin or scores a strike, the score will be zero.

The 1st ball of each frame is displayed and managed as a gutter (it counts zero and it does not add to the previous frame, in case it was a spare).

To score, the ball must hit the head pin, and your score will be how many pins you knock down.

If you hit 7, 6, 5 or 4 pins, you get a bonus that converts your score to 10 (displayed as a spare).

The maximum score is 100.

Last Pin Standing

Two ball at one rack - maximum

The object is to knock down as many pins without hitting the head pin.

If you knock down pins (but not head pin) on the 1st ball, you can throw the 2nd ball.

If the 2nd ball knocks down the head pin, hits no pin or is a gutter ball, the player receives for that frame only the score of his 1st ball.

If the player throws two balls, knocking down pins every time, but without hitting the head pin he receives his score plus 3 point bonus.

If all pins (except the head pin) fall down on the 1st ball, the player gets a strike and has 5 point bonus; in this case the 2nd ball will not be thrown.

If you hit all pins but the head with the 2nd bowl, you get a spare.

In the case of a gutter (or head pin 1 fallen), the frame is over (if it occurs on the 2nd ball, the score for the frame will be what done on 1st ball)

The maximum score per game is 300.

Low Game

The aim of this game is to have the lowest possible score without throwing the ball into the gutter. It is therefore necessary that the players try to knock down only one pin with each throw (the lowest score possible is 20).

The players must avoid the gutters on the first bowl because a Gutter throw is recognized as a Strike; on the second bowl a Gutter is recognized as a Spare, while all other scores are counted as normal (e.g. Strike = Strike, 3= 3, etc.).

Fouls are managed as gutters as well.

No Tap

If the first bowl in the frame scores 7 or more, 8 or more or 9 or more (according to the settings), it is converted to a Strike.

Odd Duck

One ball per frame at a full rack

In this game, the player must bowl an odd number of pins, and not a strike.

If the player knocks down an odd number of pins, his score is normally recorded.

If the player knocks down an even number of pins or a strike or a gutter, the score will be zero.

The maximum score per game is 90.

Odds and Evens

In this game, the player must bowl an even number of pins to be automatically attributed a Strike. When an odd number of pins is bowled, the player is automatically attributed a Spare on the second frame, regardless of the actual number of pins knocked down.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Lane Options - BES & Bowland > Classic Games

=========== Conqueror-2-114.html — 15.10  Signature Grids ===========

15.10 Signature Grids

15.10 Signature Grids

BES Only

Setup > Bowling Setup > Lane Options > Basic > Environment

Click on the picture preview to choose the Environment to be displayed on the lane monitors. In the window that opens, simply click on the environment picture to send it to the lane.

By clicking on Custom
, the Signature Grids
is accessed, where it is possible to choose its style, colors and a mix of animation themes to be displayed.

option is automatically disabled.

between those available and then choose a Background

options are disabled.

Loading Custom Pictures

In addition to those available (fixed in the system), it is possible to load a picture by clicking on + 
.

Pictures loaded in this module are kept in Conqueror Pro until they are deleted with the eraser button.

After loading 150 pictures, it is necessary to delete some of them in order to be able to load others.

All common file formats are supported (jpg, png, bmp, gif). If the pictures are smaller, they will be centered on the screen, while if the pictures are bigger they will be downsized.

to save your custom grid.

=========== Conqueror-2-116.html — 16.  BES Birthday Parties ===========

16. BES Birthday Parties

16. BES Birthday Parties

With the BES birthday party package, pictures of the birthday child and their friends can be taken at the front desk and then displayed on the lane monitors while they are bowling.

As bowlers proceed through the game, photos are displayed with every frame in a specially dedicated environment that combines 2D and 3D graphical elements.

An optional setting is available with BES Birthday parties: the Magic Wheel Special Game can appear randomly awarding bowlers with an extra special bonus surprise.

=========== Conqueror-2-117.html — 16.2  Enabling a Birthday Party on the Lanes ===========

16.2 Enabling a Birthday Party on the Lanes

16.2 Enabling a Birthday Party on the Lanes

Birthday Parties are enabled in the Lane Options module, in two different ways:

By Choosing the Default Birthday Lane Option Set

Choose the default Lane Option Set
(called Birthday
) in the left part of the screen: it includes the Birthday environment and enables the Birthday Photo
button, in order to take photos of the party children.

A new Birthday session is automatically created for the current lanes and is easily identifiable thanks to its session ID: Birthday sessions automatically created are called Birthday #1, Birthday #2, etc.

If there is a birthday party already running on the lanes, it is possible to link the lane that is currently being opened to it. A list of the current birthday parties is proposed to the operator, together with the lanes on which they are running: select the appropriate birthday party to join.

By Choosing a Customized Session

It is also possible to choose a customized birthday session for the lane that is currently being opened by clicking on the Special Game
field and then select the birthday session in the window that opens.

Customized birthday sessions can be created in Special Game Setup, in the Birthday
tab, by pressing on New
.

LANE MANAGEMENT > BES Birthday Parties > Enabling a Birthday Party on the Lanes

=========== Conqueror-2-121.html — 18.  Lights - Lane Effects ===========

18. Lights - Lane Effects

18. Lights - Lane Effects

Setup > Bowling Setup > Lights - Lane Effects

A set of default effects is provided, but new sets with custom effects can be added.

Legacy AMF scoring system not supported

States and Events Default Effects

States
supported are listed in the first tab, with a default light effect already associated. Events 
are listed in the second tab, with a default light effect already associated. The latter is supported only in BES X centers.

Managing New Light Effect Combinations

Select, from the dropdown menu, the combination to modify, copy or delete; or press +
to create a new one. Each effect (States/Events) of the combination can be customized using the Edit Effect
button. The Test Effect
button sends the selected effect to the lane for a few seconds.

Customizing a Light Effect

To edit a state or event, double click or press Edit Effect
.

States: select Off
to disable any effect; Deck and Bumper Lights have two different to be customized separately. However, Deck and Bumpers cannot run a different Light Effect
. The features of the light effects are the same as those discussed in the previous paragraph (Lights - Global Moods). Flag Join Global Mode
to apply the Global Mood to the selected state. Lane on and Practice have an additional feature, called Ball tracking. To activate it, select an option from the dropdown menu and choose between "Cursor" and "Comet". The former is a light that follows the ball, and the latter creates a "glow effect" on the bumpers.

Events: this tab indicates events occurring during a game. To open the Event setup window double click on an event or press Edit Effect
. Deck and Bumpers lights work as previously described. Select No Effect to keep the "Lane on" effect even when events occur. Modify the duration of an effect using the "Duration" box. As an example, strikes can last longer than faults.

Overriding Lane Effect or Global Mood

Use this function to temporarily override lane effects and/or global moods on a given lane set. Use it on: a single lane / group of lanes to set a color or an effect for testing or maintenance purposes; celebrating an event, such as birthday parties; or on the entire center / selected areas of the center to override Global Mood sequences defined in the Bowling mode for a certain amount of time. After the time is elapsed, lanes return automatically to their associated light effect.

Associating a Light Combination to a Bowling Mode

A Lane Lighting
Combination
can be chosen for each Bowing Mode. The lights combination can be different for different lane sets. The Lane Lighting box consists of two sections; click on Lane Effects and/or Global Mood to open the drop-down menu from which the light combination can be selected.

Light Effect Combination Lane Option

Use the Lane Options module (Advanced tab) to define a new Light Setting
, doing so allows overriding the Bowling Mode
settings. To change light settings, select the Custom option and choose a combination from the drop-down list.

Lane Lighting Server Setup

Setup > Technical Setup > Lane Lighting Server Setup

Enable the Deck Lighting Server
by entering the Serial Number
in the dedicated field and clicking Enabled
. The red/green light indicate the server status.

After a version upgrade, the 
programs must be uploaded. In this case, the plugin automatically opens, and the program upload can be directly managed.

Open Lane Console Light

It is possible to set up and personalized the console lights. The user can select any set of lights currently available by default. Please beware: there are no available effects for console lights.

Enable Console Event Effects

In some occasions, such as "Sign In", console lights can vary.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Lights - Lane Effects

19. Lights - Global Moods

The Lights - Global Mood module defines the global effects of the center and its general mood. Use this module to setup deck, bumpers and capping lights. To maximize the efficiency of this module, create moods in advance and use them on a later stage. Global Moods can run on closed lanes only.

19.1 Managing Global Moods

The Global Moods window allows you to add, modify or choose a sequence of effects, which can include Lane Lights (Deck only/Deck + Bumpers lights) and Capping lights.

There are four buttons and one drop-down menu at the top of the window.

Use it to select an existing Global Mood or in alternative:

Add a new Global Mood by clicking the + 
sign.
Duplicate the current Global Mood by clicking the Duplicate button.
Edit the current Global Mood by clicking the Edit button to rename it.
Delete the current Global Mood by clicking the delete button.

=========== Conqueror-2-125.html — 19.3  Sequence Item Setup ===========

19.3 Sequence Item Setup

19.3 Sequence Item Setup

Double click on any sequence to open the Sequence Item Setup window either to modify Lane Lights Effects or Capping Lights Effects.

Modifying Lane Lights and Capping lights 

Select an effect from the Lane Lights list to modify:

From
/To Color
: Define the color range that you want to rotate (From) and the color range you want to rotate (To). Double click on the color to open the color palette. 
Speed
: Change Speed of the effect, open the dropdown menu and select your speed. 
Direction:
Change the direction of the effect, from left to right or vice-versa.
Duration:
Edit the duration of the sequence, just add the duration in the Duration box
Size: 
Change the size of the colored section on the bumpers.
Delay: 
Change the delay value to modify the color transition.
Deck lights only: 
The effect runs only on deck lights.
Deck + bumper light:
The effect runs on deck and bumpers lights. 

Please note: some features are available for limited effects only.

=========== Conqueror-2-126.html — 20.  Lane Options - BOSS ===========

20. Lane Options - BOSS

20. Lane Options - BOSS

Bowler Keyboard

This option is enabled by default because it allows bowlers to start to play. If a tournament is taking place and only the tournament director is allowed to carry out modifications, this option will be disabled so players cannot control the keyboard.

Action Graphics

By enabling this option, static graphics are displayed on lane monitors when a player scores a Strike
, a Spare
, a Split
, a Gutter,
etc.

Pindication

Enables a fixed image on the monitors that shows the throw and the number of pins still standing.

Spare Maker

Option available only if the Pindication has been enabled.

Displays the bowl necessary to achieve a Spare.

Color Sequencing

This option allows to change the color of the grid and of the whole background of the screen at the beginning of every game.

Exciter Bumper Lights

Enables the lights on the bumpers that flash after a Strike or Spare.

Bumpers

Activates the Bumpers.

Bumpers by Player

If the Bumpers option has been activated, it is possible to enable this option that allows to activate Bumpers for single players in Lane Control, through the Bowler Options.

Baker

Through the Baker
option, the first frame of the game is played by the first player, the second frame of the same game by the second player and so on.

Classic Game

This option allows to set one of the Classic Games or one of the Skill Games.

Swap Lanes

Players bowling on lanes opened in pair mode can exchange lanes:

after every frame: Alternating

never: No Alternating

after every game: Alternating after Game

An arrow appears next to the name indicating the lane the player is on at the moment.

Frames per Bowler

This setting defines the number of frames each player has to play before another player's turn. 

The possibilities are: 1 frame
, 2 frames
, 5 frames
, 10 frames
.

Language

Select a language to be used on the lanes from the ones available in the scroll down menu.

Practice

Practice can be: On with Pins
, On without Pins
, Off
.

Practice Time

Set practice duration in minutes.

Walk Down

Only for Time mode

If disabled, time starts running upon lane opening. If enabled time starts running only after the first throw.

Print Score after Closure

Enable this option if the scores are to be printed automatically upon lane closure.

When opening lanes in the case of a tournament squad or a league week, a message warns that this option is enabled, so the operator can choose to confirm the printout or to disable it in order to save paper.

The score sheets are printed by the printer specified in the Terminal Setup.

Game Eligibility

Set the games played as For the Center Average
or not, or Official Games
. This option can be modified after the event has been played in the Game Printouts module.

Image Set for Lanes off

Select the set of images to be sent to the lanes at lane closure. The choices are: Advertising 1
, Advertising 2
, Advertising 3
, Logo
, Screen Saver
and Blank State
(no image). Advertising
sets of images have to be previously set in the Advertising module, while Logo
, Screen Saver
and Blank State
are fixed in the system.

Display Format

It is possible to choose how to display the score on the lanes between: 10 frames
, 5 frames
, Tournament format
.

Chase Light Pattern

Choose a default Chase Light Pattern
in the scroll down menu between: Reverse
, Forward
, All Flash
, Twinkles
, All on
, 1-2-3-2-1
, Forward Dark Spot
, Reverse Dark Spot
, 10
or define a Custom Pattern
by choosing the relative option.

A Custom Pattern
is made up of several sequences of 3 lights, and each sequence is customizable. Choose the Number of Sequences
the custom pattern is made of, configure each Sequence
by clicking on the light bulbs (e.g. "on-off-on") and press Start Preview
to display the custom pattern. The sequences are alternated and a second will pass between a sequence and another.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Lane Options - BOSS

Bowler Keyboard

If disabled, bowlers cannot use the Next Game
function, substitute bowlers from keyboard and modify bowler name and games. If a tournament is taking place and only the tournament director is allowed to carry out modifications, this option has to be disabled so players cannot control the keyboard.

function from the keyboard. The game currently being played will be interrupted and the new game will start.

, a Split

option, the first frame of the game is played by the first player, the second frame of the same game by the second player and so on.

3-6-9
: the 3rd, 6th and 9th frames are not played because three Strikes are automatically assigned to players.

it is possible to set practice, which means bowling without arrows and without keeping scores
the maximum number of bowlers on the lane is 5, but the roster can include up to 10 players (5 regular players and 5 team substitutes to choose from on the lane)

and Tournament
format.

On with Pins

Off

=========== Conqueror-2-129.html — 23.  Lane Options - Frameworx ===========

=========== Conqueror-2-130.html — 24.  Bowling Modes ===========

24. Bowling Modes

24. Bowling Modes

The term Bowling Mode
refers to the set of default options (including the opening modes, the default price keys and the lane options), that prevails upon the general options set in Center Setup > Lane Control.

For maximum efficiency, Bowling Modes are programmed to be used in certain time zones and on certain days, but they can also be set manually when necessary.

For example, a Bowling mode can be prepared with predefined settings for Saturday night and then activated for the "every Saturday, from 8 to 12" time zone, meaning that Saturday evenings will be a lot easier for the cashiers in that they are spared the task of selecting price keys and Lane Options.

A special Bowling Mode can be prepared for school children in that when a school group arrives at the Center, the preset "Junior" Bowling Mode can be activated manually that automatically activates price keys for children or youth groups, activating the bumpers and deactivating the keyboard on the lane.

These sets can be saved with an appropriate name in order to be easily retrieved.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Bowling Modes

=========== Conqueror-2-131.html — 24.2  Bowling Mode Management ===========

24.2 Bowling Mode Management

24.2 Bowling Mode Management

In the external frame, under the clock, an area indicates the Bowling Day and which Bowling Mode is currently effective.

Click on this field to shortcut to the Bowling Modes
module, where it is possible to modify it, to activate another Bowling Mode, or setup a new one.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Bowling Modes > Bowling Mode Management

=========== Conqueror-2-132.html — 24.3  Bowling Mode Setup ===========

24.3 Bowling Mode Setup

24.3 Bowling Mode Setup

Setup > Bowling Setup > Bowling Modes

Here it is possible to create new Bowling Modes and modify existing ones.

Select a Bowling Mode
from the scroll menu to view its settings (Description
, Set of Lane Options
, Message
, price keys, etc.), to Delete
it or to modify it.

Creating a Bowling Mode

Press New
to create a new Bowling Mode, then insert a name and Description
.

Set of Lane Options

Select from the scroll menus which Set of Lane Options
for single/pair of lanes.

Message

Choose the Message to send to the lane for this Bowling Mode.

Default Price Keys

Choose the default price key for game/time/unlimited opening, for the extra frames and for Time Games
.

Opening Mode

Define the lane Opening Mode
for this Bowling Mode (the Opening Modes set in Center Setup are proposed by default). For example, a Bowling Center may wish to set Saturdays as not having Unlimited
play, therefore setting Game
and Time
as the primary and secondary modes and None
as the third mode.

Unlimited Default Mode

QAMF Scoring Systems Only

Specify a limited duration or a closure time for Unlimited Mode.

Lane lighting 

Click on Lane Effect and Global Mood to open a dropdown list. Choose a new Lane Effect or/and Global Mood from the list. 

External Devices

Then choose whether the External Devices set for the Centre (such as glow lights, foul units, etc.), should be turned on or off for that Bowling Mode.

Console Lights

Decide the light color for when the lane is open and another one to use when lane is close.

By clicking on the Open Lane
and Closed Lane
fields, the color selection window will be open.

After the choices have been made, the colors selected appear in both fields.

This will be the default value, in the case the Lane Option is not modified at lane opening.

Extend Bowling Session

Lane Lighting

Click on Lane Effects or Global Mood to select a new combination of Lane Effects from the drop-down list, this helps you define Lane Effects and Global Moods separately. Lane Effects and Global Moods can also be defined for different lane sets. 

Time Zones

Define the Time Zones
for which the Bowling Mode is to be activated.

If the same Bowling Mode is to be applied to the whole table, select it in the scroll down menu and press Apply
or Enter
on the keyboard.

In order to assign the Bowling Mode to a specific time zone, the whole table must be deselected by clicking on All
. Then, select the area of the table (day of the week or specific time by clicking at the beginning of a row or at the top of a column) to which the Bowling Mode has to be to applied and press Apply
. If the Bowling Mode is not to be sold in a particular time zone, select it and press Deactivate
and the area concerned is cleared.

To select an interval of time, press the Shift
key on the keyboard and click on the first and the last cells of the desired area. If single cells of the table are to be selected, press the Ctrl
key on the keyboard and click on the cells to be selected.

To scroll through the timetable displaying the three different time zones of the bowling day (divided in 30 minute cells) use the two arrows
on the right. Here the earliest time displayed is the one set as Day Changing Time.

The last three columns of the table, called EX1, EX2, EX3, represent three extra holidays that each Center can define for its particular needs, by going to Setup > Calendar Setup.

MMS Service Set

An MMS Set is a predefined set of MMS services, which defines what goes on every monitor when a given bowling mode is running. By default, all bowling modes share the same Service set, i.e. the only MMS configuration that the center has been using until the upgrade. From the MMS Setup, centers can override "on the fly" what goes on a given monitor (e.g. the scoring of a tournament's final), without altering the default configuration. The default service will come back on when the bowling mode changes again. 

To add an MMS Service, use the New 
button and choose an MMS Service for each monitor. Once the changes have been saved, select it from the MMS Service Set drop-down. 

The ability to schedule MMS by bowling mode does not require any license.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Bowling Modes > Bowling Mode Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-133.html — 25.  Virtual Waiter and Video Intercom Setup ===========

25. Virtual Waiter and Video Intercom Setup

25. Virtual Waiter and Video Intercom Setup

Setup > Bowling Setup > Virtual Waiter

Virtual Waiter allows customers to order F&B very comfortably, as if a real waiter was always on the lane, at their disposal. The system provides menus right on the lane through the SuperTouch device and allows the customer to order by making a video call. Bowling Center staff can also video call the lane console screen proactively, to see if the customers need anything.

When a bowler makes a call from the SuperTouch a little Lane Calling
dialog appears on Conqueror. As in a standard phone call, the operator can either accept or reject the lane call. When the operator clicks on the Accept
button, the Video Call window appears automatically. If the operator misses a call, the Virtual Waiter
Quick Button lights up - when the operator clicks on it the Video Call window opens automatically, allowing the operator to call the bowler back.

Menu File Management

Press Open
to choose the Menu file
to be displayed on the SuperTouch; then press Apply
to send it to the consoles.

Terminal Settings

In the Terminals
tab it is possible to set each terminal for Virtual Waiter
and Video Intercom
calls, associating them to specific lanes.

Assign a lane set to each terminal, and set them for Virtual Waiter
calls, for Video Intercom
calls, for both (All
) or for None
.

Press Apply
when the setup is complete.

Missed Calls Button

To have the Virtual Waiter
Quick Button enable it is necessary to choose it under Setup > Terminal Setup > Quick Buttons. 

LANE MANAGEMENT > Virtual Waiter and Video Intercom Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-134.html — 26.  Scrolling Messages ===========

26. Scrolling Messages

26. Scrolling Messages

26.1 In BES and Bowland Centers

Front Desk > Lane Control > Messages

In this section up to twelve special scrolling messages can be created in various colors, sizes and fonts and then displayed on lane monitors for purposes such as special events within the Bowling Center, holidays, package deals, etc. Once the message has been created and saved, it can be reapplied whenever necessary. Images can also be attached to these messages and sent to lane monitors.

To create a message, click on one of the twelve buttons (that represent twelve possible messages) on the left hand side of the screen and enter an appropriate Message Name
such as "Christmas", making it easy to identify each reusable message.

Write the message in the Message Text
field, which can be previewed in the miniature scrolling view window as it's being written.

At the base of the screen is a color grid where the color of the text and background can be selected by clicking on the colors.

Press Font
underneath the color bars to choose from a variety of different fonts, sizes and styles and press OK
in the Character Selection
window to confirm selections made.

To display a scrolling corporate logo, for example that of the Bowling Center or an external advertiser; click in the message text space and press Attach
, select the desired image and press OK
.

The image is then displayed in the Message Text
field between < and >. To cancel the tag inserted, delete the Message Text
content.

New images can be created in the logo file format, which should have the following settings: 16 colors, .bmp format and size 8 x 26. After an image has been created it should be saved in the server directory C:\QDesk\Database\Messages so that it can be accessed through Conqueror.

At the top of the screen, choose to which lanes the message has to be sent to by clicking in the Selected Lanes
field. The Select Lanes
screen will open and all lanes/pairs can be either selected or deselected.

Once the desired message has been created and the appropriate lanes selected, press Enable
to send it to the selected lanes. To remove an existing scrolling message from the lanes press Disable
.

These messages can also be associated with a Bowling Mode. For example if a bowling mode is applied on Saturday evenings, the scrolling message may be used with it to advertise the Saturday night happy hour or a Sunday special price.

It is not advisable to use the last programmable message space as the system overrides any message there in order to send Standings to the lanes.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Scrolling Messages > In BES and Bowland Centers

=========== Conqueror-2-135.html — 26.2  In Universal Centers ===========

26.2 In Universal Centers

26.2 In Universal Centers

In this section it is possible to create up to twelve special messages and then display them on open lane monitors for purposes such as special events in the Bowling Center, holidays, package deals, etc.

Once the message has been created and saved, it can be reapplied whenever necessary. Images can also be attached to these messages and sent to lane monitors.

To create a message, click on one of the twelve buttons (that represent twelve possible messages) on the left hand side of the screen and enter an appropriate Message Name
such as "Christmas", making it easy to identify each reusable message.

Write the message in the Message Text
field, which can be previewed in the miniature scrolling view window as it's being written.

Enter the Message Display Time
, that is the number of seconds that the message is displayed (set it to zero if the message has to be displayed constantly, until lane closure).

Choose the message type between:

Scrolling
: the message scrolls on the top part of the screen.
Full Screen
: only the message is displayed on the screen, on more than one line.

Fixed
: the message does not scroll. It is fixed on the top part of the screen. (Not available with Frameworx
)

At the top of the screen, choose to which lanes the message has to be sent to by clicking in the Selected Lanes
field. The Select Lanes
screen will open and all lanes/pairs can be either selected or deselected.

Once the desired message has been created and the appropriate lanes selected, press Enable
to send it to the selected lanes. To remove an existing scrolling message from the lanes press Disable
.

These messages can also be associated with a Bowling Mode. For example, if a bowling mode is applied on Saturday evenings, the scrolling message may be used with it to advertise the Saturday night happy hour or a Sunday special price.

It is not advisable to use the last programmable message space because the system overrides any message there in order to send Standings to the lanes.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Scrolling Messages > In Universal Centers

=========== Conqueror-2-136.html — 27.  Impact Images ===========

27. Impact Images

27. Impact Images

Front Desk > Lane Control > Impact Images

Static Impact Images
can be displayed on lane monitors through this section at opportune moments, such as when a player scores a Strike
, a Spare
, a Split
, a Gutter
or a Split Converted
. Images can also be displayed when players Foul
, others for Birthday
parties and Junior
graphics for children which are displayed when the child knocks down 1 or more pins. The Spare Finder
layout can also be chosen, containing suggestions as to how to knock down the remaining pins. There are also Off-lane
images that can be displayed on the lanes for Advertising purposes, etc.

Choose one of the ten libraries on the left, to which selected images from each tab can be added which are then displayed randomly on-screen upon impact.

Select the left and right arrows
in the tab area of the screen to move from one tab to another.

If the tab contains a lot of images, use the left and right arrows
in the center of the screen to see additional images within a specific tab.

Set Name

Name the current impact images set here.

On-lane Only/Off-lane Only/Always

In the Advertising
tab, it is possible to choose if the picture has to be displayed only when the lane is closed, only when the lane is open or always by pressing the Ctrl
key on the keyboard and contemporarily clicking on the desired picture.

Graphics Time

Refers to the amount of time (in seconds) that each image regarding play is displayed (i.e. the Strike image).

Off-lane Time

Refers to the amount of time (in seconds) that the off lane images are displayed when lanes are not in use (i.e. advertising images).

Advertising Time/Frequency

Set the amount of time in seconds that the images are displayed when lanes are in use and the frequency with which images are displayed (in seconds).

A good default example of these settings could have the advertisement displayed for 5 seconds (Advertising Time
) every 300 seconds (Advertising Frequency
).

Historical Highs

This option allows the operator to send the highest scores (as set in the Lane Highs module) to selected lanes which is useful for tournaments and the like.

Qubicards

Bowland Only

Select this option to enable Bowland Special Card Games to be sent to the lanes.

Advertising

By selecting this option, the images selected in the Advertising tab are sent to the lanes.

Sound

Check this option to activate sound for all "Exciter Images".

Copy to

By pressing the Copy to
button, the operator can copy the contents of one set to another.

Pictures Chosen

This is a current memory gauge (referring to the capacity of the VDB) which displays the amount of memory used to download the images onto the lanes and the amount remaining, in bar form. The gauge adjusts accordingly as images are added or removed.

Enable/Disable

Enable
sends the current selection to the lanes, while Disable
removes the current selection from the lanes. It is unnecessary to remove a selection before applying a new one, as the new selection automatically overwrites the current selection.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Impact Images

=========== Conqueror-2-137.html — 28.  Advertising - BES &amp; Bowland ===========

28. Advertising - BES & Bowland

28. Advertising - BES & Bowland

Advertising images to be sent to lanes via the Impact Images

module.

To load the images in this module, it is necessary to set the directory Source Path
. Having done so, the Source Images
are displayed on the left and can be converted into the .imm format by pressing Convert
.

The images loaded into Conqueror must be in .bmp format (16 or 24 bits), maximum size 512 (width) x 286 (height) pixels with BES or 256 height pixels with Bowland.

If the pictures are smaller, they will be centered on the screen, while if the pictures are bigger they will be cut out.

View

Rename

The Rename
option allows the operator to change the name of the selected file.

Delete

to delete the image selected.

Paste

The Paste
button can be utilized to copy any screen image to this module. Bring the desired image up on the computer screen and press Print Screen
on the keyboard followed by Paste
and the image is automatically saved in the .imm format, ready to be utilized by Conqueror for advertising.

All/None

The All
/ None
buttons serve to select/deselect all items in either list in order to then delete, convert or view them.

=========== Conqueror-2-139.html — 29.2  Advertising Categories ===========

29.2 Advertising Categories

Press New Category
to create a new category and to give it an appropriate name, such as "Restaurant Advertising" or "Saturday Night Packages".

To modify the name of the category, press Rename
.

To fill in the category with the desired images, press Add Image
. The image chosen and loaded will be pre-displayed as it will be shown on the lanes. The images loaded into Conqueror Pro must be in Bitmap, Jpg or Gif format, 256 colors, size 640 (width) x 480 (height) pixels. If the images are smaller they will be enlarged on the screen, while if the images are bigger they will be minimized.

while to delete a category with all the images it contains, press Delete Category
.

, to quickly synchronize the terminal with the server.

=========== Conqueror-2-140.html — 29.3  Lane Advertisings ===========

29.3 Lane Advertisings

29.3 Lane Advertisings

The sequence of advertisement images to be shown on the lanes is set in the first tab. It is possible to define maximum 3 sequences, called by default Advertising 1
, Advertising 2
and Advertising 3
.

This set of images can be sent to the lanes at lane closure through the Image Set for Lanes off Lane Option.

(from Advertising module), Logo
and Screen Saver
.

.

list.

The two arrows at the base of the Advertising Sequences
list are used to modify the display order of the images: the selected image is moved up or down according to the arrow pressed.

After selecting all the images and setting their display order, choose the lanes to which the sequences have to be sent to (From Lane
/ To Lane
) and for how many seconds every single image of the sequence has to be shown (Display Time
).

Press Save
to save the sequence and Send to Lane
to send the sequence in progress to the lane. Press Play Advertising
to enable the sequence on the monitors.

Press Stop Advertising
to interrupt the sequences enabled on the lanes.

=========== Conqueror-2-146.html — 32.4  Modifying Game Score before Printing ===========

32.4 Modifying Game Score before Printing

32.4 Modifying Game Score before Printing

Score modification is useful for example, when a player accidentally bowls on the other player's turn and the players then request to modify the score, or when the pinsetter makes a scoring error (when a pin falls a moment later).

Click on the game to be modified and press Modify Game
: the Modify Score
panel opens and the score of the two bowls of each frame can be modified/entered: for each frame it is possible whether to insert the score or modify the status of every single pin by clicking on the picture.

Note that in the Hdcp
field a negative handicap (e.g. -10) can be entered.

As soon as score modification is saved, recalculation of all points, scores, averages, etc. is carried out automatically.

LANE MANAGEMENT > Game Printouts > Modifying Game Score before Printing

=========== Conqueror-2-148.html — 32.6  Printing Scores and Standings ===========

32.6 Printing Scores and Standings

32.6 Printing Scores and Standings

Score Printouts

Press Score
to access the score printout setup with the default settings, which have been pre-set trough the Default Options
button:

Select whether the frames have to be grouped by player, by lane or if game total only is desired.
Select whether the players have to be ordered according to their Player Name
, their Scratch
or Total
(for example, to filter the best players) or their position on the lanes.

The following additional Print Options
can be selected, to display more information in supplementary rows or columns:

In the scroll down menu choose between Display Total Only
and Display Total and Scratch;

Scratch
displays both the scratch and handicap totals;
Pin Position Scheme
puts in a graphical scheme of the pin position, if desired;
Statistics
adds number of strikes, spares, converted splits, gutters, etc. for each bowler;
Team Total
includes a row of totals for each game;
Increasing Totals
displays the progressive totals: the total is added to the previous ones, increasing;
Prizes
prints a page of the prizes won through Special Games by players included in the printout;
Transactions
inserts the financial transaction total of the current event at the end of the printout;
Tickets
displays a resume of the number of redemption tickets win per each player, very useful at the end of the game to help players to divide the tickets provided by the dispenser.

Once the appropriate options have been selected, insert the number of Copies to be Printed
, then press Print
to print out the report immediately, or press Preview
to allow the operator to view the report before deciding whether to print it or not.

button.

Press Standing
to access the standing setup.

Choose between the individual Player
standing and the Team
standing. Choose if the standing has to be based on the Total
or on the Scratch Total
, and afterwards in case of a tie, if the Low/High Game Difference
has to be considered.

Choose the type of numbering to use in case of ties and select the Optional Details
. It is also possible to add to the standing one or more Optional Sections
based on the Scratch Total
, the Total
, or Both
.

The player standing contains the player position number in the standing, the player name and ID together with his handicap, number of games played, lane number and scratch total; the team standing contains the team position in the standing, the team name, handicap, number of games played, lane number and scratch total.

Once the appropriate options have been selected, insert the number of Copies to be Printed
, then by pressing Print
the standing is printed out immediately, while by pressing Preview
the operator can view the standing before deciding whether to print it or not.

It is also possible to send it to the lanes through a Scrolling Message by pressing Message
: the Lane Messages
window will open and the message can be previewed and modified.
