=========== Conqueror-2-151.html — EXPERIENCE ===========

EXPERIENCE 

EXPERIENCE

=========== Conqueror-2-152.html — 1.  Experience ===========

1. Experience

1. Experience

This new feature allows centers to define and manage different price keys for each on-lane experience and ensures operators use only price keys and options compatible with the selected experience. The solution introduces the new concept of "Experience", which defines the relation between the services provided, in terms of environments\games, and the price keys to use in that context. Centers can prepare the experiences setup in advance, and activate the "Experience mode" whenever they want. When a center works in Experience mode, every lane opening prompts the operator to select an experience, Conqueror filters the price keys and environments compatible with the selected Experience while BES X filters the environments that bowlers can select from YLYW. 

EXPERIENCE > Experience

=========== Conqueror-2-153.html — 1.1  Privileges ===========

1.1 Privileges

1.1 Privileges

SETUP > STAFF SETUP > USER PROFILE SETUP > OPERATE

Enable/disable the privileges to use the Experience module. 

EXPERIENCE > Experience > Privileges

=========== Conqueror-2-154.html — 1.2  Experience Setup ===========

1.2 Experience Setup

1.2 Experience Setup

First, you need to add an Experience to start using this new functionality. Select Experience Setup module to create, modify or delete an Experience. There are six buttons located at the bottom of the Experience Setup module. Click New
to add a new experience, Select an experience and click Delete
to delete it 

Select an experience and click Modify
to modify it. Use the Up and Down arrows keys to move an experience up or down. The Enable Experience
selection mode option allows you to enable\disable all the components of the feature. You could use this option to setup the Experiences in advance and enable them at a later stage. For each Experience, you can define: a name, a lane set in which the Experience can be used, colors to facilitate the selection of the Experience, an exhaustive description of what the Experience includes to help the operator. 

Setting up Game/Time/Unlimited Price Keys

Use Game/Time/Unlimited Price keys tabs to select the price keys you want to add to the Description list, which includes all the price keys that belong to the Experience you are just about to create. Flag the price keys and use the small arrows to add/remove them from the Description list. Set a default price key for each opening mode prior to using an Experience. Please note: FB price key cannot be set as default. You must set a proportional price key when it comes to Time Price keys. Select Save to confirm your changes or Cancel to return to the Experience Setup module. Once the experiences have been setup, they are ready to be used. 

Setting up Environments

Use the Environments tab to add an environment to the experience. Use the Game duration options if you want to filter the environments by number of frames (10 or 5). To save the options, you must first set a Default Lane Option and a Default Environment using the dropdown menu. The players have the chance to change environment from YLYW, choosing from the ones you have previously included in the Experience. Select Save to confirm your changes or Cancel to return to Experience Setup module. 

Selecting an Experience

When opening a lane, a window prompts the operator to select an Experience. The available experiences depend on the opening mode and the lane set. The Experience list dialog window pops up so that the operator can choose the Experience compatible with the opening mode and the lane set. If the default price key is not available at that time, the Experience will be greyed out and it will not be possible to select it. Click Skip
to open a lane without Experience or Cancel. The system works as usual if no Experience is available. Please note: only privileged users can open a lane without selecting an Experience.

Lane Control - Set Price

Open Lane Control, click Select Price
to visualize all the price keys compatible with the selected experience. The operator cannot: use price keys not compatible with the selected experience and change the list of available environments in YLYW. Click Experience
to select a different Experience. You cannot change experience if the lane is open. 

Lane Control - Lane Options

The operator can select a different environment from Lane Options. Only environments compatible with the selected experiences are available in Lane Options.

Merge/Transfer/Split

Merge: only available if both lanes are compatible with the selected experience.

Transfer (Lane/Bowler): only available if the destination lane is compatible with the selected experience. 

Split: it is always available.

However, in case of emergency it is possible to transfer an open lane to one not compatible with the Experience lane set. The available environments\games remain those set up in the Experience (and compatible with the lane features). In this situation, the operator is notified and if he confirms, the operation will be logged as suspicious action.

EXPERIENCE > Experience > Experience Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-155.html — SHIFT MANAGEMENT ===========

SHIFT MANAGEMENT

SHIFT MANAGEMENT

=========== Conqueror-2-156.html — 1.  Overview ===========

1. Overview

1. Overview

This section deals with the management of employees' work sessions.

The system is flexible and can be configured according to the needs of both small Centers managed by a family and large Centers with a lot of employees in various distinct sectors (e.g. bowling, restaurant, lockers, pro-shop, etc.).

For smaller Centers not requiring staff management, the No Shift mode is recommended. Activating the security system is recommended for Bowling Centers utilizing Shift management, while smaller Bowling Centers can limit their security to the Daily/Periodical Reports.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Overview

=========== Conqueror-2-157.html — 2.  Key Concepts ===========

2. Key Concepts

2. Key Concepts

Sector

The Bowling Center can be organized into different sectors, representing the various areas dealing with income.

It is possible to have a different Shift for each sector. For example, the bowling and restaurant sectors can have the same hours and the same Shift, while the pro-shop may have more restrictive hours and therefore an independent Shift.

For those wishing to manage Shifts according to sector, start by determining the Sector settings in Center Setup.

Shift

The Conqueror Shift
refers to the period of time worked within the sales area of the Bowling Center between the opening and closing of all cash drawers in any one sector (Lanes, Bar, Shop, etc.). All transactions performed by cashiers during their work sessions are summarized in the Shift Report.

Cash Drawer Sessions

Cash Drawer Session
refers to the work session of one or more cashiers working at the same cash drawer.

There can be more than one cash drawer in each sector, which can be shared by more than one terminal.

For example, in the restaurant sector the bar cash drawer is accessed by two terminals, while the cash drawer in the pizza area of the restaurant is accessed through another terminal.

Reports

At the end of the work session, each cash drawer generates a report describing the transactions performed at that cash drawer. When all of the cash drawers in a sector have printed their reports, a Shift report is printed to consolidate the data pertaining to all of the cash drawers. The cash drawer and Shift reports are delivered to the manager, who verifies that all of the session details are correct and have not been tampered with.

For those not working with Shifts, the Daily Report provides all information on cash management in report form, on a daily basis.

Shift Progression

A Shift is opened automatically when an operator opens their cash drawer session at the beginning of their work session. All cash drawer sessions must be closed or parked before the Shift to which they belong can be closed and terminals cannot connect to a Shift that is being closed.

A shift can be parked so that the next shift can start without delays due to counting floats for the last shift.

A Shift is in the Printable
status when all cash drawer sessions have been closed and the Shift report is ready to be printed, whereas Printed
is the final Shift status evident when the Shift has been closed and printed.

Magic Number

This refers to the control number used to verify the authenticity of the data contained in the report.

It is generated by combining and scrambling key data pertaining to the report (Print Date
/Time
, Amount Tendered
, Under/Over
, Host Number
).

To verify a report using the magic number it is necessary to be assigned a managerial profile. Enter the Shift Management module, insert the above data and make sure that the number generated corresponds.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Key Concepts

=========== Conqueror-2-158.html — 2.1  No Shift Mode ===========

2.1 No Shift Mode

2.1 No Shift Mode

Smaller Bowling Centers would preferably work in No Shift
mode as they don't have many employees and therefore require minimum security (that is, they don't work with profiles or privileges).

In this case, management of Shifts and cash drawer sessions are unnecessary and the content of the cash drawer is counted by the manager at the end of the day which is recorded through printing a Daily Report.

This report contains all transactions starting from the last Print & Reset.

Daily Report

Front Desk > Daily Report

This module is displayed instead of the Shift
module and from here it is possible to Preview
and Print
reports of transactions performed.

Print and Reset

Depending on the status of the Print Function in Center Setup, this button changes.

Press Print and Reset
in order to clear all data ready for the next day's report data.

A Shift is in the Printable
status when all cash drawer sessions have been closed and the Shift report is ready to be printed, whereas Printed
is the final Shift status evident when the Shift has been closed and printed.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Key Concepts > No Shift Mode

=========== Conqueror-2-159.html — 3.  Operating with Shifts ===========

3. Operating with Shifts

3. Operating with Shifts

Front Desk > Shifts

This window can also be accessed by clicking on the green button under the clock in the main menu.

It contains the following:

The statuses of Shifts and cash drawer sessions
Report printing options
The option to insert further information (cashiers working on the cash drawer session or Shift, the weather or other income determining factors, etc.)

The first scroll menu displays the Sector
to which the terminal belongs.

The second scroll menu displays the Shift
to which the terminal is connected, with its opening date, number and status.

Selecting a Cash drawer
on the left, displays Information
on the right regarding the Shift or cash drawer sessions: Status
, Started at
(time and date), By
and the Connected Users
.

The Open, Close and Park options are described in detail in subsequent paragraphs.

Info

Press Info
to open the Cash Drawer Session Operators
window, where information can be entered at any stage, although it is recommended that it be done during Shift closure so all information is compiled automatically and only operators that worked through other terminals need be entered.

On the right is a list of all users that participated in the Cash Drawer Session
, while the list on the left contains all other employees. Use the arrows to add operators to the Cash Drawer Session
or to remove them (Added Operators
).

The operator can also enter the Weather
present during the Shift and also insert a Note
describing specific conditions present during the Shift.

This window automatically opens upon Shift closure when the Note, Weather and Staff option is selected in Center Setup.

Counter Report

to print a report regarding the cash drawers.

This opens the End of Shift Reporting
window, which can also be accessed through Back Office.

A Shift is in the Printable
status when all cash drawer sessions have been closed and the Shift report is ready to be printed, whereas Printed
is the final Shift status evident when the Shift has been closed and printed.

Report

Here it is possible to send the selected Shift Reports to the printer.

To activate this option, there must be more than one cash drawer pertaining to the Shift.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Operating with Shifts

=========== Conqueror-2-160.html — 3.1  Starting Cash Drawer Sessions ===========

3.1 Starting Cash Drawer Sessions

3.1 Starting Cash Drawer Sessions

The operator commences their working day by opening a cash drawer session (and therefore a Shift) pertaining to their sector from their own terminal by performing either of the following;

Pressing Open
in the Shift
window in the front desk module.
Responding Yes
to the question "Do you wish to start a new Shift:...?" upon performing actions requiring payment.

Having performed one of the two operations above, the operator beginning a cash drawer session, verifies that the float in the cash drawer corresponds to the amount displayed by the system (set by default in Center Setup when creating a cash drawer in the Sectors and Cash Drawers tab).

If the operator confirms the float amount, the cash drawer session can be opened (as can the Shift).

If the float is found to be of a greater or lesser amount than that proposed by the system, the operator should report the discrepancy to the person responsible and the cash drawer session should not be started until the situation has been resolved.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Operating with Shifts > Starting Cash Drawer Sessions

=========== Conqueror-2-161.html — 3.2  Closing Cash Drawer Sessions ===========

3.2 Closing Cash Drawer Sessions

3.2 Closing Cash Drawer Sessions

To close a cash drawer session, press Close
in the Shift
module and print the cash drawer session report.

A cash drawer session can be parked by a cashier (with the appropriate privileges), permitting the operator to count the moneys received on another terminal that does not necessarily pertain to that cash drawer or sector.

When closing a cash drawer session, all other terminals connected to that Shift receive a message inviting them to log off in order to close the Shift and other users are not permitted to connect.

When all terminals have disconnected from the cash drawer session, the moneys received can be counted if set accordingly.

Once the moneys received have been counted, the End of Shift Reporting
window opens and the Shift report can be printed and/or exported (automatically or manually) through QuCad and QuickBooks Desktop.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Operating with Shifts > Closing Cash Drawer Sessions

=========== Conqueror-2-162.html — 3.3  Parking Cash Drawer Sessions ===========

3.3 Parking Cash Drawer Sessions

3.3 Parking Cash Drawer Sessions

Cash drawer sessions can be parked when Shift closure has been ordered and all operators working within the cash drawer session have logged off except one, who opts to suspend closure.

The cash drawer session is then parked so that the next Shift can be opened, finalizing closure details later.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Operating with Shifts > Parking Cash Drawer Sessions

=========== Conqueror-2-163.html — 3.4  Moneys Received Summary ===========

3.4 Moneys Received Summary

3.4 Moneys Received Summary

In the case the Count Float on Closure option has been enabled, the Moneys Received Summary
window appears when closing a Shift/cash drawer session. This window functions similarly to the Sales window, whereby the operator selects the denomination and then specifies the amount of that particular denomination that is in the cash drawer.

If the Display Total in Money Summary
option has been enabled, totals related to Special Payments are automatically calculated and displayed.

The Total
is updated as the money is counted into the system. If typing errors are made, press Reset
to empty the table. Press OK
when all moneys have been accounted for in the system.

At this point a window reveals the Starting Float
, the Net Cash Counted
, the System Calculated Total
and the money Over/Short
of the calculated total.

To go back to the Shift
window in order to correct any counting errors or the like, press No
or press Yes
to continue, where confirmation of the amount deposited for the next cash drawer session is required in the case the Float Confirmation option has been set. Press OK
to confirm the proposed amount or insert a new amount.

If the Notes, Weather and Staff
option is set, it is also necessary to confirm the involved operators. The operator can also enter the Weather
present during the Shift and also insert a Note
describing specific conditions present during the Shift.

Once the operation is complete, press OK
to proceed to the End of Shift Reporting window.

The cashier inserts all data before seeing the report, thus avoiding the manipulation of income. If, however, the cashier requests to Preview
the report without printing it, this is registered as a Suspect Action in the System Log.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Operating with Shifts > Moneys Received Summary

=========== Conqueror-2-164.html — 3.5  Closing a Shift ===========

3.5 Closing a Shift

3.5 Closing a Shift

To finish a cash drawer session it is necessary to print the report pertaining to the current Shift.

This is possible after all cash drawer sessions in the Shift have been closed.

To close the Shift, the operator can perform one of the following operations from the terminal:

Press Close
in the Shift
window in the front desk module.
Close the cash drawer session and respond Yes
to the question "Do you wish to close the entire Shift:...?" that appears automatically.

In both cases, the other cash drawers pertaining to that Shift, receive instruction to close or park the current transaction and the cash drawer session.

From the moment Shift/cash drawer session closure is requested, until all cash drawer sessions have been physically closed or parked, the Shift remains in Closing
status.

While waiting for all cash drawer sessions to be closed or parked, the operator that requested Shift closure can add any useful statistical information regarding the Shift, in the Info
section prior to receiving a message confirming that the next Shift can be opened.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Operating with Shifts > Closing a Shift

=========== Conqueror-2-165.html — 4.  Personal Cash Drawer ===========

4. Personal Cash Drawer

4. Personal Cash Drawer

With the Personal Cash Drawer management:

� The income of each operator must belong to a specific and personal cash drawer in order to be able to associate a specific cashier to a shortfall.

� Each cashier can collect money from different terminals by recording all movements in the same shift and in the same cash drawer (hardware or money pouch).

� Two cashiers can collect money from the same terminal, but in a specific, different hardware cash drawer.

� The access to the Personal Cash Drawer can be restricted to the connected cashier, but can also be accessed by others if they have the privilege to do so.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Personal Cash Drawer

=========== Conqueror-2-166.html — 4.1  How does it Work? ===========

4.1 How does it Work?

4.1 How does it Work?

The cashier can be set to be able to use a Money Pouch and/or a hardware cash drawer.

Each cashier can have the Work with a Money Pouch privilege and/or Work with a Cash Tray privilege.

The cashier can have both privileges or only one. When the cashier with both privileges logs in the system, they will have to specify with which cash drawer they are going to work:

This window is shown only in the case of a real choice:

if both the hardware cash drawer are already busy, the cashier will be automatically connected to a Money Pouch.
if the cashier can work only on a hardware cash drawer and one is already busy, they will be automatically connected to the available cash drawer

In order to view, park, close and print a shift pertaining to another operator, the staff member has to have the Manage cash drawer sessions and shifts opened by others privilege enabled. They will see the Remove Drawer and the Join Shift buttons enabled:

Join Shift allows the operator with a cash drawer connected to that terminal working on the currently selected shift. When joining a shift pertaining to another operator, the new operator name in the upper bar is highlighted in order to point up this "irregular" situation.
The Remove Drawer button allows disconnecting the hardware cash drawer from the terminal, and the shift remains open. 

The cashier with a hardware cash drawer working on a shift on different terminals has to have the Work on shifts through different terminals privilege. In this case, they can move from a terminal to another with their cash tray using the Remove Drawer and Join Shift functions.

Logging off from terminal

To be completely logged off from the terminal, the operator with a closed shift has to press Remove Drawer to disconnect their hardware cash drawer, allowing the next operator to insert their cash drawer.

Managing Shifts

With the Personal Cash Drawer management this window displays all the shifts in a single table.

Note that the Sector column remains in order to have the old shifts correctly displayed.

The total displayed for Closed Shifts corresponds to the Shift Total printed on reports. If the shift is open, the total is calculated and displayed in real time.

Select a Personal Cash Drawer to generate reports related to that operator shift.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Personal Cash Drawer > How does it Work?

=========== Conqueror-2-167.html — 4.2  Conqueror Pro Settings ===========

4.2 Conqueror Pro Settings

4.2 Conqueror Pro Settings

Configuring the Personal Cash Drawer Management

Setup > Center Setup > Shifts

Enable the Personal Cash Drawer option in order to work with this new drawer management: the system checks if there are Shifts opened (not already printed), and then restarts.

When restarted with the Personal Cash Drawer management active, the Sectors & Cash Drawers tab is hidden and it is replaced by the new Cash Drawers tab.

Setting Personal Cash Drawers Values

Setup > Center Setup > Cash Drawers

In the Cash Drawers tab, a Personal Cash Drawer called with the name of the cashier is automatically created, with a default Bank.

Click on Modify to change the cash drawer name and the bank amount.

The Under/over Limit can be managed if the Stricter Under/over Management option has been enabled in the Shifts tab.

Please note that when a new staff member is added in the system, a new Personal Cash Drawer is automatically created with their name.

Connecting Hardware Cash Drawer to the Terminal

After having set the Personal Cash Drawer management in the Center Setup, it is necessary to configure the hardware cash drawers.

On each terminal it is possible to set 2 cash drawers under Setup > Terminal Setup > Devices.

Configuring the Printer Profiles

If the Printer Profile for All Counters was enabled in the previous management, it remains enabled and the Printer Profile is active for all cash drawers. 

If different profiles were associated to the cash drawers (Printer Profile for All Counters option deselected), it is necessary to associate a Printer Profile to the new cash drawers (1 for each operator), otherwise the order will not be printed in the kitchen.

Assigning User Privileges to Cashiers

Visualize all current shifts of the bowling center

Only cashiers with this privilege can view shifts pertaining to other operators.

Manage cash drawer sessions and shifts opened by others

Only cashiers with this privilege can view, park, close and print shifts pertaining to another operators.

Work on shifts through different terminals

This privilege allows operator with a hardware cash drawer to work on a shift from different terminals.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Personal Cash Drawer > Conqueror Pro Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-168.html — 5.  Shift Reporting ===========

5. Shift Reporting

5. Shift Reporting

Back Office > Shift Management

Both the Report
buttons open the End of Shift Reporting
window in which the level of detail contained in the report and any additional information can be chosen. The upper Report
button is related to the Shift while the lower Report
button is related to the currently selected Cash Drawer Session.

This window can also be accessed through the front desk menu by pressing Counter Report
.

Detail Level

Choose the level of detail to be included, which will then also be proposed the next time a report is printed.

Main Departments
- Displays the totals of moneys received for the main departments, including data such as the department name and net value.
Sub-departments
- Displays sub-department data, with the main department name and sub-department. Both this option and the previous option are necessary to provide a high level of data pertaining to the money received in the Center.
Price Key
- Reveals data for each price key utilized divided into the departments and sub-departments to which the price key belongs (transactions for items sold and refunded), including the quantity and total net amount.
Price Key by Value
- As above, yet here each price key value is displayed per row, for example, dynamic price keys are divided into each of their various values according to time zones utilized.
Receipt Rows by Departments
- Here transactions are divided according to the item department on each row. Further to the data mentioned above, the receipt number, time of payment, operator name, cash drawer name and member details (if customer is a Center member) are displayed.
Transaction List
- Here the level of detail is on par with that of the Sub-departments report. This report displays two sections, the first pertaining to the bowling session and moneys received on the lanes divided per lane or player, whereas the second section displays all financial details.
End of Shift Cash out
- In this case, only the Money Received Summary
details are displayed for a double check on the cash drawer. Only on closed Shifts.

To be Printed on Receipt Printer

Shift report at main departmental level can be printed on the receipt printer.

This feature, which can be used also when resetting a shift, allows operators to print the Shift report on terminals directly connected only to a receipt printer, such as POS terminals.

The target printer is the one configured as the Receipt Printer in Terminal Setup.

Group Type

The Group by Time Zone
option is enabled if Time Zones for Shifts have been defined in Center Setup (through the Shift Zone
button). This option groups the report data according to sections, each with its own heading Time Zone name: start time - end time
. This option only applies to Main Departments
, Sub-departments
, Price Keys
and Price Keys by Value
reports.

The Group by Tax Exemption
option groups the report data according to the configured Tax Exemption
groups.

Price Information

Choose to display additional sections revealing the following details:

Voided Items

Special Prices
Voided Price Keys

Refunded Price Keys

Tax Exemption

Optional Information

In this section the following optional information can be added to the report:

Tips
- adds a detailed section about tips collected and tips given to operators.

Payment Types
- lists the method of payment used by the customer (i.e. credit card, cash, check, etc.) and the amount spent using each method of payment.

Summary of Operator Totals
- displays the revenues divided by operator, with the final total.

Played Games
- reveals the number of games based on the total number of frames played during the Shift, divided into the opening mode applied (Open, League, Tournament...).

People bowling Statistics
- in this optional section it is possible to display the total number of games sold, the total number of players who bought the games and the total cashed for these games.

Taxes Included
- displays the various taxes included in the transaction in a separate row, before the grand total.

Suspect Actions
- suspect actions such as previewing a Shift report without printing it or changing the system time and date or performing a `No Sale', are summarized here with the number of occurrences.

Uncommon Bowls
- a number is displayed for bowls performed out of the ordinary modes, i.e. bowls on closed lanes, practice bowls, etc.

Points Handled
- displays points added and subtracted manually from members by the operators.

Player Club Cash Payments
- displays a section containing information on payments done using the players' points.

List of Operators
- reveals staff members that participated in the Shift.

Bills and Coins
- specifies the denominations utilized when counting the float.

Notes and Weather
- includes the notes inserted when closing the Shift.

Export

Using this option exports the report information in .csv format that can be read by various applications including Excel.

E-mail

Press E-mail
to send the report to the e-mail address specified in Center Setup.

The option is deactivated if the address hasn't been specified in Center Setup.

Print

Prints the report on the set printer.

Preview

Press Preview
to view the report before sending or printing it.

QuCad/Zonal

Utilize this option to export the report using QuCad or Zonal applications.

This option is only present if the relevant option has been set in Terminal Setup.

QuickBooks Desktop

Exports report data to the external QuickBooks Desktop managerial program.

This button is only visible if enabled in Center Setup.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Shift Reporting

=========== Conqueror-2-169.html — 6.  Managing Shifts ===========

6. Managing Shifts

6. Managing Shifts

Back Office > Shift Management

This window contains two tables, the upper table regarding Shifts and the lower, cash drawer sessions.

The cash drawer session report is a descriptive report for each cash drawer, printed after each cash drawer session, while the Shift report is a cumulative report for all cash drawers pertaining to the Shift.

In this module it is possible to perform the following:

Print Shift/cash drawer sessions details (even Open
Shifts).
Reprint or cancel Shifts/cash drawer sessions labeled Printed
.

If the printout is satisfactory, the operator confirms, otherwise a reprint can be requested which is then registered in the System Log.

The report contains:

The names of the operators participating in the Shift
A detailed summary of transactions
The time and date of the start and end of the Shift
The expected total
The total declared by the cashier that closed the Shift
Any difference between the above two totals
The Magic Number
and the summary of any unusual activity detected by the system. This section is present if previously set in the Center Setup
The closure data

Report

The Shift and Cash Drawer Session Report
buttons open the End of Shift Reporting window where the level of detail can be selected for reports pertaining to the Shifts or cash drawer sessions, which can then be printed, previewed, exported or e-mailed through this window.

The status of Shifts and cash drawer sessions is not altered when utilizing the Report
buttons in this module.

Delete Shift

To delete a Shift manually, the Shift must be in the Printed
status, that is, completely concluded and therefore transferred to the history database, according to the settings in Center Setup.

After a Shift has been deleted, it can only be consulted through the history database (if transferred).

Transfer History

In the case of an emergency in which the automatic export at shift closure failed, here it is possible to force the transfer to the historical database. Only if the Transfer Data to Historical Database option has been set.

Ordered by

Here the table can be ordered according to Date
, Sector
, Cash Drawer
or Shift
.

Magic Number

This button opens a window where the following Shift data to be verified can be entered: Print Date
, Print Time
, Amount Tendered
, Over/Short
, Host Number
.

At the base of the report, the Center ID
can be found so that the authenticity of the report can be verified even if the report pertains to another Bowling Center.

Assign a Magic Number to the Shift by pressing Compute
.

This number is used to verify that the report has not been falsified.

Filters

This option allows the operator to select from the following available filters in order to modify the table display: All
, Since
, Until
, Between
& Select Sector
/ Shift
/ Cash drawer
.

Having inserted the relevant criteria, press Filter
to return to the table adjusted accordingly or press Cancel
to cancel the operation.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Managing Shifts

7. Historical Reports

and a Shift

=========== Conqueror-2-171.html — 8.  Profiles and Privileges ===========

8. Profiles and Privileges

8. Profiles and Privileges

For complete Shift management, different access levels can be set, depending on levels of responsibility within the Center.

To do so, profiles are created and attributed to staff members in the User Profile Setup and Staff Setup modules.

Start a New Shift
: The Open
button is activated for users with this privilege.

Close One's Own Sector Shift
: Enables users with this privilege to close Shifts.

Open a Cash Drawer Session and Confirm Float
: Allows the operator to open a cash drawer session and confirm the amount in the float, which does not automatically give the user the privilege to open and close Shifts.

Count Money and Close One's Own Cash Drawer Session
: Allows the user to count the money earned during their work session and then close their own cash drawer session.

Print the Cash Drawer Report
, Send the Cash Drawer Report by E-mail
, Export the Cash Drawer Report
: Gives the operator the privilege to either print, e-mail or export the cash drawer report so that the management has all copies documented in the same place at the end of the Shift.

Print the Shift Report
, Send the Shift Report by E-mail, Export the Shift Report
: Gives the operator the privilege to either print, e-mail or export the Shift report so that the management has all copies documented in the same place at the end of the Shift.

View and Print the Historical Reports
: Allows the operator to check all data that has been moved to the historical database.

Operate in Every Sector of the Bowling Center
: The user with this privilege can operate in every sector, opening and closing Shifts.

Login Cash Drawer Session Opened by Another Operator
: Give this user privilege to operators who can access all the cash drawers, including the ones pertaining to another operator. An operator without the privilege can access only their cash drawer.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Profiles and Privileges

=========== Conqueror-2-172.html — 8.1  Examples ===========

8.1 Examples

8.1 Examples

The following is an example of how these different access levels could be applied.

The Cashier
utilizes the cash drawer and therefore requires the privileges to open and close cash drawer sessions, confirming the float and counting the moneys received.

The Cashier Manager
is responsible for the cash drawer and any possible discrepancies and therefore performs the following duties, further to the operations mentioned above: opens and closes Shifts and cash drawer sessions within the sector and prints the cash drawer session reports.

The Sector Manager
manages a sector within the Bowling Center (bowling, pro shop, etc.) and in addition to the responsibilities mentioned above, performs the following: closes Shifts, prints cash drawer session reports pertaining to the sector and prints Shift reports.

The Manager
organizes Shifts and sets the options and further to the above, the manager requires the relevant privileges to perform the following: operate on cash drawer sessions and Shifts pertaining to any sector within the Center, reprint reports pertaining to previous Shifts or cash drawer sessions, set configuration options including whether or not to print all cash drawer sessions in order to generate the Shift report.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Profiles and Privileges > Examples

=========== Conqueror-2-173.html — 9.  Shift Setup ===========

9. Shift Setup

9. Shift Setup

Center Setup > Shifts Tab

Shifts

Select whether or not Shift management is desired.

If not selected, the system works in No Shift
mode with Daily Reports.

Personal Cash Drawer

With the new Personal Cash Drawer management:

� The income of each operator must belong to a specific and personal cash drawer in order to be able to associate a specific cashier to a shortfall.

� Each cashier can collect money from different terminals by recording all movements in the same shift and in the same cash drawer (hardware or money pouch).

� Two cashiers can collect money from the same terminal, but in a specific, different hardware cash drawer.

� The access to the Personal Cash Drawer can be restricted to the connected cashier, but can also be accessed by others if they have the privilege to do so.

Enable the Personal Cash Drawer option in order to work with this new drawer management: the system checks if there are Shifts opened (not already printed), and then restarts.

When restarted with the Personal Cash Drawer management active, the Sectors & Cash Drawers tab is hidden and it is replaced by the new Cash Drawers tab.

Count Float on Closure

Select this option if the system is to propose counting the float upon Shift closure.

Bill and Coin Details

Select this option if the Moneys Received Summary window is to be proposed automatically when closing Shifts so that bills and coins from the float can be counted into the system.

Stricter Under/over Management

It is possible to set an under/over limit for each cash drawer. At shift closure, if the under/over is beyond the limit, the operator has to enter a note. These justificatory notes are then reported in the Shift reports.

Enable this option then go to Setup > Center Setup > Sectors and Cash Drawers
in order to set the single cash drawer limits.

Notes, Weather and Staff

With this option, the system automatically proposes the Notes, Weather and Staff
window upon Shift closure.

Float Confirmation

Select this option to confirm the float amount upon Shift closure.

Shift Report Signing Section

Select to include the Confirmed by Supervisor
section, where the cashier and manager can sign the report.

Automatic Shift Closure

For Centers that use the system of automatic exporting for the moneys received each day, this option checks if any cash drawer sessions or Shifts remained open at the day change time, and automatically closes them.

In Centers located in France, this option is activated by default in order to prevent any fraudulent intervention.

Print Function

Choose the archive operation desired for the Shift from: Print
, E-mail
, Export
, Nothing
.

If e-mail is chosen, specify the address on the right, to which the report will be sent.

Automatic Export

Select the QuickBooks Desktop
check underneath to add the QuickBooks Desktop
and Both
options to the Automatic Export
scroll menu.

Tax Exemption Suffix

Configure the tax-exempt suffix for Dassle export.

That suffix will be appended to price key information during export when the price key is sold with tax-exemption.

QuickBooks Desktop

Select whether the QuickBooks Desktop management software is to be used.

With this option selected, the Export Detail Level
scroll menu can be accessed, in which the default level of detail can be selected for data exportation.

Press Setup
to access the QuickBooks Desktop account settings.

Reset Shift Report Number

Choose whether the number of the Shift report gets reset Every Day
or Never
.

Backtask Sector

It is advisable to create a "virtual" Sector that will be utilized by Conqueror's Backtask (especially when working with the Booking System), where transactions such as automatic cancellations of bookings with deposits paid, are registered.

Send Report

Specify the e-mail addresses to which the reports have to be sent in the case the archive operation desired is set on e-mail.

This is useful when the manager has more than one Bowling Center to run, or is away from the premises and wishes to check on things.

Shift Zone

Press this option to access the Define Shift Report Time Zones
window.

Here the bowling day can be divided into various Time Zones (e.g. morning, evening and night) giving them a name, so that in the data in the reports is grouped into these sections.

The Group by Time Zone
option will be activated for the End of Shift
Report.

=========== Conqueror-2-174.html — 9.1  Sector Setup ===========

9.1 Sector Setup

9.1 Sector Setup

Setup > Center Setup > Sectors and Cash Drawers

In this window sectors and their cash drawers can be created.

On the left side of the screen, create a list of all the different sectors the Bowling Center is divided into (bowling, bar and so on), while on the right create the associated cash drawers with the corresponding float.

It is possible to create, Modify
or Cancel
sectors and cash drawers separately.

After following the steps to create sectors and cash drawers, set the right sector and cash drawer for each terminal through Terminal Setup > Preferences.

Exclusive Responsibility of Cash Drawers

A user privilege allows to define which operators can login on terminals already in use, joining the cash turn session of another operator; operators without this privilege can access only terminals not already associated to another operator, and cannot share the responsibility of their cash drawer with a colleague.

Enable the Exclusive Cash Drawer
option to give the exclusive responsibility to use a particular cash drawer to the first operator who logs in and opens the cash turn session. The protected cash drawer will be accessed only by the operator who has opened the cash turn session and by any operator with the user privilege.
Give the Login Cash Drawer Session Opened by Another Operator
user privilege to operators who can access all the cash drawers, including the ones pertaining to another operator. An operator without the privilege can access only their cash drawer.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Shift Setup > Sector Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-175.html — 9.2  QuickBooks Desktop Account Settings ===========

9.2 QuickBooks Desktop Account Settings

9.2 QuickBooks Desktop Account Settings

The account export function is used to join the income of different price keys or departments all into a single account.

A QuickBooks Desktop account is composed of an alphanumerical identification code, a description and a type (e.g. NAB 234 Non-Alcoholic Beverages).

The QuickBooks Desktop import function works if the file exported from Conqueror Pro contains accounts that are not present in QuickBooks Desktop or that are defined in the same way in both Conqueror Pro and QuickBooks Desktop.

To make this operation easier it is possible to synchronize the accounts defined in Conqueror Pro with the ones in QuickBooks Desktop by following this recommended procedure:

Under Center Setup > Shifts press Setup
to open a dialog window with tabs.
Set the QuickBooks Desktop Account Code
(alphanumerical identification code) for each price key, department, tax, modifier, payment mode, etc. and then Save
.
All the defined accounts with a default description and a type (both can be modified) will be displayed in the last tab.
As all the accounts have to match with the ones in QuickBooks Desktop, it is advisable to proceed with the automatic synchronization rather that the manual one.
Access QuickBooks Desktop and export the list of accounts through the following path: File > Utilities > Export > List to .IIF File. In the small window that opens select Charts of Accounts,
press OK
and then select a directory to save the .IIF file (that contains the accounts to synchronize with Conqueror Pro).
In the QuickBooks Desktop Account Setup
window of Conqueror Pro, press Synchronize
, select the file exported from QuickBooks Desktop and confirm.

At this point Conqueror pro will check if all the accounts in the Account List
tab are defined in the same way in both Conqueror Pro and QuickBooks Desktop.

If any differences are found, they will be highlighted in a specific window that contains a button for "correcting" the accounts in Conqueror Pro. If a correction is carried out, it is necessary to save before exiting the window in order to make the changes effective.

The synchronization function is not used to import accounts from QuickBooks Desktop (probably in QuickBooks Desktop there are many accounts that are not necessary to Conqueror) but it is used for matching accounts defined in Conqueror Pro with the definition present in QuickBooks Desktop.

Select the Account Export
function in the setup window. The export of the file to QuickBooks Desktop is automatically carried out (if enabled) upon shift closure or on demand from the Shift Reports.

Exporting Payment Modes

When closing a shift, together with the other data, the Payment Methods
can be exported to QuickBooks Desktop, differentiating every credit card type.

Choose a Payment Mode Export
option, from: Never
, Always
(payments are exported, but without differentiating the credit card type used to pay), Always with Credit Card Details
(the different credit cards used are exported, separated by the Card Scheme
code returned by the credit card providers; in this case it is necessary to configure the Credit Cards
tab).

In order to `export by account', enable the Export by Account Numbers
option and set the QuickBooks Desktop Account Code
for each credit card type.

SHIFT MANAGEMENT > Shift Setup > QuickBooks Desktop Account Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-176.html — FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING ===========

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING

=========== Conqueror-2-177.html — 1.  Overview ===========

1. Overview

1. Overview

The Conqueror FBT
module allows the Center a custom built member database, increasing the Center's profitability by allowing a broader level of communication with customers.

The FBT
module is a fully integrated system which tracks and reports every transaction made by members, allowing the Center to offer their customers more in the way of special offers (as they are able to identify the member's habits in the Center), classifications (bowling scores, etc.) and special management of companies through group membership.

Efficient and cost effective marketing and advertising is possible through the Mail Merge module and through the Point Collection scheme.

Members can be assigned exclusive price keys and discounts according to their category and they can also hire lockers.

By swiping the magnetic membership card, the member is immediately identified by the system, providing both member and operator with many advantages, such as:

When opening a lane for a member, their name is automatically entered in the Lane Control window.
Member default bowling rates (price keys) and discounts can be pre-set, saving time when performing transactions.
Members can purchase games, or charge money (QCash) to their accounts.
Members can have more than one membership card, with the possibility to set other cards (such as credit cards) to function as membership cards as well.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Overview

=========== Conqueror-2-178.html — 2.  Managing Members ===========

2. Managing Members

2. Managing Members

Front Desk > Frequent Bowlers

The Frequent Bowlers
window is divided into the Search and History tabs where it is possible to perform the following operations:

New Member/New Group

Add a new member or group to the database and perform the various operations necessary in order to register a new member, such as taking the member's photograph, assigning membership cards and links, etc.

Modify

Change/update selected member's details (or group details) by using this option to open the Modify
module.

Info

The Info
button is used to display the selected member's personal data such as the number of games and credit on the membership card, the card expiry date, the default price keys assigned to the member and the contact linked to him.

This window also contains the member's photo so that their identity can be verified if necessary.

Clear

Clear the tab of member profiles using the Clear
button.

Search

Enter the relevant criteria in the appropriate fields and press Search
to find a specific member or simply press Search
to reveal the complete list of members.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Managing Members

=========== Conqueror-2-179.html — 3.  Finding a Member ===========

3. Finding a Member

3. Finding a Member

Front Desk > Frequent Bowlers

The Search
tab allows the operator to find one or more members based on the information entered in the fields present.

Insert the relevant information and press Search
to begin the search.

It is possible to enter part of a name, e.g. Mel instead of Melissa when searching for a member, or to view all members press Search
or enter the underscore _ in the name field and then press Search
.

The search is a cumulative process, whereby all members found possess data in line with the search parameters entered.

Once the appropriate member has been found, it is possible to change and save the data by pressing Modify
, view the member's photograph and details by pressing Info, or Clear
the fields to perform another search.

Select a member in the table, to display their reservation history and the operators responsible for the actions present, in the History
tab.

Filtering FB without Mandatory Fields Filled

It is possible to search in the member archive which FB have some mandatory fields empty, in order to contact them and to complete their data: enter the data in the Member Search
Window, then press on Missing Fields
.

Member Search through Conqueror Modules

The member database can be accessed through many areas of Conqueror such as the lanes, POS, Staff Setup and Lockers, in order to select a member for a lane opening, assign them a transaction, attribute a locker, etc.

In each of the aforementioned modules, the Member Search
window can be opened by pressing the FBT Search
button.

For example, when wishing to sell certain items to a member through the Sales window, simply press the FBT Search
button, enter the search criteria and press Search
, whereby the Members Found
table in the lower part of the screen, displays all members found, according to the search criteria entered. Click on the desired member and press Select
and all items selected will then be attributed to that specific member.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Finding a Member

=========== Conqueror-2-180.html — 4.  Registering a New Member ===========

4. Registering a New Member

4. Registering a New Member

Front Desk > Frequent Bowlers

To define a new member, press New Member
/ New Group
in the Member Search
window and add the desired level of detail regarding the new member, create a magnetic membership card (and credit it), take the member's photo, etc., in the tabs described below.

Log

Press Log
to enter a log regarding the selected member, such as a reminder to e-mail the member, requested details with the date of the request. This log is recorded and can be viewed in the History tab.

Print

The Print
button opens the Filters and Reports window complete with the selected member's details, where reports can be printed, mail merges can be created for correspondence purposes, member data can be exported or another search can be performed.

Delete

Press Delete
to eliminate the currently displayed member from the database.

This elimination is only possible if there are no financial transactions open and no bookings regarding this member. If this is not the case, it is necessary to finalize the financial transactions and remove any bookings.

If there are lockers assigned to the member (or if the member is on the waiting list for a locker), they are automatically vacated as for tournaments whereby the member is erased from the tournament.

When cancelling a staff member here, they are automatically cancelled from the staff member list.

The Delete
option is only present when modifying details.

Save

Press Save
to confirm and save the data entered.

Cancel

Press Cancel
to exit the New Member
window.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Registering a New Member

=========== Conqueror-2-181.html — 4.1  Basic ===========

4.1 Basic

4.1 Basic

Select Automatic Case Adjustment
on the bottom left hand side of the screen to adjust the first letter of the name entered to upper-case. For example, "meadow" would be automatically adjusted to "Meadow".

Yellow fields require obligatory information.

Member Details

Enter the member's names (last/first name, nickname), address, e-mail address, telephone and fax numbers in the Basic
tab.

The telephone number types to be inserted for the member can be selected in the scroll down menus between: main, secondary, mobile, work, etc.

Also enter the member's personal details, such as Birth Date
, Marital Status
and the Marriage Date
(if applicable). This data could be useful when organizing events in the Center.

Specify whether he plays in tournaments or leagues and if the member hires shoes every time he plays.

Enable the Web User
option for members who want to login to the Center Booking Website. (To login they have to use their e-mail address and the Default Password set in Web Setup
.)

Then enter the member's Gender
, Age Bracket
(adult, junior or senior), if left or right handed, Shoe Size
and whether he smokes or not (this setting is useful for Centers with zones for smokers on the lanes, for Time Games, in the restaurant, etc. as the bookings for the member can be organized accordingly).

Note that Gender
and Age Bracket
data are imported in the League module for Bowler Category management.

Group Details

For group creation, enter the Group Name
, Location Name
(for example the name of the shopping center where the company is at), Group Type
(for example: church group, sport association, retired), Profession/Workplace
(the company's field of activity) and Number of members
.

The Group Types and Workplaces available in this scroll down menu have been previously set in Center Setup.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Registering a New Member > Basic

=========== Conqueror-2-182.html — 4.2  Advanced ===========

4.2 Advanced

4.2 Advanced

No Longer a Member

Check this option if a member wishes to cancel their membership, but the Center still requires the member details.

The ex-member's details remain in the database so that if they later wish to re-activate their membership, it can be performed easily.

Privacy

In the Privacy
section, specify if the Frequent Bowler allows the center to contact them by post/mail, e-mail, text message and/or by telephone.

When the first option is enabled, the other ones are set automatically, but the operator can still change them one by one.

Data Available for Others

Selecting this option reveals that the information regarding this member can be shared with external parties for purposes such as publicity, according to the laws of privacy.

Can Be Contacted Directly

The operator has the ability to create a new Frequent Bowler. After doing so, the operator clicks on the "Links" tab so that he/she can add a new member. This new member does not own an account but, on the contrary, his name is linked to the main frequent bowler. Once created, the operator can flag the option "Can be contacted directly" which means that regardless of the fact that the bowler does not own his/her own profile, her/his account appears in the list of all the Frequent Bowlers. Unflag this option to hide any linked bowlers from the frequent bowler list. 

Statistics

This caption reveals information regarding reservations, no shows, cancellations and automatic cancellations as of a specific date.

Press Reset Counters
to reset these statistics to zero and to the current time and date.

Registration Date

Select the date that the member was signed up here.

Expiry Date

Enter the expiry date of the membership.

One Year Extension

Press One Year Extension
to extend the membership for a year.

This option is available only if the membership expiration date was set.

Discover Source

Select the place where the member first discovered this Bowling Center (useful for advertising purposes).

The Discover Sources
available in this scroll down menu have been previously set in Center Setup.

VAT Number

Enter the VAT Number that has to be printed on the member's invoices.

National Code

Enter the member's taxation number here.

Free Entry Code

The free entry code refers to the sanction ID code for federation bowlers.

Tax-exempt ID

Enter the member Tax-exempt ID
.

If a tab is linked to an FB with a Tax-exempt ID, when the FB pays the tab, the system automatically uses the Tax-exempt ID to calculate the tab payment total with tax exemption.

Company Name

This option allows adding the name of the company for which the player works. 

Notes

It is possible to enter any other relevant information regarding the member, in the Notes
section.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Registering a New Member > Advanced

=========== Conqueror-2-183.html — 4.3  Links ===========

4.3 Links

4.3 Links

Main Member

Click here to search for the Main Member
; for example if a child's details are being entered, the main member linked to the child could be a parent.

Once found and selected, the name is automatically inserted here.

To remove the Main Member
set, click on the little cross next to the box.

Introduced by

Click here to search for the member who introduced the new member to the Center. Once the member has been found and selected, the name is automatically inserted here.

This data can be used to assign incentives to those who introduce new members.

To remove the member set here, click on the little cross next to the box.

Destination of Statistics

Select the destination of the statistics regarding this member from the drop down menu - they can be directed to the main member in order to be saved together with other relevant data of a group or family.

Destination of Points

Select the destination of the points earned by this member from the drop down menu - they can be directed to the main member which can be useful especially for children in order to redirect their points to their parents.

Locker Link

This is a list of the lockers assigned to the member.

To assign a locker to a member, click in this bar, opening the Lockers module where the appropriate locker can be selected for the member.

Main Contact/Active Contact

Select whether the member is the Main Contact
of a group, whether they are still the Active Group Contact
and enter the Department
to which they belong in the group.

Other Member Links

Insert the members connected to the member currently selected, for example if they are a group of friends that play together regularly.

Press Add Contact
to open the New Member
window in order to register a new member of the Center.

Select a member in the table and press Modify Contact
to modify their details, or to cancel a name from the list, select it and press Delete Contact
.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Registering a New Member > Links

=========== Conqueror-2-184.html — 4.4  Account ===========

4.4 Account

4.4 Account

Category/Payments

Select the Category
from those available in the scroll menu (set previously in FBT Setup), into which the member will be entered. The member category can be then modified also from the Filters & Reports module.

By selecting one of the member categories, the four price keys in the Payments
section below are automatically set with the predefined values pertaining to that category. These can be modified by clicking on the relative field.

Select the member's Preferred Payment Method
for reservations, from those available in the scroll menu (previously set in the Price Setup). This Payment Method
will be proposed by default every time the member will book in the Center.

Owns an Account

Check this option to activate the Account
section of the window, where games, points and QCash can be attributed and managed.

Games on Account

To load pre-sold games onto the membership account, click in the Games on Account
field. The Sales window opens in which the price key can be selected for the sale of these games, then press OK
and the Payment window appears.

Refund Games

To refund one or more pre-sold games, press the Refund Games
button, select the price key and amount to utilize for the refund, regardless of the price key utilized for the sale and press OK
, followed by Refund
in the Refund Games
window.

Points in Account

The number of points present in the membership account are indicated here. The points are collected by spending money in the Center, following rules set in the Point Collection Setup.

To manually add points, click in this field and insert the number of points in the window that opens.

Use Points

It is possible to use points in the membership account, for example when the member wishes to purchase games, t-shirts and other items, by using the points in his account instead of cash: press Use Points
and digit the Number of Points
to be deducted from the total in the account.

QCash in Account

In this field it is possible charge money to the account.

Pressing in this field opens the Prepaid QCash Sales window, containing the recharge price keys set in the Trust > QCash section. Select the desired price key and press OK
, followed by Pay
in the Payment
window.

Refund QCash

To refund QCash, press the Refund QCash
button, enter the amount be reimbursed, press OK
and then press Refund
in the Refund QCash
window.

QCash Debt Limit

Set the maximum QCash debt allowed for the member.

Account Balance and Account Debt Limit

Set the maximum debt allowed for the member through his account here.

By setting a value different than zero the member automatically has the possibility to use the Member Account feature.

In the Account Balance
box it is possible to visualize the total amount of the not yet paid member receipts.

The Debt Limit
can be set only by operators with the Modify Member Account Debit Limits
privilege.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Registering a New Member > Account

=========== Conqueror-2-185.html — 4.5  Cards ===========

4.5 Cards

4.5 Cards

The card reader/writer device has to be set in Terminal Setup > Devices.

Two types of cards are managed in this section:

QubicaAMF
cards, that can be created as membership cards by writing data onto the magnetic strip of the card,
any other type of card with a magnetic strip that can be used as a membership card.

To create a QubicaAMF
card, press Create Card
, swipe/insert it in the card-writing device and then enter the required information (fields are described below).

This action must be performed only on cards used for this specific purpose, because any other information already on them will be overwritten.

To create another type of card for the member (Generic
), press Add Card
, swipe/insert the card in the reading device and enter the required information, as described below, and press OK
: the card is now associated to the frequent bowler and can be used in place of the FBT card, with no limitations.

To modify any of the details pertaining to a card, select the card from the list of cards belonging to this member and press Modify
.

To cancel a card, select the card and press Delete Card
.

Type

Select the type of card to be added between QubicaAMF
or Generic
.

Status

Select the status of the card from the scroll down menu, between To be activated
, Active
, Expired
and No longer valid
.

Description

Enter the description of the card (for example Sanction Card
, etc.)

Free Entry Code

The code on the magnetic strip is automatically entered here.

Notes

Information regarding the member can be entered here.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Registering a New Member > Cards

=========== Conqueror-2-186.html — 4.6  Photo ===========

4.6 Photo

4.6 Photo

Here it is possible to take the member's photograph if the web-cam is connected or to add a photo taken previously.

The photo is saved together with the other data pertaining to the member and is then displayed in the Member Identification window every time the member card is swiped. It can also be used for the Birthday animation on the lane, which can be activated through the member's Bowler Options.

Press Take the Photo
and a window opens displaying the web-cam view. When the member is ready and in the right position, take the photo. If the photo is not satisfactory, press Take the Photo
again and re-take the member's photo.

To load a picture already present on the computer, press Load Photo
and select the picture directory.

To clear the current picture and restart the picture procedure from zero, press Clear Photo
.

It is also possible to save the member's picture on the computer, by pressing Save Photo
.

=========== Conqueror-2-188.html — 5.  Managing Member Accounts, Cards and QCash ===========

5. Managing Member Accounts, Cards and QCash

5. Managing Member Accounts, Cards and QCash

Membership accounts provide advanced features for pre-paid games, storing & tracking member points for reward programs, debit (storing money on the account) and credit options for trusted members.

Using the membership card when opening a lane for the member, allows many options, including the following:

The member's name is automatically entered on the lane, saving time at the counter and on the lanes.
The member's default bowling rates and discounts are entered automatically.
The member can purchase more games, or put more money into their account.

Swipe the membership card to open the Member Data
window where:

The member's remaining games and money are displayed.
The member's photo is displayed for identification purposes.
Member notes are displayed to inform the operator of any special situations.
The membership card expiry date is displayed.

When the debit function is utilized, incentives can be applied in order to promote the use of QCash, such as bonus programs, where an extra value for purchasing could be applied, e.g. $22 worth of goods/services for $20.

Q-Cash allows the members to put money in their accounts for future use. This is also a great way for parents to control the spending of their children, in that they can give their children the card instead of cash. QCash can be used anywhere in the Center: at the Snack Bar, Pro Shop, for Bowling, etc. This also allows a fee to be introduced, whereby the member pays a small amount (e.g. $5) to recharge the card.

Cards used to access membership accounts, can be attributed to the member in the Card tab.

Magnetic membership cards can also be assigned to staff members, by registering them as per normal with Center members and linking this registration to the staff member in the Staff Setup module.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Managing Member Accounts, Cards and QCash

=========== Conqueror-2-189.html — 6.  Managing Invoices ===========

6. Managing Invoices

6. Managing Invoices

Back Office > FBT > Invoice

It is possible to give certain Frequent Bowlers the possibility to open a Credit Account in the Bowling Center.

In this way, the authorized members accumulate their receipts in their own account, using the Member Account
Special Payment, and they can pay one (or more) receipts at a time after the Bowling Center has sent them the invoice.

When the center receives the invoice payment, it registers it as paid in the Payment window.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Managing Invoices

=========== Conqueror-2-190.html — 6.1  Managing Receipts and Invoices ===========

6.1 Managing Receipts and Invoices

6.1 Managing Receipts and Invoices

Back Office > FBT > Invoice

In this module the receipts put in member accounts are displayed and grouped by members; to visualize only the receipts pertaining to a specific member, press Search
and select the member in the database.

Select one or more receipts and press New Invoice
to create the invoice to be sent to the member in order to seek payment: specify the Invoice ID
(compulsory data in order to proceed) and a Due Date
for payment. By pressing OK
the Invoices
tab automatically opens and it is possible to:

load a header picture and a footer text before printing the invoice (Page Setup
)
preview and print the invoice
delete/void the invoice 
visualize all the invoices that have to be paid (All invoices
)
confirm the invoice payment

The VAT Number automatically printed on the invoice is the one previously specified in the member data.

When receiving the payment of an invoice, search for it by pressing Search
, enter the Invoice ID
and then proceed with the registration of the payment in the Payment window.

When voiding an invoice, the receipts previously selected are replaced in the Pending Receipts
and will be available to be selected again to create a new invoice.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Managing Invoices > Managing Receipts and Invoices

=========== Conqueror-2-191.html — 6.2  Settings ===========

6.2 Settings

6.2 Settings

Enabling the FB's Member Account 

Front Desk > Frequent Bowlers > Account

By setting a Debt Limit different than zero the member automatically has the possibility to use the Member Account
feature.

Their Account Balance
is displayed and constantly updated: after a receipt is issued to that member, their debt to the center grows to pre-set limit.

Setting the Member Account Payment Mode

Setup > Price Setup > Denominations

To enter the Member Account
payment mode, click in the Special Payments
column, press New
and enter the payment mode Description
(that is a free choice) and select the related Payment Type
, in this case choose Member Account
.

Specifying the FB's VAT Number

Enter the VAT Number that has to be printed on the member's invoices under Front Desk > Frequent Bowlers > Advanced.

Setting the Invoice Page

Back Office > FBT > Invoice > Page Setup

The space available for the header picture is:

h: 8 cm (~ 3.15 in)
w: 9 cm (~ 3.54 in)
w/h = 1.125

If the dimensions are different the image is scaled, so the w/h ratio is important.

The maximum space available for the .rtf file is:

h: 3 cm (~ 1.18 in)
w: 19 cm (~ 7.48 in).

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Managing Invoices > Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-192.html — 7.  Member Reporting ===========

7. Member Reporting

7. Member Reporting

Back Office > FBT > Filters and Reports

Customized individual member reports can be used as a valuable tool for bowlers to track their skills and these can be printed or e-mailed to the member.

Use the filters to obtain reports or mailing lists for everything from birthdays and anniversaries to defined bowler groups, frequency of visits and money spent at the Center.

Reports can be printed for everyone in the Center's FBT database, or for individual members, based on the information entered in the filters. The more information entered, the more precise the reports. Choose between individual reports, global reports, mail merge or data export.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Member Reporting

=========== Conqueror-2-193.html — 7.1  Filtering Clientele ===========

7.1 Filtering Clientele

7.1 Filtering Clientele

Choose the filters to be applied when selecting a limited number of members according to the data they have in common.

For example, only children with a birthday next month, or only super Strikers or highest spenders, etc.

The Reset
button clears all of the fields in the search page, so the operator can perform another search with different criteria.

The Search
button begins the searching process on the basis of the data entered. Once the process is complete, the Results
tab is displayed complete with the members found.

Basic Filters

In the first two tabs, Basic 1
and Basic 2
, are the data fields present upon member registration, such as personal details, card details, membership and locker expiry dates, etc.

Set the search parameters and press OK
.

Advanced Filters

It is also possible to utilize advanced filters in the Advanced
tab. Press the Add Filter
button and in the list that appears, click on the desired fields, such as Games Bowled
, Members Introduced
, Strikes per Game
, Total Number of Visits
, etc., set the search parameters and press OK
.

Here, members can be searched for according to how many times they have played in the Center which, for example, could be useful in order to send them publicity or a bonus for having played more than ten times.

For other filters, such as Purchases
and Points
the departments for the search can be specified. Select All
if the search is not to be limited to a specific department.

To modify or cancel a filter, select it in the table and press Modify
/ Delete
.

The search can also be limited in terms of time, as follows:

With the From Registration
option, the search starts from the registration of the member in the Center database.
The Current Year
option limits the search to the current year.
To set the date desired, enter the dates in the Set Date Range
section.

A filter can be set both for the games and for the transactions for the Open Bowling
and/or League
and/or Tournament
modes.

Use the Game Eligibility
scroll down menu to find the members who played Official Games
, games assigned to the Center Average
or not.

FREQUENT BOWLER TRACKING > Member Reporting > Filtering Clientele

7.2 Reporting
