The report itself displays the usual details of the reservation plus the Contact Telephone Number
, the Date of Cancellation
/No Show
, Amounts Paid
and/or Refunded
, Notes
(if the booking was cancelled automatically an automatic explanation note appears) and the name of the Operator
that cancelled the reservation (displayed as "auto'' if cancellation was made automatically).

Function Sheet

The Function Sheet type allows the Bowling Center a simple report of the bookings and requirements for those bookings for a particular day or time period. This type of report is generated from Confirmed, Arrived or Complete bookings and can be utilized at the beginning of each day in order to visualize and prepare for the particular bookings for the day, if it is a Party booking, whether they require food and drink, have Time Games, etc. or for the end of the day when a report of the reservations of the day is required.

The report itself differs from the other types of reports in that there are two rows of information dedicated to each reservation. The first row contains the reservation details plus whether bumpers are required (B
- yes/no), the Date of Play
and Arrival Time
, the Mealtime
, the Bowling Time
, Number of People Bowling
, Eating
and the Quantity
, that is, the number of bowling sessions or amount of time booked - for Unlimited bookings this section remains blank.

On the second row, notes pertaining to the booking can be found. Other
refers to the Time Games linked to that booking, while Menu Choice
displays the customer's food preferences, while the Notes
field displays any specifics regarding the booking (entered in the Reservation Details window).

Revenue Report

Revenue Reports contain information gathered from both the Conqueror database and the Historical database providing useful data regarding the total sales revenue achieved by the Bowling Center. For example, selecting the Operator
as a filter would display the revenue generated for the Center by that particular operator and the Bowling Center can then choose how they wish to utilize this information.

Highest/Lowest Spenders

Here the highest or lowest spending bookings are displayed in the report and how much is spent per person per booking. Through the data in these reports, new methods can be devised by the Bowling Center to encourage customers to spend more for certain types of bookings or on certain days.

Selecting the Booking Type
filter, a report on the highest/lowest spending bookings for that particular Booking Type (i.e. party, league) can be formed and selecting the customer filter creates a report on when a particular customer spends the most or the least.

The reports are divided into columns displaying the Insertion Date
, the Total Amount
, the Amount Spent per Head
(in total) and are in order of highest/lowest Bowling Amount
.

BOOKING SYSTEM > Reports > Report Types

=========== Conqueror-2-383.html — 8.  Booking System Settings ===========

8. Booking System Settings

8. Booking System Settings

Setup > Center Setup > Reservations

Here the Booking System can be molded to cater perfectly for each Bowling Center.

Grid Time Unit

The unit of measurement in the Reservation Sheet is based on the minutes entered here (preferably 10 or 15 minutes), which is also the minimum amount of time that can be booked. The unit of time set here is visible on the Reservation Sheet as vertical broken lines.

Lane Status Advice Time

The number of minutes inserted here indicates how far in advance the reservation relative to a specific lane is displayed in the Lane Status window with the Reserved
label and the starting time.

Start Auto-allocation from Lane...

Select the lane from which the system starts to propose the first lane compatible with the reservation being created. Here it is advisable to select one of the least used lanes to ensure equal 'wear and tear'. For example in a Bowling Center with 24 lanes where the first 12 are used more than the next, setting the 13th lane here, proposes suitable lanes from that lane onwards so that the other 12 lanes are used more than the first 12. This setting works best in allowing balanced lane usage when changed at regular intervals.

To evaluate the 'wear and tear' of the lanes with more precision, see Lane Usage Reports.

Arrival Time in Advance

Specify how much time in advance the player must arrive in order to allow adequate preparation time before the game. At the set Arrival Time, if the booking has not been set as Arrived, the reservation will automatically be labeled Late.

This setting can then be modified in the Reservation Details
window.

Suggested Maximum of Players per Lane

This option limits the number of players per lane, in order to maintain a steady pace when playing and must be less than or equal to the maximum set in Center Setup. When this limit of players is exceeded, another lane is automatically assigned. For example if the Bowling Center chooses to set a maximum number of 10 players and 8 players as a Suggested Maximum
here, when creating a booking and setting 9 players, the system proposes that the booking be set on two lanes instead of one. This is only a suggestion, whereas if the number of players is over 10, the reservation will occupy an extra lane.

Resource Type

Select the Bowling Center's resource (bowling session or Time Game) from the scroll menu and set the default Number of Players
, the default Number of Games
or the default Time in Minutes
; this option is given in the New Reservation window.

Meals

Here it is possible to set the capacity of the restaurant, that is, the number of people that can be served meals in the restaurant/snack bar during a certain time span. In the space next to Kitchen Shift Length
, indicate the length of time in minutes into which each Shift is divided. In the space next to Maximum Number of People per Shift
, indicate the maximum number of people that can be served in the restaurant during each kitchen Shift.

Opening Hours

Define the time frame within which it is possible to allocate a reservation within the Reservation Sheet, a time frame that can be visualized outlined in red on the Reservation Sheet. Set Always Open
if it is possible to play/reserve games without interruption for 24 hours. The setting From... To...
excludes the time that the Bowling Center is closed.

Reservation Types

In this window it is possible to create new types of reservations and modify or remove ones that already existing.

To create a new Reservation Type
press Add
and give it a name, e.g. National League.

Enable the Type by Default
option if the current reservation type is the one to be set by default when creating a new reservation. To change the default reservation type, enable another reservation type: the one set previously will be automatically disabled.

If desired, assign a Bowling Mode and then choose the details to be displayed in the Reservation Details window from: Lanes
, Meals
, Payments
, History
, Staff Requirements
, Time Games 
(a tab for each Time Game is displayed).

Select the Confirmation E-mail Template to be associated to the Reservation Type
. The e-mail templates available in the scroll down menu are those previously set in Center Setup > Reservations > Template Setup.

If working with a third party program, set the relevant External Key
.

In the lower section of the window, colors can be set for the types of reservations making them easily distinguishable: select the Reservation Type in the table, choose the colors by clicking on the Background
, Text
and Border
box and choose if the reservation rectangle has to blink. If these changes are satisfactory, they can be set as default for that Reservation Type by pressing on the little save button. If the new settings are not to be set press the little reload button, which recalls the previous default colors.

Service Reservations
relative to the management of the Bowling Center are used to block lanes to allow time for scheduled maintenance and the like (or to keep lanes free for walk-in trade). These are not real reservations and do not follow the logic of a normal reservation, therefore when setting a Service Reservation
, the other options remain disabled.

To delete a type of reservation, select it in the table and press Remove
.

Reservation Status

Here it is possible to set a color for each reservation status: select the status, set the colors and close.

Press the Color Table
button in the Reservation Sheet to see the legend of values set.

Group Types

Here it is possible to define and modify the type of organization of a particular group e.g. Schools, Scouts, etc. This information will be available in the FBT module, when a group is created.

Professions and Workplaces

Allows the operator to define and modify the job description of a group.

This information will be available in the FBT module, when a group is created.

Discover Sources

The Discover Sources
(that is how/where the customer first learned of the Bowling Center, like radio, TV advertising, newspaper etc.) can be defined or modified. The information can be used for marketing purposes.

This information will be available in the FBT module, when member/group is created.

History Log Types

Here one can create informative messages that can then be chosen and inserted into the Customer History
and the Reservation History
by clicking on the Log
button in the Reservation Details window.

Subtract Time from Late Bookings

Tick this option if the operator is to be given the choice to reduce the length of timed bowling sessions when the customer is late, incurring full payment of the reservation regardless of reduced playing time (in such cases a window appears whereby the amount of time to be subtracted from the booking can be entered). If this option is not selected, the original booking time remains valid, delaying all subsequent bookings.

Incoming Reservation Delay (Minutes)

Insert here how many minutes in advance the incoming reservations have to be displayed in the Tab module.

Promotion Setup

Web Promotions require a specific Conqueror Pro license

Marketing campaigns can be managed to promote phone and/or web bookings based on discounts or special prices: by specifying the relevant code at booking time, customers can access the special conditions related to that specific promotion.

In the New Reservation
window, a dedicated field called Promotional Code
allows operators to insert the promotional code; if the Promotional Code
does not satisfy the related constraints (validity dates, minimum amount required, sales channel), the operator is warned. Checks on the promotion validity are done also when changing the reservation start time.

In this window the center can define marketing campaigns. Customers can receive promotional advantages if they specify a Promotional Code
at booking time and if they respect the Validity Dates
and Sales Channel
(web, call center and/or local).

The promotion can be based on:

Discounts
: in this case, select one of the discounts previously set in Price Setup and the Minimum Reservation Amount Required
to take advantage of the promotional discount.
Dedicated price keys
: in this case it is possible to choose special price keys that will be available only for customers who have specified the related Code
. Click on the yellow field to open the whole price key structure and choose all the price keys to associate to the promotion. Click on a department to display its price keys, then select the price keys and press the assign arrow. Similarly, in order to remove a previously chosen price key, select it and press the remove arrow (it is possible to use the multiple selection of price keys by pressing the Ctrl
key on the keyboard).

Tracking Only
campaigns can be set for statistical purposes.

E-mail Template

Call Center Only

When the new booking is created, the confirmation e-mail is sent to the customer e-mail address or saved to a shared folder for batch printing and sending.

To create a Confirmation E-mail Template
press New
, specify a name for the template, enter customized header and footer and press Preview
to check it. Modify it if necessary and Save
.

When the various templates are ready, close the window, enter the Reservation Types window and associate a template to each Reservation Type
by choosing it in the E-mail Template
scroll down menu.

Fee Setup

Fees applied to reservations can be:

a Fixed Amount
: in this case, the amount entered here will be charged to each web reservation.
a Percentage
: in this case, the fee will vary with the reservation total. Enter here the percentage to charge.
a Fixed Amount per Game
: in this case, the amount entered here will be charged for each game included in the reservation
a Fixed Amount per Player
: in this case, the amount entered here will be multiplied by the number of bowlers included in the reservation

For the last 3 options it is possible to set the minimum fee that has to be charged and the maximum fee that can be applied. E.g. Enter 1 as the minimum fee in order to charge $1 even if the fee calculated is lower than $1. Enter $10 as the maximum fee in order to charge $10 even if the fee calculated is higher than $10.

These settings only affect local reservations. In order to manage fees for web reservations, similar settings have to be specified in Web Setup.

To void or remove the predefined booking fee the relevant privilege is required.

Provisional Auto Delete Setup

This panel contains settings that determine the automatic elimination of Provisional reservations made by Individual Customers
, Groups
or Anonymous
customers.

For each type of customer the operator can set when the Provisional reservation will be automatically deleted if the customer has failed to confirm their reservation.

Late Arrival Management

Specify here how Late Reservations are to be managed. Choose from:

Automatic No Show After ... Minutes

Automatic No Show at the End of the Day

Manual No Show
: Manually setting No Show
allows more flexibility. For example, when the Reservation Sheet is not full, the reservation can be moved if the customer is late.
Automatic Movement into the Overbooking Area
: late reservations are automatically moved to the overbooking area after the number of minutes specified here has passed. This allows centers to free up the lanes for new bookings, to recover late bookings when the customer shows up and to arrange the customer on the next available lanes.

Reservation Confirmation

If this option is selected, a summary of reservation details which can be printed, e-mailed or made into a report appears as soon as a reservation has been saved. This is especially useful as written confirmation for customer or Center, of a reservation.

Display Original Reservation

Tick this option to visualize the original reservation (in bar form) underneath the actual reservation duration on the Reservation Sheet. This can be useful in cases where the reservation has been moved or delayed. Deselect if only actual reservations are to be visualized, i.e. for Centers visualizing many lanes full of bookings at once, the bar can take up valuable space.

Confirmed Reservations by Default

If a Bowling Center chooses not to work with Provisional reservations this item must be selected, if however the Center works with Provisional reservations, they remain Provisional until a deposit is obtained or the reservation is confirmed manually in the Reservation Details window.

Anonymous Reservations by Default

Select this option if reservations are to be set as anonymous by default, skipping the Member Search
window when creating a reservation. Useful for Centers that don't always work with Frequent Bowlers, preferring to attach only a name, instead of a customer, to a booking.

Pay after Customer Arrival

This option can be selected if the Bowling Center wishes to refuse pre-payment, thus dealing only with deposits until the customer arrives and pays in full.

Use Quick Booking Window

Option for Bowling Centers using fast booking: when creating the reservation a simple window appears instead of the standard one. Only two fields have to be filled in: Customer
and Notes
.

If desired, by pressing the More
button, the standard window will open and more details can be defined.

Show Lane and Player Details in Receipts

Enable this option to print the reservation lane and player details on the receipts. The player details printed are the name, the handicap, the gender, the player attribute, etc.

Dynamic Prices for Local Booking System

This option enables the Dynamic Prices also for the local Booking System reservations and not only to the booking website.

Close Reserved Lanes Still to be Paid

Enable this option to automatically close lanes related to reservations, even if they are not paid in full.

Deposit Default Value (%)

Set a default percentage to calculate the deposit the customer has to pay in order to confirm their reservation.

To allow reservations without any deposit, enter zero.

Days after Date of Insertion the Deposit Must be Paid

Enter how many days are available to the customer to pay their reservation deposit, starting from the insertion day.

Days before Date of Play the Balance Must be Paid

Enter the number of days before the date of play the reservation has to be paid in full.

Message of the Day

In this space the operator can write a message that will be visualized by all of the operators above the Control Panel of the Reservation Sheet. For example: "No league games are scheduled for today". This message remains the same until changed manually.

This can also be achieved by clicking directly on the Customer Space in the Reservation Sheet.

Extended Availability

Enables the search for the Best Prices within a time range through the New Reservation
window.

The day has to be divided into four non-overlapping time zones. By default they are: Morning
, Afternoon
, Evening
and Night
.

Dynamic Pricing

Dynamic prices can be created for bookable bowling price keys: discounts can be applied automatically during non-peak hours, and higher prices during peak periods.

The dynamic price is automatically calculated and displayed accordingly with the setup; when the customer selects a price key that doesn't belong to any discount rule sets, the items are offered at the regular price (not discounted). Also, discounts and surcharges are automatically applied to bowling products only, so if a customer purchases a package including food and bowling, only the bowling part will be discounted/surcharged.

Sets of rules have to be created and then the price keys involved have to be associated to the sets of rules. Two sets cannot define rules for the same price key in the same period: the system automatically checks the correctness of the rules and warns the operator to avoid mistakes.

In order to create a new set of rules press Add
, specify a Name
and a Validity Date Range
(the rule set is valid from ... to ...) and press OK
. Now the different rules have to be created, and the price keys that have to follow the set of rules have to be specified.

Press on + 
to create a rule. Each rule that will manage the dynamic price is based on:

The day of the week: it is possible to create a rule to be applied on Saturday only, another rule to be applied from Monday to Friday, etc.
A time range: enter at what time the rule starts and ends (the rule is applied from ... to ...)
The number of days the customer books in advance: e.g. a rule can be applied only if the customer books a lane between 5 and 7 days in advance.
The occupancy calculation time range: it is a global setting for the Bowling Center; it is the amount of time to add to the starting time specified by the user, in order to define the period when calculating the Bowling Center occupancy. The minimum value is 30 minutes.
The Bowling Center occupancy: the price rule also considers how many lanes are booked for the reservation day and time, so a discount will be applied when the Center is empty and a surcharge can be applied if the Center is nearly full. The occupancy is considered as the percentage of lanes already booked within a calculation period. For example, to set a discount when 2-4 lanes are booked (in a center with a total of 10 lanes), enter 20 - 40 (%) in the Occupancy 
range.

Then enter the Price Variation
as a percentage to be deducted or added to the normal price and save by pressing OK
: the rule is now showed in the list, with the various parameters displayed in the different columns.

When making a reservation the operator can help the customer to choose the most convenient date & time by opening a screen displaying the Best Prices across 4 weeks. This screen is open by pressing Prices
in the New Reservation
window.

BOOKING SYSTEM > Booking System Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-384.html — 9.  Privileges ===========

9. Privileges

9. Privileges

Setup > User Profiles > Reservations

Here the manager of the Bowling Center is able to create different profiles regarding reservations for different operators.

Many of these Privileges are directly or indirectly related to managing payments or deposits, others are for security purposes and there are also privileges to customize the Booking System to the Center's needs.

Inserting and Modifying Reservations

This privilege gives the operator the possibility to create and modify reservations. Without this privilege the operator can exclusively consult the Reservation Sheet and can't do anything else.

Collecting Reservation Payments or Deposits

These privileges are offered separately for Centers that wish to divide responsibilities (i.e. operators taking bookings can be permitted to work only with deposits, while operators working directly with customers can be given the option to take full payments).

Giving Refunds

Permits the operator with this privilege to perform refunds.

Giving Deposit Refunds

Permits the operator with this privilege to refund deposits for bookings.

Modifying Reservation Price Keys

This privilege lets the operator change price keys in reservations.

Inserting Payment Date after Date of Play

With this privilege the operator can let the customer pay after they have played, which is especially useful for companies to be invoiced the amount due.

Opening "Not Completely Paid" Reservations

Allows the operator with this privilege to open a booking without having received full payment.

Modifying Reservation Lane Options and Bowler Options

BES, Bowland and Bowland-X Only

Allows the operator with this privilege to modify the names of the bowlers of the reservation, to change Bowler Options and Lane Options.

Cancelling Provisional/Confirmed/Today's Reservations

These privileges are separated as cancelling Confirmed reservations incurs more responsibilities than cancelling Provisional reservations, depending on the Bowling Center.

Setting No Show Manually

Permits the operator to set a reservation as No Show, converting the deposit (if taken) to income.

Inserting Unlimited Reservations

This privilege gives the operator the ability to insert reservations that can run for an unlimited time, if Unlimited play is available in the Center.

Inserting Service Reservations

Gives the operator the ability to either block lanes for league customers or tournaments or to reserve lanes for maintenance purposes.

Inserting Anonymous Reservations

Allows the operator who works mostly with walk-in customers to create bookings without specifying a member.

Confirming Provisional Reservations

Permits the confirmation of Provisional reservations without taking deposits.

Moving Provisional and Confirmed Reservations

Here the customer must be contacted in both cases, but these privileges are given separately because moving Confirmed reservations incurs more responsibility than moving Provisional reservations, depending on the Bowling Center.

Editing Reservation Description

This privilege allows the operator to change the name visualized on the booking rectangle in the Reservation Sheet.

Editing Message of the Day

This privilege allows the operator to change the Message of the Day at the base of the Reservation Sheet.

Skipping Member Search while Creating Reservations

Allows the operator to enter reservations without the Member Search
window appearing, saving time for operators dealing with walk-in customers.

Defining Groups with no Contacts

Allows the operator to create a group without assigning it a contact which is especially useful for an operator taking telephone bookings in a hurry. Whereas those with more time at their disposal, need not have this privilege.

BOOKING SYSTEM > Privileges

=========== Conqueror-2-385.html — TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM ===========

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM

=========== Conqueror-2-386.html — 1.  Overview ===========

1. Overview

1. Overview

BES, Bowland and Bowland-X Only

The Trouble Call System (TCS) offers advanced management of the hardware problems that a Bowling Center can experience, regarding the pinsetter, ball return system, etc. that block the game.

This advanced error management allows the Bowling Center to oversee their mechanics and their work, keep track of the cost of repairs (the amount of time that the lanes are out of action) and to analyze the weaker points on the lanes in order to foresee the purchasing of new material.

Problems are identified immediately by the intelligent MAG 3 pinsetter (or by the bowler or the operator) which sets off the alarms and automatically calls the mechanic to resolve the problem in order to have lanes repaired in the least time possible (minimizing loss of income for the Bowling Center). The MAG 3 pinsetter is a central instrument in the Trouble Call process in that it not only registers an error and calls the mechanic without any external input, but also reports the type of error.

When a problem on the lane is alerted to, the mechanic is automatically informed via the telephone/loud speaker with a synthesized message created by Conqueror upon notification of the Trouble Call, as to the type of error and the lane upon which it occurred. The mechanic resolves the problem and restarts the lane in error so that bowling on that lane can recommence.

The use of the telephone within the Trouble Call System enables communication between Conqueror and the mechanic, providing the mechanic with information as to the status of lane errors and Conqueror with feedback as to the mechanic's movements.

TCS provides a detailed description of any problems preventing play on the lanes in real time. It also provides reports relating the management of such problems, from the moment they were signaled until their resolution and which include the time taken to resolve the error. The Bowling Center can use such reports also to gauge (and therefore plan) how often maintenance work needs to be performed on the lanes.

TCS displays animation on the lane monitors and changing icons within Lane Status that allow visualization of the progress of problem resolution, allowing immediate visualization of the status of the Trouble Call.

This icon indicates that a mechanic call has been made for the lane.

The above icon indicates that the technician has received and responded to the mechanic call and is working on the problem.

To set the Trouble Call System Quick Button, see Terminal Setup.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > Overview

=========== Conqueror-2-387.html — 2.  Trouble Calling ===========

2. Trouble Calling

2. Trouble Calling

A problem can be reported on the lanes by various sources; the MAG 3 pinsetter, the bowler or the operator at the front desk, and the alarm and various warning devices which can be set as desired (according to the Bowling Center's needs) are activated immediately. The mechanic is automatically called by the system to solve the problem.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > Trouble Calling

=========== Conqueror-2-388.html — 2.1  Signaling Errors ===========

2.1 Signaling Errors

2.1 Signaling Errors

The MAG 3 Pinsetter

MAG 3 is an intelligent pinsetter that identifies and reports its own problems, provoking the alarms/warnings. This is an important part of the Trouble Call process in that it cuts out intermediaries, directly setting off alarms and calling the mechanic immediately.

The Bowler

When an error occurs on the lane, players (lane equipment permitting) can inform the operator by pressing the button on the ball return.

If the Bowling Center works with BES, this can also be performed on the lane console through the menu.

The alarms and warnings are activated with a default message for the mechanic and loud speaker such as "Lane 4, Bowler call".

Bowlers can utilize the menu/button to report errors on the lanes if the Mechanic Call option has been checked through Setup> Bowling Setup> Lane Options.

The Operator

Problems can be registered from the Lane Status module by pressing Mechanic Service
- then Mechanic Call
and entering the message to be transmitted both to the mechanic by phone and over the loud speaker and press OK
.

A Trouble Call can also be made through the TCS module (Back Office > TCS) by pressing New
, selecting the lane with a problem followed by OK
. The panel requesting the voice message then appears; enter the appropriate message and press OK
.

If a message isn't entered, a default message will be transmitted for the lane selected.

The Mechanic

The mechanic can acknowledge an error by pressing the button behind the pinsetter or through the Acknowledge option on the telephone and commence repairing the lane, provoking the Work in Progress mode.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > Trouble Calling > Signaling Errors

=========== Conqueror-2-389.html — 2.2  Alarms and User Warnings ===========

2.2 Alarms and User Warnings

2.2 Alarms and User Warnings

When a problem on a lane is signaled, alarms and warnings are activated for technical staff.

According to the configuration of the Bowling Center the following warnings/alarms can be set:

Telephone calls relaying descriptive messages to the mechanic as to what type of error has occurred - the phone repeats the same message, until a response has been received.
Conveys voice messages through the loud speaker which is repeated until a response to the Trouble Call has been received.
The alarm siren.
Pop up of TCS module for front desk users "Intervention required on lane no..." if the request for repairs comes from the MAG 3 pinsetter or from bowlers on lanes (enabled in Setup > Terminal Setup > Preferences).
The individual lights behind each lane, that flash on the lane with a problem.
The general light above the lanes indicating that a lane is out of order.

The lights continue flashing, the telephone rings and the siren sounds in alert mode until acknowledgement/cancellation of the trouble call has been given.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > Trouble Calling > Alarms and User Warnings

=========== Conqueror-2-390.html — 3.  Managing a Trouble Call ===========

3. Managing a Trouble Call

3. Managing a Trouble Call

3.1 Dealing with Trouble Calls through Lane Status

Front Desk > All Lanes

To acknowledge, cancel or complete a Trouble Call from the Lane Status module, click on the relevant lane icon and press Mechanic Service
whereby a panel appears allowing the operator to Acknowledge
, Complete
or Cancel
the Trouble Call.

The icons in the Lane Status module reflect the status of the Trouble Call in real time, allowing the operator to be up to date with the Trouble Calls and when it is possible to re-open lanes.

These actions can also be performed by the mechanic, using the telephone.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > Managing a Trouble Call > Dealing with Trouble Calls through Lane Status

=========== Conqueror-2-391.html — 3.2  Dealing with Trouble Calls through Back Office ===========

3.2 Dealing with Trouble Calls through Back Office

3.2 Dealing with Trouble Calls through Back Office

Back Office > Trouble Call System

The upper section of this window contains the Trouble Call events table, with the events that have been selected for visualization through the Filter button below.

Selecting an event activates various options at the base of the screen as well as completing the fields under the table with the relevant information.

Underneath the table, fields display pertinent data to the event selected, such as the Date
and times of specific actions, the Lane
number, the Source
of the Trouble Call (i.e. from terminal, lane or mechanic), the Game Type
, Error
explanation and the Status
of the Trouble Call.

Here the name of the Mechanic
can also be entered which is then saved with that event for future reference.

To acknowledge, cancel or complete a Trouble Call from the Trouble Call System module, select the relevant event from the table in the upper part of the window which activates the options and the operator can Acknowledge
, Complete
or Cancel
the Trouble Call.

The following keys are activated only when necessary:

Start Work
substitutes the Complete
button if the Trouble Call hasn't already been acknowledged by the mechanic.
Confirm lane
appears (where the Acknowledge
and Complete
buttons would normally be) if the request regards a pair of lanes and it is necessary to identify which of the two lanes has a problem.

4. TCS Reports

=========== Conqueror-2-394.html — 4.2  Reports ===========

4.2 Reports

4.2 Reports

Here reports can be generated containing relevant information as to the problems/technical errors that occur on the lanes. This report type can be of particular use for the mechanic when planning maintenance work in advance or keeping track of maintenance staff's working hours, etc. This general report can also include a supplementary section regarding the data of lanes in Workshop mode.

Technical interventions can also be carried out according to the error type, the lane (statistics of errors per each lane), the frequency of errors occurred in the Bowling Center (from the most frequent to the less frequent), the down time (highlighting the time bowlers have remained blocked), allowing the Bowling Center to monitor costs linked to repairs.

Once suitable filters have been selected, press Report
whereby the Print Report
window appears.

The report itself includes the Date
, Lane
, Opening
Mode
, Source
, Start
, Mechanic Arrival
, End
, Response Time
, Work Time
, Down Time
(the amount of time that the lane was out of action), Status
, Mechanic
, Error/Comments
.

At the end of the report is the summary of data regarding the Trouble Calls in that specific report; voice messages sent to the mechanic are summarized separately.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > TCS Reports > Reports

=========== Conqueror-2-395.html — 5.  TCS Setup ===========

5. TCS Setup

5. TCS Setup

Setup > Center Setup > Trouble Call System

The Access TCS Settings
privilege is necessary to modify these values.

In this window it is possible to configure QDacs, set the alarm and warnings and configure the voice alarm.

When all of the settings have been defined, press Save
.

Here it is also possible to send a reboot command to the QDac
currently selected in the drop down menu, by pressing the Reboot
button.

Alert on All QDacs

If checked, the settings contained herein are applied to all QDacs present within the Bowling Center.

If, for example, there are two levels within the Center with 20 lanes each, the QDacs should have separate settings (this option should therefore remain unchecked) so that a lane error upstairs doesn't provoke the alarms downstairs if there are mechanics present on both levels.

5.1 Alarm and Warning Settings

to automatically set a new QDac. Enter the Serial Number
pertaining to the newly created QDac and a customized Description
.

to eliminate the currently selected QDac.

Lanes

The lanes setting must be set from the first lane to the maximum number of lanes connected to the QDac being configured e.g. 1-24 if the Bowling the QDac is connect to the first 24 lanes in the Center and 25-50 for the second QDac.

or Disconnected
to the Conqueror Server.

=========== Conqueror-2-397.html — 5.2  Voice Alarm Settings ===========

5.2 Voice Alarm Settings

5.2 Voice Alarm Settings

Voice

The "voice" can be chosen from those installed, utilized when transmitting voice messages via the telephone to the mechanic or via the loud speaker.

Voice Speed

The speed of the voice - set by default to 10, choose between 0 and 20.

Voice Volume

Control the volume of the voice heard on telephone and loud speaker messages - set by default to 100, choose between 0 and 100.

Phone Timeout

The timing out of the telephone message (in seconds, from 30 to 240, with default set at 60) is the time between which a new trouble call can be transmitted to the mechanic if there are other lanes waiting for assistance.

Repeat Frequency

The timing out of the speaker message (in seconds, from 30 to 240, with default set at 60) is the time between which the message transmission is repeated to the loud speaker when the lane is still waiting for assistance.

Phone Pause

Pauses of a tenth of a second are inserted at the beginning of a message for the mechanic's telephone, set by default to 10, but can be set between 0 e 30.

Speaker Pause

Pauses of a tenth of a second are inserted at the beginning of a message for the loud speaker, set by default to 10, but can be set between 0 e 30.

The pause at the beginning of the message permits the user to identify when the message starts and ends - especially useful for telephone messages, where the message is repeated indefinitely until it is acknowledged, cancelled or changed.

Speaker Repetition

The number of times the message is repeated on the loud speaker, set by default to 2.

Test Sentence

A test sentence can be entered in order to test the above settings. Enter a phrase and press Test
.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > TCS Setup > Voice Alarm Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-398.html — 6.  Privileges ===========

6. Privileges

6. Privileges

Make a New Trouble Call

Setup > User Profiles > Operate

This privilege allows the user to signal a problem on the lanes, activating alarms and calling the mechanic.

Acknowledge a Call

Setup > User Profiles > Operate

With this privilege enabled, the operator can acknowledge a Trouble Call, and is therefore responsible for registering through Conqueror that a mechanic has started working on a problem. This privilege can be useful when monitoring time taking for problem resolution, keeping track of mechanic's working hours, (see Disable Phone Acknowledgement
within TCS Setup).

Complete a Call

Setup > User Profiles > Operate

An operator with this privilege is able to register when the mechanic has repaired the error and therefore completed the Trouble Call, (i.e. the operator is directly responsible for entering the mechanic's working hours).

Cancel a Call

Setup > User Profiles > Operate

Without these access privileges, the operator cannot cancel a Trouble Call in the Trouble Call System
window.

Access TCS Plugin

Setup > User Profiles > Technical

This privilege allows the operator to access the Trouble Call System
window.

Access TCS Setup Plugin

Setup > User Profiles > Technical

This privilege allows the operator to access the Trouble Call System
tab within Center Setup in order to modify the alarm and warning settings.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > Privileges

=========== Conqueror-2-399.html — 7.  TCS Telephone Commands ===========

7. TCS Telephone Commands

7. TCS Telephone Commands

The use of the telephone within the Trouble Call System enables communication between Conqueror and the mechanic, providing the mechanic with information as to the status of lane errors and Conqueror with feedback as to the mechanic's movements.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > TCS Telephone Commands

=========== Conqueror-2-400.html — 7.1  Entering Response Codes ===========

7.1 Entering Response Codes

7.1 Entering Response Codes

The generic command format is:

* <lane number><command>*

With the * symbol present at the start and end of a sequence.

The lane number is composed of either 2 or 3 digits. That is, the number of lanes affects the number of digits, so if there are more than 99 lanes, 3 digits must be entered for the lane number; for up to 100 lanes (including 1-10 lanes), 2 digits are entered.

The number of lanes in the Bowling Center is set by a command sent from Conqueror to the QDac, setting the number of digits to use. The mechanic needs to use the correct number of digits for the lane, or the command will not be executed (if the number of lanes is set at 20, the code to enter for lane 8 is '08', not simply '8').

The mechanic's own recorded message (used to send messages to Conqueror) requires that the mechanic enters zero to start recording, therefore the code must be '00' or '000' according to that explained previously.

The mechanic gets feedback as to the correct/wrong sequence entered through confirmation (for a correct sequence or a correct sequence on an incorrect lane) or an error tone (for incorrect sequences) given by the phone. The mechanic hereby understands if the correct command has been recognized or if the incorrect code was entered and it is necessary to try again.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > TCS Telephone Commands > Entering Response Codes

=========== Conqueror-2-401.html — 7.2  Commands ===========

7.2 Commands

7.2 Commands

The following commands are entered in numeric form after the lane number:

Acknowledge (Key 1)

This command can be used in 2 ways:

When referring to a specific lane it puts the lane in Work In Progress mode (i.e. if a Trouble Call has been signaled on lane 2 of 20 lanes, the mechanic would acknowledge this call by entering the code *021*.
If the lane specified is zero, this response acknowledges the generic message sent by the operator through Conqueror. Generic messages are used for communication between operator and mechanic when a specific lane is not referred to (e.g. the message "Do you need the spare tool kit?").

Repairs Completed (Key 2)

The mechanic enters the lane number followed by this code (i.e. *032* to convey that repairs are complete on lane 3), resetting the lane as OK, having finished the repairs.

Cancel Request (Key 3)

The mechanic resets the lane as OK, cancelling the request received as there was no error found (false alarm).

For example *053* cancels the error reported on lane 5, as no repairs were necessary.

Perform a Pinsetter Partial Set (Key 4)

Enter *184* to perform a pinsetter partial set on lane 18.

If the pinsetter is not capable of performing a partial set, a cycle will be executed instead.

If not supported by the pinsetter, the operation will not be executed.

Perform a Pinsetter Full Set (Key 5)

Enter *025* to perform a pinsetter full set on lane 2.

If the pinsetter is not capable of performing a full set, a cycle will be executed instead.

If not supported by the pinsetter, the operation will not be executed.

Spot Pins (Key 6)

This allows the mechanic to identify the pins to be set as they were when the game was interrupted due to an error, so that the game can recommence from when the error occurred. After the command '6', a list of pins to spot must be entered as follows:

*056 78* to spot specific pins on lane 5 (in this case, only pin 7 and 8).
*206 0631* to spot specific pins on lane 20 (pins 10, 6, 3, and 1 - pin 10 is referred as digit 0).
*206* this is an error: no pin specified for the spot pin command on lane 20!

When a command has been executed correctly, the list should contain at least one pin digit and no pin digits should be repeated.

If not supported by the pinsetter, the operation will not be executed.

Record Voice Message (Key 9)

To record a message, enter zeros instead of the lane number.

The second * symbol confirms the command; after which, the recording begins and goes for a fixed amount of time (20 seconds). To finish recording before the time is up, press * and the recorded message will be saved.

To discard the message while recording, press #
.

If the message has been recorded correctly, confirmation of correct sequence (as used for other commands) will be received; on the other hand, if the command is aborted, an error message will be played.

Aborting a Command (Key #)

Pressing the #
symbol, aborts the command. After which, enter the correct sequence starting and finishing with the * symbol.

TROUBLE CALL SYSTEM > TCS Telephone Commands > Commands

=========== Conqueror-2-409.html — 1.3  Price Lists ===========

1.3 Price Lists

1.3 Price Lists

Set here the price lists to be displayed and the promotional message and logo that are displayed together.

Different price lists can be created which are then assigned to the MMS screens through the Monitors tab.

Various price lists can be created for different occasions; for example a price list could be created for Sunday evenings and another for children's parties.

On the left of the window the Center's price keys are displayed, divided into the various departments, in a tree structure.

On the right of the screen the price lists to be displayed on the screen can be set. Each price list can be subdivided into price groups, e.g. price group for sandwiches, soft drinks and price groups for pro-shop merchandise, etc. Each price group has its own title that is highlighted on the screens.

Managing Price Lists

To create a new price list, press Add List
and enter a name for it to be displayed in the Monitors module.

Then set the first page to be displayed on the MMS screens, with a Top Message
, Logo
and a promotional message (Special Offer
).

Enter the Top Message
that will be displayed in the upper section of the MMS screen, select the picture for the Logo
and in the Special Offer
tab, enter a welcome message and/or a promotional message, to advertise products or promote events.

Next create the various Price Groups for the price list.

/ Rename List
from the scroll menu.

Managing Price Groups

To add a group to the current price list, press + 
and enter a name for the new group in the window that opens. The name will be displayed on the MMS screens as the title of that group of items.

To insert items, double click on the desired price key in the tree structure or select the price key and press Assign
: then complete the table for the price group.

To remove a price key from the list, double click on the price key in the table, or select the price key and press Remove
.

The two arrows at the base of the table, on the left, are used to modify the order of the price keys: the selected price key is moved up or down according to the arrow pressed, making it easy to order them.

To go to the next tab in order to set the price keys for the next price group, use the arrows located near the group name.

To eliminate the current tab, that is the current price group, press the trash button.

Recommended Formats

The pictures loaded should be 1280x720 pixels; if the pictures are smaller or bigger, they will be automatically adjusted.

The videos loaded should respect the following characteristics:

Video Container
: MP4, MOV, M4A, 3GP, 3G2, MJ2, AVI, MPEG, ASF
Video Codec
: H.264, MPEG-2 Video, VC1, WMV2, WMV3
Video Bit Rate
: 4.000.000 bps
Video Resolution
: 1280x720 pixel
Audio Codec
: AAC, WMA v1, WMA v2, MP3, MP2, DV
Audio Bit Rate
: 160000 bps
Audio Sampling Rate
: 44100 Hz
Audio Channels
: 2 (stereo)

Other Supported Formats

PowerPoint presentations can be displayed by converting the presentation into a video file using a commercially available software tool, not provided by QubicaAMF, such as "Leawo� PowerPoint to Video Pro".

Conversion Warning

Videos loaded are converted by Conqueror Pro in order to be correctly displayed on the MMS monitors.

Converted videos have the following characteristics:

Container: MP4 - Video codec: H-264 - Video bitrate: 4.000.000 bps - Video resolution: 1280x720 pixel
Audio Codec: AAC - Audio bitrate: 160000 bps - Audio sampling rate: 44100 Hz - Audio channels: 2 (stereo)

If an error occurs during the conversion procedures, a window opens displaying the error details.

In the case of conversion error, we provide a video converter to be used on a Windows 7 computer connected to the web, which is located in the Video Converter directory on the Misc folder of the Installation CD1. Move this directory and the video to be converted on a Windows 7 computer, launch Setup.exe and when the video is converted, load it on the Advertising
module.

If a Conqueror terminal of the center runs on Windows 7, load the video from this terminal. If you don't have any Windows 7 computer available, use a video converter of your own choice and respect the video characteristics listed above.

MULTI MEDIA SYSTEM > MMS Monitor Management > Advertising Playlists

=========== Conqueror-2-412.html — 1.6  Experiences ===========

1.6 Experiences

1.6 Experiences

To add an experience press Add 

and select an Experience from the Experience list. The Opening Type and Graphic Set fields get automatically populated with the first available option in alphabetical order; to change it, use the drop-down menu. The same Experience can be added multiple times as long as it gets associated with a different Opening Type. 

MMS Experiences are defined individually. The center can define as many experiences as they want and combine them with different times of the year or moments of the week. Each MMS experience is linked to a number, to prevent any experience from changing it when it gets associated with a different monitor.

Note: the same experience can be played on multiple monitors, e.g. experience 2 can be played on monitor "Entrance 2" and "VIP area 2".

The Price Subtitle and the second filed of the Tagline are not mandatory, but it could provide useful and relevant information such as the maximum number of players. The price shown on screen is the default price key of the Experience. When the default price key changes, the price on the MMS Experience changes accordingly in real time.

Each MMS Experience has his own "Graphic set", which defines the visual appearance in the monitors. Each Graphic set is made of a fixed graphic layout and background, and a predefined list of videos. Centers can add or remove videos from the list, according to the environments they are offering.

To preserve the integrity and quality of the feature, centers cannot add their own videos.

MULTI MEDIA SYSTEM > MMS Monitor Management > Experiences

=========== Conqueror-2-414.html — 3.  Strike Challenge ===========

3. Strike Challenge

3. Strike Challenge

BES, Bowland and Bowland-X Only

The Strike Challenge is a competition between all of the lanes, based on the player's ability to strike.

The special ability Strike Challenge game is preset to be played on a certain number of lanes, each lane advancing in the race as the players achieve Strikes. After each Strike is achieved on the lanes, a summary of the race placing is displayed on the MMS screens.

The first lane to cross the finish line by achieving the set number of Strikes wins the race and a new race can begin. There is also the possibility of continuing the game after the first lane crosses the finishing line, until all prizes have been won through the Loop Mode setting.

MULTI MEDIA SYSTEM > Strike Challenge

=========== Conqueror-2-415.html — 3.1  Strike Challenge Management ===========

3.1 Strike Challenge Management

3.1 Strike Challenge Management

The Strike Challenge management is accessed through:

Lane Status > Special Functions > Strike Challenge with Bowland,
Setup > Bowling Setup > Special Games with BES.

In this window different Strike Challenge sessions must be created which are then assigned to the lanes.

To access this module, the Access to the Special Games configuration
privilege must be assigned to the relevant operator.

In the upper part of the window are the informative fields relative to the actual Status
of the Session
selected, while the lower section contains the buttons utilized to create and delete sessions of the Strike Challenge, trace the prizes distributed and cancel Strike Challenge sequences from the table.

Use the New
button to create a new session of the Strike Challenge and Remove
to cancel a session.

The Save
button is activated when any details of a session have been added or modified.

The settings applied to each session can then be modified if need be while the Special Game is running.

Strikes per Race

Set the number of Strikes necessary to finish the race, whereby the player that achieves this amount first, wins.

Available Prizes

Available Prizes
refers to the number of prizes that the Bowling Center is willing to award for that particular session of Special Game, in other words, the number of wins allowed for that session.

Prizes Issued

Prizes Issued
refers to the prizes that have been won from the beginning of the selected Strike Challenge session.

Loop Mode

This setting allows the race to continue after the first lane has crossed the finish line.

Prestart Alert

Set the Pre-start Alert
field, by entering the amount of time (no more than 5 minutes). During this period, animation counts down the minutes until the Strike Challenge sequence begins.

Lanes

First select the Strike Challenge session in the table, then click here to select the Lanes
on which it (the Strike Challenge session) has to be played and Save
.

When Strike Challenge session is running, the monitor of the MMS on which the animations are shown has to be chosen.

Status

This refers to the status of the selected session, which can be either created, running or stopped.

Awarded

In the session details table it is possible to keep a record of prizes that have been given to customers, to do so, select sequences with Win
in the Result
column and press Awarded
.

When closing lanes, the View Prizes
button is activated in the Lane Control and in the Score windows. By clicking on it the names of the customers who have won prizes are displayed and a receipt can be printed for all players who have won prizes

or for one specific winner (by using the scroll menu).

Delete

Press Delete
to open a panel where it is possible to choose whether to delete the sequence selected in the session details table or only the sequences prior to the selected sequence or all of the sequences in the table. Here there is also the option to delete all of the sequences except for those not yet attributed prizes; to do so, check the option and then delete all.

=========== Conqueror-2-418.html — CALL CENTER ===========

CALL CENTER

CALL CENTER

=========== Conqueror-2-419.html — 1.  Overview ===========

1. Overview

1. Overview

Conqueror Pro solution specifically designed for the needs of bowling chains worldwide: Conqueror Pro's Call Center module provides bowling chains with a fully-functional call center for reservation services where a single Call Center can manage bookings for all centers in the bowling center chain.

A fast center-selection window allows operators to connect quickly to remote bowling centers and move rapidly between different centers. When connecting to a center the system has been specifically designed to speed up the whole booking process for Call Center operators by letting them define the key reservation details (date, time, number of bowlers) on the opening page.

The Call Center interfaces in real-time with the QubicaAMF Conqueror systems and servers in each remote center for seamless integration without the need for any manual intervention at the remote centers apart from the fundamental requirement that the Conqueror server at each center is accessible from the central Conqueror server at the Call Center, and that the Call Center users are also present as users at each remote center. In addition, the Call Center must have the "Call Center" Conqueror Pro license while each remote center must have at least one "Remote Access" license.

Template letters for reservation confirmations and booking confirmation e-mails are sent automatically from Conqueror without passing through the local mail client on the Call Center Conqueror terminal. The Consolidated Reports feature allows reservation reports to be easily obtained from any/all remote centers without needing to access each center individually.

Terminal and operator user preferences on the local Call Center terminals are preserved when connecting to the remote centers, Call Center terminals are able to connect to remote centers with different Conqueror versions; a Conqueror user privilege controls which Call Center users have access to Authorization Required
centers through a privilege; and it is possible to set remote centers as Not Available
for call center operators in the case of problems at the remote center.

CALL CENTER > Overview

=========== Conqueror-2-420.html — 2.  Operating in the Call Center ===========

2. Operating in the Call Center

2. Operating in the Call Center

Back Office > Call Center

Simply double-click on the desired remote center to connect to, or enter the center's name in the Quick Search
field and press Enter
from the keyboard to start the network connection.

It is essential that the Call Center operator has also been created as a Conqueror user at the remote center.

CALL CENTER > Operating in the Call Center

=========== Conqueror-2-421.html — 2.1  Creating a Reservation in a Chain Center ===========

2.1 Creating a Reservation in a Chain Center

2.1 Creating a Reservation in a Chain Center

When the Call Center operator is connected to the remote center, a welcome window opens where they can define all the basic reservation details in order to speed up the reservation process. Select the booking date and time, enter the number of players and press New
. The Booking System opens and the New Reservation dialog is prompted, with the basic data previously defined, automatically set.

Note that the default Number of Players is the value set in Setup > Center Setup > Reservations. Alternatively the operator can press the Reservations button to access the remote center's booking system and create bookings from there as normal. When an operator with the user privilege to view the Conqueror User Messages connects to a remote center, the User Messages
window is skipped during login in order to speed up the booking procedure.

When a Call Center operator is connected to a remote center, the center name is displayed in the external frame title instead of the operator name. By clicking on this center name, the operator disconnects from the remote center and returns to the Call Center window which allows them to change quickly between different remote centers. Note that in every window involved in the booking process (e.g. customer search, reservation search, new reservation, etc.), the window caption now also displays the name of the remote bowling center. The settings configured in Setup > Terminal Setup on the local Call Center terminal (e.g. terminal language, font type, printers, etc.) are maintained when the Call Center operator connects to the remote bowling centers.

CALL CENTER > Operating in the Call Center > Creating a Reservation in a Chain Center

=========== Conqueror-2-422.html — 3.  Reporting ===========

3. Reporting

3. Reporting

Back Office > Reporting > Consolidated Reports

A new module allows operators with the Access Consolidated Reports Module user privilege to export the data contained within the Outstanding bookings, Deposit due, Revenues and Canceled bookings reservation reports for a specific date range from any or all of the remote centers defined in the Call center setup module into xml format files, where each report is labeled with the center name and report type (e.g. PlanetBowling_RevenuesReport.xml).

The remote Centers are listed in a table and selected by default. It is possible to unselect some centers if necessary, then set a Date Range
(between which the reservations were inserted) and select one or more of the reservation Reports from the four available. Note that the date filter criteria applies to the following dates for each report type:

the Outstanding Bookings
report uses the Full Payment Due Date, 
the Deposit Due
report uses the Deposit Due Date,
the Revenues
report uses the Insertion Date,
the Cancelled Bookings
report uses the Cancellation Date.

During the process, the download status is displayed in the table and it is possible to interrupt the download at any time by pressing Cancel
. The download status can be: 

Downloading - download is in progress,
Completed - download procedure has been completed successfully (and the downloaded reports are available),
Cancelled - the Cancel button was pressed and the download procedure has been interrupted,
Connection Error - the connection timeout was reached or the remote center was not reachable,
Saving Error - the specified Report Folder is incorrectly configured.

CALL CENTER > Reporting

=========== Conqueror-2-423.html — 4.  Call Center Setup ===========

4. Call Center Setup

4. Call Center Setup

Setup > Call Center Setup

In this window it is possible to configure the complete Call Center system and the Access Call Center Setup user privilege is required.

CALL CENTER > Call Center Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-424.html — 4.1  Adding Bowling Centers ===========

4.1 Adding Bowling Centers

4.1 Adding Bowling Centers

In order to create and configure a new connection to a remote bowling center, press New
and enter the following data:

Name

Enter the center name that will be displayed in the center icon on the Call Center window.

Color

Choose the color for the center icon.

Server IP Address

Enter the Conqueror Server IP address of the remote center.

Welcome Message

Enter the message that will be displayed to the Call Center operator during the connection to the remote center, e.g. a promotional message to be reported to the customer.

Authorization Required

This option restricts access to a remote center to only those Call Center operators with the required user privilege Connect to Authorization Required Centers
.

Currently Available

This option enables/disables the connection to the remote center from the Call Center.

Not Available Because

The message entered here is shown when the Call Center operator selects a not-available remote center, and you can explain the reason the remote center is currently unavailable.

CALL CENTER > Call Center Setup > Adding Bowling Centers

=========== Conqueror-2-425.html — 4.2  Call Center Settings ===========

4.2 Call Center Settings

4.2 Call Center Settings

Unsent E-mail Folder

Choose the shared directory where unsent e-mails will be saved. When a new booking is created, the confirmation e-mail is sent to the customer's e-mail address or saved to this shared folder as an XML file for batch printing and sending by mail. The e-mail may be "unsent" because the customer's e-mail address is incorrect or the Call Center has a (temporary) problem sending out e-mails in which case the confirmation e-mail is automatically saved by Conqueror.

Report Folder

Choose the shared directory where the reservations reports will be saved in XML format, after having generated them in the Consolidated Reports module.

Confirmation E-mail Templates

It is possible to create various booking confirmation e-mail templates, that are associated to each Reservation Type and are then sent or mailed to customers after making a reservation. In order to create a Confirmation E-mail Template press the E-mail Template button and then New
to create a new e-mail template.

Specify a name for the template, enter your customized header and footer and then press Preview to check it. After which you can Modify it if necessary and finally Save. When the various templates are ready, close the window and enter the Reservation Types menu - inside which it is possible to associate an e-mail template to each Reservation Type by selecting it from the E-mail Template drop-down menu.

E-mail Protocol Settings

Setup > Center Setup > Basic

Enter the E-mail Protocol Settings.

Number of Versions to Maintain

Setup > Center Setup > System

Call Center operators can connect to remote centers running different (the same or earlier) versions of Conqueror Pro provided that the Call Center terminal has the earlier QDesk folder saved in the same directory as the "active" QDesk folder for the currently-installed Conqueror version running at the Call Center.

The number of versions to maintain option specifies how many older QDesk folders to keep in order to maintain backward-compatibility with remote centers running older Conqueror versions.

The number of versions that can be kept must be between 2 and 10.

CALL CENTER > Call Center Setup > Call Center Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-426.html — WEB SETUP ===========

WEB SETUP

WEB SETUP

=========== Conqueror-2-427.html — 1.  Web Reservations ===========

1. Web Reservations

1. Web Reservations

Setup > Web Setup > Web Reservations

Before using the Booking Website, it is necessary to set at least 1 Web Scenario
.

A reservation scenario is a web reservation type and it is made up of a name, a personalized picture, a Deposit
value, a Reservation Type
, a brief Description
and price keys.

In this case, for example, the Bowling Center has 4 scenarios, displayed in 4 squares:

WEB SETUP > Web Reservations

=========== Conqueror-2-428.html — 1.1  Reservation Scenarios ===========

1.1 Reservation Scenarios

1.1 Reservation Scenarios

To create a scenario press New
, enter the Scenario
settings as described below and press Save
.

Name/Picture/Description

Give the scenario a name that will be displayed at the top of the scenario square.

Choose a Picture
to illustrate the scenario and type a short Description
that will be displayed next to the picture.

To load one of the default pictures already present in the system, click on the folder icon and choose the desired picture. The folder that opens is in the following directory: QDesk\Graphics\Web\Scenarios.

To load other (customized) pictures, save them on the computer and proceed as above. Please note that they must be in .bmp, .gif, .jpg, .jpeg or .png format. The maximum size must be: width 133 x height 100 pixels.

Reservation Type

Choose the Reservation Type
to associate to the current scenario.

The Reservation Types available in the scroll down menu are those set previously in Center Setup > Reservations.

Tag

This option allows to enter one or more tags helping to categorize scenarios in different groups.

Please refer to the Center's webmaster in order to manage scenario tags.

Number of Players

Set the minimum and the maximum number of players for the scenario.

Allow Web Customer Note

Enable this option to display a free space where the web customer can enter a note. This note will be reported in the Reservation Details.

Extended Availability

It is now possible to specify for each individual web scenario when the Extended Availability feature is to be used. E.g. when a scenario is dedicated to a single event (such as for the Halloween weekend), the center may wish to disable the Extended Availability in order to avoid displaying an almost empty grid.

Deposit

Here it is possible to set the deposit the web customer has to pay by credit card to confirm their reservation.

The deposit can be either a Percentage
of the total reservation due or a Fixed
amount: select the deposit type and specify the desired values. To allow reservation without any deposit, check the No Deposit
option.

Booking Restrictions

Specify time limits in which web customers are allowed to book. For example it is possible to set that:

It is not possible to book less than [2] [hours] before playing
It is not possible to book more than [3] [years] before playing

Validity Dates

This option allows creating web reservation scenarios dedicated to an event or a certain period (e.g. the holiday season, summer holidays) etc.

The scenario is available to be booked as soon as it has been created, but can be booked only for a date included in the range specified here.

E.g. It is possible to book between December 20, 2013 and January 6, 2014.

Note for Web Customers

Enter a memo for the scenario, which will be displayed above the customer requirements and highlighted in boldface type in the reservation confirmation window.

Lane Groups

Define the lane set dedicated to this Reservation Scenario.

E.g. Associate the birthday parties scenario to the lanes near the party rooms.

Linking Price Keys to the Scenario

Now the scenario is created and it is necessary to associate price keys to it.

The scenario price keys will be displayed in the Experience
window of the website and the customer will be able to choose the one they prefer.

To associate price keys to the scenario, click on the scenario on the left-hand side and press Price Keys
: the whole price key structure opens and it is possible to choose all the price keys to associate to the scenario.

Click on a department to display its price keys, then select the price keys to be assigned to the scenario and press the assign arrow. Similarly, to remove a previously chosen price key, select it and press the remove arrow.

It is possible to use the multiple selection of price keys by pressing the Ctrl
key on the keyboard.

After choosing the scenario price keys press OK
and save.

At least one price key must be associated to the scenario in order to have it displayed on the website.

Customizing Web Price Keys

To enter the web settings for each price key, click on a row/price key and press Modify
.

In the window that opens it is possible to:

modify the web price key name
enter a short Description

choose a Picture
to illustrate the price key. Click on the folder icon and load a picture in .bmp, .gif, .jpg, .jpeg or .png format (the maximum size must be: width 60 x height 60 pixels)
specify the Number of Bowlers
for whom this price key is valid
manage the Include in Best Price
option: enable it for the price keys to be used in the calculation of the best price shown in the Date and Time
page of the website, when the Extended Availability feature is used.

Scenario Display Order

The various scenarios will be displayed on the website in the same order they are displayed in this window.

Use the blue arrows to move the scenario up and down.

Managing Add-ons

Non-bowling price keys (e.g. Food and Beverage items) can be sold to web customers.

Press Add-ons
to manage these price keys, in particular to categorize them in order to make the search and selection easier for the web customer.

To create a category, press New
, give the new category a name and press OK
.

The various categories will be displayed on the website in the same order they are displayed in this list. Press the blue arrows to move the category up and down as desired.

Select the category to be filled in and press Price Keys
.

In the window that opens click on a department to display its price keys, then select the price keys to be assigned to the category and press the assign arrow. Similarly, to remove a previously chosen price key, select it and press the remove arrow.

It is possible to use the multiple selection of price keys by pressing the Ctrl
key on the keyboard.

After choosing the price keys that make up the category, press OK
and save.

The price keys chosen will be displayed in the website add-on page in the order they are displayed in this column of this window. Press the blue arrows to move the price keys up and down as desired.

To customize the price key display for the web, click on a price key in the right-hand column and press Modify
.

In the window that opens it is possible to enter a customized display name and to choose a Picture
to illustrate the price key: click on the folder icon and load a picture in .bmp, .gif, .jpg, .jpeg or .png format (the maximum size must be: width 60 x height 60 pixels).

Enter a Promotional Message
for the web customers that will be displayed on the add-on page.

WEB SETUP > Web Reservations > Reservation Scenarios

=========== Conqueror-2-429.html — 1.2  Web Reservation Global Settings ===========

1.2 Web Reservation Global Settings

1.2 Web Reservation Global Settings

Allow Payment without Activation

With this option enabled the web customers will not receive the confirmation e-mail and will be able to pay directly after entering their data in the form. After payment they will receive 2 e-mails confirming their activation and their payment.

CV2 Code Necessary

Enable this option if the web customer has to enter the CV2 code when paying by credit card.

Reservation Time Unit (Minutes)

The unit of measurement for web reservations is set here (e.g. 15 or 30 minutes). This is also the minimum amount of time that can be booked.

The number of minutes has to be a multiple of the Grid Time Unit of the Reservation Sheet set in Center Setup > Reservations.

Book before Playing (Hours)

Specify how much time (hours) in advance the customer must book.

Display Price Keys Available before/after Selected Time (Minutes)

The main price key associated to the scenario will be displayed in the Experience
window of the website in boldface type, but it is also possible to display alternative price keys to the customer.

With the 2 options Display Price Keys Available before Selected Time
and Display Price Keys Available after Selected Time
it is possible to set the time frame within which alternative price keys have to be suggested.

Maximum Playing Duration after Last Bookable Time

Specify the time before which a web reservation has to finish after the last bookable time in order to avoid accepting web booking that are destined to run beyond the bowling center closure time.

E.g. Bookable Times 1.00 p.m./11.30 p.m. and Web Last Playing Duration
off-set 1h30: a web customer can book from 11.00 p.m. until 1.00 a.m. because 1.00 a.m. is between 11.30 p.m. and 1.00 a.m. (1.00 a.m. = 11.30 p.m. + 1h30)

Default Password for Web Users

Members with the Web User option enabled can login to the Center Website using their e-mail address and the default password set here.

After logging in with this password, the member can choose a personal one.

Reservation Conditions

Enter the conditions customers have to respect regarding their reservation.

The Reservation Conditions
entered here will be displayed below customer requirements and will be highlighted in boldface type in the reservation confirmation window.

Menu Choice Message

Enter here a customized message to be displayed on the website in case of sell open choice menu.

Disabled Scenario Message

Specify the message explaining to the web customer why web reservations are not accepted at that specific day/time.

The message is displayed in the Extended Availability page when the web customer passes with the mouse over a not-bookable cell. E.g. "Sorry but on new Year's Eve we don't accept web reservations, please call 800-222222 to book."

Discount Display Setup

Discounts can be highlighted on the website with a colored label, in order to boost the reservations during the empty hours. To use this feature, press Discount Labels
and define the 3 discount ranges that will correspond to 3 different colors.

The discount is also highlighted in the confirmation e-mail and in the Reservation Details window in order to make the operator and the customer aware of the applied discount.

Lane Groups

This feature allows to avoid web reservations on different lane groups, for example, on two lane groups for split houses: the first group on the ground floor, the second group on the first floor. In this way customer groups will not be assigned on different lane groups, e.g. they will not find themselves on different floors.

Give the first Lane Group
an appropriate Name
(e.g. "1st floor") then enter the group starting lane (From Lane
) and ending lane (To Lane
).

Lane groups are set as Bookable Via Web
by default. Disable the option for the lane groups dedicated to walk-in customers.

Specify also if in the Bowling Center:

it is possible to accept reservations on different groups when there is no availability in the same group.
reservations are only on lanes pertaining to the same group.

Fee Setup

Fees applied to reservations can be:

a Fixed Amount
: in this case, the amount entered here will be charged for each web reservation.
a Percentage
: in this case, the fee will vary with the reservation total. Enter here the percentage to charge.
a Fixed Amount per Game
: in this case, the amount entered here will be charged for each game included in the reservation
a Fixed Amount per Player
: in this case, the amount entered here will be multiply by the number of bowlers included in the reservation

For the last 3 options it is possible to set the minimum fee that has to be charged and the maximum fee that can be applied. E.g. Enter 1 as the minimum fee in order to charge 1$ even if the fee calculated is lower than 1$. Enter 10$ as the maximum fee in order to charge 10$ even if the fee calculated is higher than 10$.

These settings only affect web reservations. In order to manage fees for local reservations, similar settings have to be specified in Center Setup.

To void or remove the predefined booking fee the relevant privilege is required.

WEB SETUP > Web Reservations > Web Reservation Global Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-430.html — 1.3  Bookable Days and Times ===========

1.3 Bookable Days and Times

1.3 Bookable Days and Times

It is possible to prevent web reservations on a particular day of the week or on a specific date, to define a specific opening time and last bookable time for particular calendar days. Moreover it is possible to specify the time before which a web reservation has to finish after the last bookable time in order to avoid accepting web booking that are destined to run beyond the bowling center closure time.

Define the time range within which booking is possible for each day of the week and enter a message to be displayed to the web customer when passing the mouse pointer over an unavailable slot. E.g. "Sorry but it is not possible to book lanes from the web for Saturdays, please join us directly at the Center."

To create a particular rule for a specific calendar day (e.g. September the 8th) press + 
, choose the date and:

disable web reservations for that specific day and enter a customized message for the web customer

or

customize the time range in which web reservations can be made.

E.g. Bookable Times 1.00 p.m./11.30 p.m. and Maximum Reservation Duration after the Last Bookable Time off-set 1h30: a web customer can make a reservation that starts at 11.00 p.m. and finishes before 1.00 a.m. (1.00 a.m. = 11.30 p.m. + 1h30)

Rules defined for specific calendar days overwrite the default rule defined for that day of the week; e.g. if Halloween is a Friday, the October 31st booking time is applied, not the standard Friday one.

These times have to be compatible with the Bowling Center Opening Hours set in Center Setup > Reservations.

WEB SETUP > Web Reservations > Bookable Days and Times
