=========== Conqueror-2-483.html — 8.1  Lane Counters ===========

8.1 Lane Counters

8.1 Lane Counters

Conqueror permits the generation of Lane Usage Reports, which provide data on lane (and parquet) consumption. These reports are based on the number of bowled frames and the number of minutes the lane was open, which can also be viewed in the lane tooltip.

By pressing the Counters
button in Lane Setup, it is possible to perform the following actions regarding the values of the lane consumption counters (expressed in Frames
and Time
) for the Lanes
selected:

Reset Total

The counter totals can be reset for example after lane maintenance or at the beginning of the year to provide an account of annual lane usage.

Decrease

It is possible to decrease the counters to the lowest existing value. For example, the least used lane has been used for a total of 6000 minutes, with this option checked, the total of all lanes is reduced to 6000 minutes.

TECHNICAL SETUP > Lane Setup > Lane Counters

=========== Conqueror-2-484.html — 9.  High Scores ===========

9. High Scores

9. High Scores

The High Scores module is a feature of HyperBowling which celebrates the best HyperBowl, HyperBowl Pro and HyperActive games played over a certain period of time. Each High Score is a dynamic ranking system, which displays the top ten game sessions of a Hyper game; for example, the best game sessions of the day or month. Centers can decide which High Scores to active, choosing between four predefined and five custom standings, defined by the user. 

High Score settings cannot be customized for each Hyper game. At the end of each game, the player who entered the High Scores or raised in ranking is celebrated on overboard monitors. Standings rotate on a monitor through the new MMS "High Scores" service and, when lanes are closed, also on overhead monitors and SuperTouch screens. To avoid displaying with very low scores or with less than ten players, standings are pre-populated with ten "dummy" sessions. Centers can set an entry score for each High Score, which corresponds to the score of the tenth player. 

TECHNICAL SETUP > High Scores

=========== Conqueror-2-485.html — 9.1  High Score Standings ===========

9.1 High Score Standings

9.1 High Score Standings

There are four predefined High Scores: Daily, Weekly, Monthly and All-Time. The start and end time are based on the "business day"; therefore, some High Scores may include games played after midnight up to the date change time defined in Conqueror Pro. The week starts on the day selected as "First day of the week" in Center Setup, the month starts from day 1, and the start date of the All-Time High Scores coincides, by default, with the first opening of the setup. 

If a more recent start date is set for the All-Time High Scores; games, older than the new date, will be deleted according to the rules defined in Conqueror Pro.

The center can define up to five custom High Scores to cover special times of the year (Christmas, Easter, Halloween, Spring Break and so on) or special events arranged by the center. An active custom High Score is displayed and celebrated on its specific date range only. E.g.: Christmas High Scores will be displayed and celebrated only at Christmas. Note: two custom High Scores cannot run at the same time.

If two players have the same score, the system follows these criteria to determine their position in the ranking:

� the bowler that played fewer levels gets a better position in the standings

� the bowler that played the session before the others gets a better position in the standings

The session score, used to enter the rankings, is displayed on SuperTouch in the Session Progress and Totals screens.

TECHNICAL SETUP > High Scores > High Score Standings

=========== Conqueror-2-486.html — 9.2  Standings ===========

9.2 Standings

9.2 Standings

At the end of each Hyper games, the system verifies whether there are events to celebrate and displays celebratory animations on overhead monitors. A bowler is celebrated either when they enter the standings or raise in ranking. Players gets celebrated for two achievements at most: 

� Best result obtained in a predefined ranking 

� Any position obtained in a custom ranking 

If no custom High Scores has been defined, and the center has only two predefined standings; the bowler is celebrated for one predefined High Score only. 

The player, entering multiple predefined standings, is celebrated only for one, according to the following prioritization of events:

� Ranks first in one of them

� Rises in ranking (also including the case in which the player enters the standings)

If the same event occurs (e.g. the bowler ranks first in the Daily and All-Time High Scores), the player is celebrated for the most prestigious High Score only: All-Time (most prestigious) while Daily (less prestigious).

During on-screen celebrations, players can change their names to amend any typos. To avoid idle time, the rename screen has a timeout system at the end of which the window closes automatically.

TECHNICAL SETUP > High Scores > Standings

=========== Conqueror-2-487.html — 9.3  High Scores - Setup Tab ===========

9.3 High Scores - Setup Tab

9.3 High Scores - Setup Tab

Use this setup to activate a High Score and decide on which Hyper games the settings must be applied, define a subtitle and set an entry score for each ranking. It is also possible to change the start date of the All-Time High Scores (e.g. beginning of the previous year); and in the case of custom High Scores, centers can define a start and end date within which the ranking would be running. 

To define a custom High Score, users must enter a title and define a start and end date which cannot overlap the date range of another custom High Score. The name of the custom High Score is displayed during celebration, and both title and subtitle are shown on MMS standings and printing. To change the name of "dummy" players, used to populate standings with less than ten real sessions, use the "Default Players" button.

TECHNICAL SETUP > High Scores > High Scores - Setup Tab

=========== Conqueror-2-488.html — 9.4  High Scores - Settings Tab ===========

9.4 High Scores - Settings Tab

9.4 High Scores - Settings Tab

To view or print a High Score, open the Standings tab (Front Desk> Lane Control> High Scores). The last five intervals of Daily, Weekly and Monthly High Scores are always accessible (e.g. the last five days of the Daily High Scores, including the current day). In the case of custom High Scores, all defined rankings are accessible, while the All-Time ranking is, by definition, only one.

The "Center information header" checkbox allows including or excluding the center header from the printing, which is typically in all Conqueror reports. The Modify button allows changing players' names. To do modify the name of "dummy" players use the "Default Players" button.

TECHNICAL SETUP > High Scores > High Scores - Settings Tab

=========== Conqueror-2-489.html — 10.  HyperBowling Tests ===========

10. HyperBowling Tests

10. HyperBowling Tests

SETUP > TECHNICAL SETUP > HYPERBOWLING TESTS

Perform the test to ensure the smooth functioning of Hyper games and provide a seamless gaming experience. Run the tests to promptly identify errors and keep Hyper bumpers in the best possible operating conditions.

Flag Access the Hyper Bowling tests module privilege to access the HyperBowling tests module.

HyperBowling tests module consists of two main tabs: Overview and Details. The Overview tab displays the status of all the lanes in the center; the frame turns yellow when selecting one or multiple lanes. All test buttons are located at the bottom of the window and are identical in both tabs. The Details tab simply provides more details. Click the left or right arrow to move from one lane to another.

TECHNICAL SETUP > HyperBowling Tests

=========== Conqueror-2-490.html — 10.1  Tests and Direct Commands ===========

10.1 Tests and Direct Commands

10.1 Tests and Direct Commands

You can run three tests: Test sensors, Test LEDs, Test bumpers and two direct commands: Bumpers Up and Bumpers Down. You cannot open a lane from another terminal while the test is running. To open a lane, you must stop the test first. To do so, click Stop test
at the bottom of the window. 

Test Sensors

This is the only test that provides automatic feedbacks; you do not have to check how the LEDs are performing manually as Conqueror displays the results instantly. 

Overview tab: once you start test, the corresponding lane box turns red, yellow or green depending on the result. Red and yellow indicate the sensors are performing below a given standard.

Details tab provides more details about the status of the sensors. Each sensor has a bar indicating how its performance parameters. Two triangles, located at the top of each bar, displays the minimum and maximum tracked.

Test LEDs

You can easily verify the correct functioning of LEDs and identify possible issues. When the test is in progress, all the LEDs turn white at the same time, the corresponding lane box turns yellow and the label "testing" appears on it. The label disappears as soon as the test is completed. To stop the test press Stop test
. 

Test Bumpers

You can keep the bumpers up and down repeatedly (cycle). The number of cycles can be manually set. You can run the test on either single or multiple lanes. While the test is running, the corresponding lane box turns yellow and the label "testing" appears on it. The label disappears as soon as the test is completed. To stop the test press Stop test
.

Bumpers Up/Down

You can select Bumpers Up
or Bumpers Down
to keep the bumpers up or down.

TECHNICAL SETUP > HyperBowling Tests > Tests and Direct Commands

=========== Conqueror-2-491.html — 11.  Calendar Setup ===========

11. Calendar Setup

11. Calendar Setup

Setup > Calendar Setup

The Calendar
window displays a typical layout of days and months, where it is possible to set the Center preferences regarding public holidays and special days.

The first day of the bowling week is Monday by default, and can be changed in the Basic
tab of the Center Setup module.

To move through the calendar, select the desired Month
or Year
from the drop down menus or scroll the calendar months with the arrows on both sides of the year/month selections.

Public holidays and special days for the Bowling Center can be marked as such in 3 different statuses, each with its own color (Extra1
- blue, Extra2
- yellow or Extra3
- green) and labeled with an appropriate name. On such days, special prices can be applied to bowling sessions, products, etc in the Price Setup section.

See the color code at the base of the screen: these colors make it easy to see which holiday option is being used and where.

To set a calendar day with an Extra
status, click on the suitable day in the calendar, and the Modify Day Parameters
panel will open. Here it is possible to give the day a title in the Description
section, such as "New Year's Day" and apply the desired status. If the modification is to be valid every year, select the Use this Setting Every Year
option.

For example: on New Year's day the Center may wish to apply the special discounted rates set for the Extra1
holiday. In this case, select the New Year's day, give the day a Description
such as "New Year's Day" and press the Extra1
option: the selected day is now shaded in light blue, indicating that the Extra1
option has been applied. On this day, the special prices set for the Extra1 days will be automatically applied.

Because they are considered delicate operations, all of the modifications of these settings are registered in the System Log.

TECHNICAL SETUP > Calendar Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-492.html — ECONOMIC SETUP ===========

ECONOMIC SETUP

ECONOMIC SETUP

=========== Conqueror-2-493.html — 1.  Overview ===========

1. Overview

1. Overview

In order to sell items in the Bowling Center such as hours, sandwiches, locker rentals, etc., it is necessary to create price keys containing the price, the taxes and the department that the item belongs to.

The first operation is defining the currency whereby the system will operate and express all its monetary values. Subsequently, financial transactions can be converted into other currencies, which can be displayed on screen and on the receipts.

Taxes can be applied to price keys according to the tax regulations of each country. Once the individual taxes to be used have been defined, they are to be organized in tax groups, so they can easily be applied to price keys.

Conqueror Pro provides a structure organized in departments and sub-departments. It makes the Shift report much more informative and complete and it allows optimum organization of price keys and exportation of data to accounting software. This structure must be set before defining the price keys so that each price key can then be located in the suitable department.

Once all departments have been structured and all tax groups defined, price keys, packages and menus can be created.

Then, aiding the operator in closing the Shift count and checking the money collected, the money is divided in different denominations, making it possible to define all the different denominations of money used and accepted in the Center.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Overview

=========== Conqueror-2-494.html — 2.  Currencies ===========

2. Currencies

2. Currencies

The system allows to set and define the currencies that the Center deals with.

It is essential to define the Main Currency
: the system will operate and express all its monetary values (including all the Shifts) in this currency. The default main currency the system proposes is US Dollars, which can be changed following the instructions in the Changing the Main Currency section.

Besides the Main Currency
(which must be defined - otherwise the Bowling Center cannot operate with monetary values) it is possible to identify and set any additional currencies that the Center might work with:

it is possible to display the amounts converted into a fixed Secondary Currency in the Payment window and on the receipts,
if there is no particular reason to set a secondary currency, it is possible to display, on demand, the amount of the current transaction converted into one of the Other
currencies previously set.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Currencies

, Secondary

=========== Conqueror-2-496.html — 2.2  Adding a New Currency ===========

2.2 Adding a New Currency

2.2 Adding a New Currency

The Bowling Center's other necessary currencies can be added to this list of currencies by pressing New
in the main window. A list of predefined default currencies appears where it is possible to choose one of the existing currencies; in this case insert the Exchange Rate
in relation to the main currency and press Choose
.

If the desired currency is not present as a default currency in the list, press Add
and enter the following information:

Indicate the Description
of the currency (e.g. Euro).
Enter the currency's international code under ID
(e.g. EUR).
Enter the Symbol
representing the currency (e.g. €).
Enter the number of Decimal Digits
that the system is to accept.
Enter a comma or decimal point (Separator
) for thousands and decimal numbers.
Decide whether the symbol is to be positioned before or after the amount.
Under Rounding Type
specify whether the amounts should be approximated to the highest, lowest or nearest value.
Under Rounding Unit
indicate the lowest monetary value that can be accepted and converted in the Center.

Pressing OK
brings back the list of existing currencies, where the conversion rate for the new currency inserted must be defined. Then press Choose
to put the new currency in the list of currencies being used in the Center.

In order to work effectively with the new currency, Conqueror Pro will automatically restart.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Currencies > Adding a New Currency

=========== Conqueror-2-497.html — 2.3  Modifying Currencies ===========

2.3 Modifying Currencies

2.3 Modifying Currencies

, adjust the desired details (as explained previously when adding new currencies) and if required set the currency as Main
, Secondary
or Other
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Currencies > Modifying Currencies

=========== Conqueror-2-498.html — 2.4  Changing the Main Currency ===========

2.4 Changing the Main Currency

2.4 Changing the Main Currency

Changing the main currency involves the conversion of all monetary values the system deals with. In doing so, all price keys and packages, payments and funds, QCash amounts, all data relating to Shifts (when closing the Shift or refunding, etc.) and all rounding values will be converted into the new main currency.

Before changing the main currency, the following conditions should be respected: all Shifts should be closed and printed and there should not be any pending transactions (stacked transactions) or open transactions.

To set the new currency as Main
select it, press Modify
and in the screen that opens select the Main Currency
option. If necessary, modify all other required details (as when adding new currencies).

ECONOMIC SETUP > Currencies > Changing the Main Currency

=========== Conqueror-2-499.html — 3.  Taxes ===========

3. Taxes

3. Taxes

The system allows to set and define the taxes and groups of taxes that the Center deals with. Before creating a price key, it is necessary to define the group of taxes to which the price key is assigned. Therefore it is necessary to create single taxes and organize them in groups so that they can easily be applied to the price keys.

Taxes will be calculated on the discounted total (where discounts have been applied).

ECONOMIC SETUP > Taxes

=========== Conqueror-2-500.html — 3.1  Settings ===========

3.1 Settings

3.1 Settings

Setup > Price Setup > Taxes

Following this path opens the Taxes Setup
screen. This screen is divided in two sections: the section on the right relates to Taxes
, while the section on the left relates to Groups of Taxes
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Taxes > Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-501.html — 3.2  Managing Taxes ===========

3.2 Managing Taxes

3.2 Managing Taxes

To create a tax, press New
(on the right-hand side of the screen).

The Delete
button cancels the tax selected. If the tax belongs to a group, the system will not allow the cancellation.

With the four arrows
in the lower right section of the screen the user can scroll through the predefined taxes, while the two arrows
in the middle of the screen allow the assignment of a tax to a group, or its removal.

Make the necessary changes and press Save
.

The following is a brief description of the settings:

Tax

Enter the Name
of the tax.

Percentage/Fixed Value

The selection of one of these excludes the other, indicating whether the tax is to be calculated as a percentage or as a specified value.

From/To Value

Enter the range of monetary values to which the tax is to be applied (e.g. tax brackets could change, in relation to different amounts).

RecTrac Code

Enter the tax code for RecTrac.

Rounding Precision/Rounding Type

The Rounding Precision
relates to the value to which the amount is to be rounded, while Rounding Type
allows the operator to define whether amounts are to be rounded down, up or to the nearest value.

For example, if the amount to be paid is $5.02 dollars and is to be rounded up to the nearest 10 cents, the final amount will be $5.10. If it is to be rounded down to the nearest 10 cents, the final amount will be $5.00.

Level

It is possible to define more tax application levels: level 1 taxes are applied on the net total, level 2 taxes on the total including level 1 taxes and so on.

Enter 1 in the Level
field to define the first tax to be applied, then enter 2 to define the second tax level, etc.

Included in Price

This setting indicates whether the tax should be included in the price or added at moment of payment.

Applied to Single Item

Specify whether the tax should be applied to each item sold on the same row of the transaction.

Apply to the Total of Item Value of Same Tax Group

For Canadian Market

As requested by the Canadian market, this tax calculation rule allows applying the tax percentage depending on the amount of all the price keys of the same tax category: instead of being applied to the single price keys, taxes are applied to the total of the price keys belonging to the same tax group.

Enable this option in order to use this tax calculation rule.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Taxes > Managing Taxes

=========== Conqueror-2-502.html — 3.3  Managing Tax Groups ===========

3.3 Managing Tax Groups

3.3 Managing Tax Groups

To create a tax group, press New
(on the left-hand side of the screen).

Then, define the name of the group (the system proposes a default name that can be changed) and use the arrows
in the middle of the screen to assign a tax to the group or remove one from it.

Pressing Delete
cancels the selected group of taxes.

Once again, make any necessary changes and press Save
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Taxes > Managing Tax Groups

=========== Conqueror-2-503.html — 4.  Departments ===========

4. Departments

4. Departments

It is necessary to define and organize the price keys used by the Center.

The system supplies a hierarchic structure that every Center can customize and organize according to its needs. This structure contains default Main Departments
(first level departments such as Bowling, Lockers, Time Games, Coin Hoppers, etc.) and Departments
(second level departments such as Game Bowling, Time Bowling, League Bowling, etc.) whereby price keys can be assigned to one of the default main departments or sub-departments and new ones may be added.

It may be unnecessary for certain Bowling Centers to define an overly detailed structure of departments and sub-departments; each Center can organize the structure that best fits its working needs.

The better the organization of the price keys used, the more practical their use will be. The Shift reports will be more informative and the use of QuickBooks Desktop software more functional.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Departments

=========== Conqueror-2-504.html — 4.1  Managing Departments ===========

4.1 Managing Departments

4.1 Managing Departments

Setup > Price Setup > Price Keys

This path opens the Select Department
window, allowing the management (creation, modification, cancellation) of the department structure.

For instance, here it is possible to create sub-departments such as `Beverages' or `Sandwiches' under the Food and Beverage
main department and organize all price keys.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Departments > Managing Departments

=========== Conqueror-2-505.html — 4.2  Creating Departments ===========

4.2 Creating Departments

4.2 Creating Departments

Press New Main Dept
to create a new main department and press New Dept
to insert a new sub-department (it will be positioned straight under the department selected).

Give the department a Name
and enter the following settings:

Unit of Measurement

Enter the Unit of Measurement
label: `Pairs' for shoes, number of `Hours' for Time Games, etc.

Display Order

It is possible to choose which sub-departments are to be the first ones displayed in the structure and in the POS by assigning them a progressive number.

This is not compulsory: if no number is assigned, the display order will replicate the insertion order and the first sub-departments to be listed remain those first inserted.

Department Type

For new main departments specify the Department Type
between Food and Beverage, Income, Pro Shop, Miscellaneous.

Price keys included in a Food and Beverage department will be displayed in the Sales window when entering through POS > Income > Food and Beverage menu; price keys included in an Income department will be displayed in the Sales window when entering through POS > Income > Other Income menu, etc.

Default Category/Create New Main Category

For each department and sub-department it is possible to set a Default Category
: price keys created in the department will be automatically associated to the category selected in this scroll down menu.

It is also possible to create a new main category for the price keys that will be added in the department being created.

Color

Choose a Color
for the department: the price keys pertaining to it will be displayed in the Sales window in this color. The default color is the color of the parent department.

Printer

For the sub-departments pertaining to the F&B main department, set a specific printer or printer profile. For example, the kitchen item department has to be associated to the kitchen printer so that kitchen orders will be printed on the printer set here.

The price key sent to the kitchen printer are those set as Kitchen Printable in the Price Key Setup.

Enable the Printer Profile for all Cash Drawers
option in order to link a single printer profile for all counters. Disable it to be able to specify a different Printer Profile
for each Cash Drawer
. To do this, click in the table and choose for every cash drawer a specific profile, between those previously set in the Kitchen Printer Profiles.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Departments > Creating Departments

=========== Conqueror-2-506.html — 4.3  Modifying Departments ===========

4.3 Modifying Departments

4.3 Modifying Departments

To modify a department, press Modify
.

It is possible to modify the unit of measurement of a "parent" department without causing changes in hierarchically subordinate sub-departments.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Departments > Modifying Departments

=========== Conqueror-2-507.html — 4.4  Deleting Departments ===========

4.4 Deleting Departments

4.4 Deleting Departments

Press Delete
to cancel a department.

As mentioned previously, it is impossible to delete certain main departments such as Bowling, Time Games, etc., but it is possible to modify the names and units of measurement.

When deleting a department containing sub-departments, the system will request confirmation for each sub-department, starting with the lowest level.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Departments > Deleting Departments

=========== Conqueror-2-508.html — 5.  Price Keys ===========

5. Price Keys

5. Price Keys

Setup > Price Setup > Price Keys

In order to sell items in the Bowling Center such as hours, sandwiches, locker rental, etc., it is necessary to create price keys containing the price, the taxes and the department that the item belongs to.

After selecting one of Departments
, define the price keys to be assigned to that department.

It is possible add/modify/delete price keys without stopping the activities of other operators so they can continue to use modules as POS, Lane Control and Booking System: the changes made are effective as soon as they are saved and the Price Setup window is closed. 

ECONOMIC SETUP > Price Keys

=========== Conqueror-2-509.html — 5.1  Creating Price Keys ===========

5.1 Creating Price Keys

5.1 Creating Price Keys

In order to create a price key, select the Price Keys
tab and then press New
.

The following details must be inserted:

Name

The Name
of the price key, i.e. a brief description of the item that the price key refers to.

Price

Here it is possible to enter a Price
(which can also be a dynamic price).

Tax Group

Select the Tax Group 
to be applied to this price key.

Quantity

Insert the quantity of the product to be sold at a time, in the Unit of Measurement defined when creating the department.

Berg Code

This option is only available in the Food & Beverage departments.

Enter the price key code for Berg beverage dispenser.

In the case the same Berg dispenser is used in two (or more) different Conqueror Pro terminals (by connecting the Berg dispenser to a switch), it is necessary to:

duplicate the price keys that are used by more than one terminal
assign different codes to the duplicate price keys
specify, for each price key, which code is valid on which terminal

RecTrac Code

Enter the price key code for RecTrac.

WinTic Code

Enter the price key code for the export to WinTic.

External Key

If working with a third party program, set the relevant External Key
.

Bar Code

Enter the Bar Code
of the price key (in cases where the Center has the appropriate scanning device).

Gratuity

The gratuity is automatically calculated and added to the bill if it includes at least an Always Consider
price key. The price keys considered in order to calculate the gratuity are all the price keys set as Always Consider
and the price keys set as Optionally Consider
.

The gratuity is not calculated in bills including only Optionally Consider
price keys: it is necessary to have at least one Always Consider
price key in the bill in order to have a gratuity automatically applied.

When a gratuity is applied, price keys set as Never Consider
are not included.

Special Price

Check this option if the use of the price key has to be tracked (such as price keys priced "0.00", for free items).

The transactions containing Special Prices
are listed and highlighted in the Shift reports.

Authorization Required

This function allows the manager to control the sale of particular items. To use this price key, the operator should have special permission (the privilege Sell Authorization Required Price Keys
, in Setup > User Profile Setup).

A new Authorization Required (2)
option with a specific User Privilege
has been added, in order to manage dedicated price keys of different types (e.g. some discounted price keys can be reserved only by managers, while others can be used also by operators managing reservations).

Set the price keys to be dedicated to a particular profile as requiring the authorization 1 and/or 2 (Setup > Price Setup > Price Keys), and then assign the Sell Authorization Required (2) Price Keys
user privilege to the User Profiles
(Setup > User Profile Setup > Operate).

Package Only

Check this option if the price key can be used only to compose a package, and is not to be sold directly.

Members Only

This option is used for the sale of specific price keys (for instance lockers have this default option) to Frequent Bowlers Only
.

Shoes

The Shoes
option is available only when referring to the Shoes & Socks department. If various types of shoes have been set as Fast Sale Items, set this option to prevent the operator from renting (and therefore charging) more than one pair of shoes to the same player.

Sale Price

The Sale Price
option allows the operator to change the price when selling an item (it is only possible to set it for price keys belonging to the Bar, Pro Shop, Income, Expenses and Deposits).

This is especially useful for the Deposits module, in order to insert the amount received as a deposit, which can vary according to the situation.

Point Collection

Enable this option if the present price key is valid for Point Collection and Player Club Cash according to the rules set for the whole department in the FBT > Point Collection.

Discountable

This option is enabled by default and can be disabled in order to prevent a price key from being discounted, thus excluding it from discounts applied to the entire bill (e.g. alcoholic drinks).

This option is also available for packages. In this case, the Discountable
option of the single items making up the specific package will be ignored.

Note Required

Set a mandatory note to price keys and packages. If a note is not entered during the Sales phase, it will not be possible to proceed with payment.

Best Prices

Enable this option if the present price key has to be included in evaluation of Best Prices
for the Extended Availability feature.

Kitchen Printable

Specify that the price key has to be printed in the kitchen orders.

Proportional

This option is activated only when referring to sub-departments whose unit of measurement is Time
. It allows the division of the time to be paid into minutes.

Applicability Dates

Press Applicability
to restrict the sale of a price key only in a specific date range; prices can be defined to be available starting from a specific date, until a specific date and between two dates (time limited promotions); moreover, it is possible to define specific calendar days in which the price key cannot be sold.

Therefore specify the range of dates in which the price key can be sold, by choosing from a specified Range of Dates
, From a Date
or Until a Date
.

Press + 
to specify a calendar day in which the price key is not available for sale (e.g. it is possible to define special prices that apply during the Winter Holidays, from 15th December to 10th January, but not on 24th, 25th and 31st December).

In the Sales window price keys that are not currently saleable will not be displayed.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Price Keys > Creating Price Keys

=========== Conqueror-2-510.html — 5.2  Defining Prices ===========

5.2 Defining Prices

5.2 Defining Prices

When creating a price key, insert the Price
that has to be assigned to it.

Clicking in the relevant field, the Define Dynamic Value for Price Key
window opens containing a table in which it is possible to specify whether the price key is to have the same price every day and at any time of the day, or different prices at different times or days, thus creating a Dynamic
price key.

If the same price is to be applied to the whole table, insert the Price
and press Apply
or Enter
on the keyboard.

In order to assign the price to a specific time zone, the whole table must be deselected by clicking on All
. Then, select the area of the table (day of the week or specific time by clicking at the beginning of a row or at the top of a column) to which the price has to be to applied and press Apply
. If the item is not to be sold in a particular time zone, select it and press Deactivate
and the area concerned is cleared.

To select an interval of time, press the Shift
key on the keyboard and click on the first and the last cells of the desired area. If single cells of the table are to be selected, press the Ctrl
key on the keyboard and click on the cells to be selected.

To scroll through the timetable displaying the three different time zones of the bowling day (divided in 30 minute cells) use the two arrows
on the right. Here the earliest time displayed is the one set as Day Changing Time.

The last three columns of the table, called EX1, EX2, EX3, represent three extra holidays that each Center can define for its particular needs, by going to Setup > Calendar Setup.

It is necessary to Save
the current price key before creating a new one.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Price Keys > Defining Prices

=========== Conqueror-2-511.html — 5.3  Copying Price Keys ===========

5.3 Copying Price Keys

5.3 Copying Price Keys

To copy a price key to another department, select the price key and press Copy to Dept
.

To copy a price key inside the same department, select the price key and press Copy
. Before saving the price key, it is necessary to assign a new name to it, to differentiate it from the original one.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Price Keys > Copying Price Keys

=========== Conqueror-2-512.html — 5.4  Price Key Report ===========

5.4 Price Key Report

5.4 Price Key Report

To print a price key report select the price key department and press Print
.

Here it is possible to Filter
by department, printing only the price keys related to that specific department, otherwise a report would be generated showing all the defined price keys. It is also possible to specify the Time
and Day
of the price keys to be printed, and whether the report is to be Flat
or structured By Departments
.

This report can also be accessed through Back Office > Reporting > Price Key Reports.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Price Keys > Price Key Report

=========== Conqueror-2-513.html — 6.  Modifiers ===========

6. Modifiers

6. Modifiers

Setup > Price Setup > Modifiers

Modifiers are necessary for managing all the changes carried out on price keys (e.g. an extra sauce on a hamburger or a slice of cheese less).

For example, to sell a "hamburger with extra ketchup but without cheese" the operator has to choose the normal "hamburger" price key, the With
ketchup modifier and the Without
cheese modifier.

During the sales phase it is necessary to specify if the modifier is With
or Without
.

A With
modifier (e.g. extra Ketchup) can involve a supplement (a price added to the one of the normal price key). A Without
modifier does not imply a discount on the price of the normal price key.

Duplicating Groups of Modifiers

The same group of modifiers could be used in different ways. As an example, the modifier "Sauce" could be either free (with hamburgers) or not (with fries). In order to expedite the creation of duplicated groups of modifiers, it can be copied/pasted. In this case, doing so allows linking the first group of modifiers to hamburgers and set prices to zero and to link the duplicated group of modifiers to fries and assign a price.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Modifiers

=========== Conqueror-2-514.html — 6.2  Creating a Modifier ===========

6.2 Creating a Modifier

6.2 Creating a Modifier

First press Add Group
, then give the modifier group a name (e.g. Sauces
) and specify the maximum number of modifiers that can be chosen from that particular modifier group (e.g. no more than 1 degree of cooking and 2 sauces).

This option avoids the waiters from making mistakes (e.g. adding the degree of cooking twice) and also speeds up their work because when the requested choices have been made, the system automatically prompts for the next group of modifiers. E.g. when the waiter has added the 2 sauces allowed, the system automatically prompts for the degree of cooking.

In order to not limit the modifier choices, set it to 0.

When the group is created, press Add Modifier
and give the modifier a name (e.g. Ketchup
), a price and a tax. After inserting all the modifiers, it is necessary to link the price keys to the modifiers. In this case all the price keys that might request a sauce have to be associated to the Sauces
modifier group.

During the setup phase, a price and a tax can be added to each modifier.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Modifiers > Creating a Modifier

=========== Conqueror-2-515.html — 6.3  Linking Price Keys to Modifiers ===========

6.3 Linking Price Keys to Modifiers

6.3 Linking Price Keys to Modifiers

To do this, click on the Price Keys
page and press the Price Keys
button.

To associate all the modifiers of the group altogether:

Click on the modifier group on the left-hand side,
click on the Price Keys
tab,
press the Price Keys
button: the whole price keys structure opens and it is possible to choose all the price keys to associate to the whole group of modifiers.

To associate only one modifier (not the whole group):

Click on the modifier group,
click on the modifier in the list on the right,
press the Price Keys
button: the whole price keys structure opens and it is possible to choose all the price keys to associate to the selected modifier.

To unlink a price key from a modifier select the price key in the list and press Unlink Price Keys
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Modifiers > Linking Price Keys to Modifiers

=========== Conqueror-2-516.html — 6.4  Automatic and Optional Modifiers ===========

6.4 Automatic and Optional Modifiers

6.4 Automatic and Optional Modifiers

By default the modifier is Optional
: i.e. during sales the operator has to press Modifiers
to access the choice of the modifier. In this case the modifier window must be opened by the operator when selling the price key.

A price key can have Automatic
modifiers: in this case when the price key is sold its modifiers are automatically proposed to the operator, forcing them to make a choice. To propose the modifier window to the cash register operator by default the modifier has to be set as automatic. To do this, select the price key in the Price Keys
tab and press Automatic
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Modifiers > Automatic and Optional Modifiers

=========== Conqueror-2-517.html — 6.5  Ordering Modifiers ===========

6.5 Ordering Modifiers

6.5 Ordering Modifiers

This function is particularly important to set the best display order for the modifier groups, when related to a package that could contain more than one item requiring modifiers.

Press Ordering
to access the window allowing to customize the modifier display order for each single price key (e.g. for a hamburger, put the cooking type first, then the additional sauces).

Use the arrows to put the modifier groups in the desired order. To apply the change to a group of price keys having the same modifier groups, select them before putting the groups in order.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Modifiers > Ordering Modifiers

=========== Conqueror-2-518.html — 7.  Categories ===========

7. Categories

7. Categories

Categories are a reorganization of price keys that allows customizing the Sales window so that the POS can be used in all of its potential. Each Center can organize the category structure that best fits its working needs.

The structure of the Categories is on 2 levels: the main category (first level) is the one displayed vertically on the right-hand side of the Sales window. The sub-categories are divisions of the main one and are displayed horizontally in the low part of the window.

In this example, Drinks is a main category and it contains the following sub-categories: Hot Drinks, Beers and Cocktails (displayed horizontally at the bottom of the window). To sell a large beer on tap, simply click in the main window, while to sell a special beer first click on Beers and then on the desired beer.

Creating Categories

Setup > Price Setup > Categories

To create the first category, press the button Add Category
, give it a name and press OK
.

To create a Sub-Category, first select the main category and then press Add
.

If the Center wants to use its price key department structure as category structure, simply press Import
. In this way the whole price key department structure will be imported here and can be modified as desired.

For Centers upgrading to version 5, the price key department structure is imported by default.

Linking Categories to Terminals

Terminal can be customized: it is possible to choose the categories to be displayed by default when opening the Sales window on that terminal.

To link categories to a specific terminal press Terminals
and choose the categories to be displayed by default on the selected terminal.

Category Display Order in the Sales Window

The first 8 main categories are always visible on the right-hand side of the Sales window, while to access the categories that follow them click on Other
.

Choosing the Category Price Keys

Select the category to be filled in and press Price Keys
.

In the window that opens click on a department to display its price keys, then select the price keys to be assigned to the category and press the assign arrow. Similarly, to remove a previously chosen price key, select it and press the remove arrow.

It is possible to use the multiple selection of price keys by pressing the Ctrl
key on the keyboard.

After choosing the price keys that make up the category, press OK
and save.

Price Key Display Order in the Sales Window

The price keys chosen will be displayed in the Sales window in the order they are displayed in the last column of this window.

Use the blue arrows to move the price keys up and down in the category.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Categories

=========== Conqueror-2-519.html — 8.  Packages ===========

8. Packages

8. Packages

Setup > Price Setup > Price Keys

Creating a package allows a quicker and more functional use of price keys and special offers in the Bowling Center. A package can include items belonging to different departments: for instance, one can create a package including 1 game ($5) + 1 sandwich ($3) and sell it for $6.50.

Packages can also be composed of generic items to be specified afterwards (during or after sales, even after payment). For example, a package can include 1 "soft drink" without specifying the particular soft drink (the choice might be between "coke", "soda", "sparkling water", "lemonade", etc.). In this case an Open Choice Menu is used.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Packages

=========== Conqueror-2-520.html — 8.1  Package Settings ===========

8.1 Package Settings

8.1 Package Settings

In order to create a package, select the department, click on the Package
tab and then press New
.

Price

The package default price is the sum of the prices assigned to each item, but the Price
can also be dynamic and the package can be deactivated in certain time zones or entire days of the week by clicking on Price
.

By default the tax applied to the package is the sum of the taxes of the single items included in the package itself, but in case of dynamic price, the taxes are proportional to the price, e.g. when the price is reduced by 5%, the taxes are as well.

Clicking in the relevant field, the Define Dynamic Value for Package
window opens containing a table in which it is possible to specify whether the package is to have the same price every day and at any time of the day, or different prices at different times or days, thus creating a Dynamic
package.

If the same price is to be applied to the whole table, insert the Price
and press Apply
or Enter
on the keyboard.

In order to assign the price to a specific time zone, the whole table must be deselected by clicking on All
. Then, select the area of the table (day of the week or specific time by clicking at the beginning of a row or at the top of a column) to which the price has to be to applied and press Apply
. If the item is not to be sold in a particular time zone, select it and press Deactivate
and the area concerned is cleared.

To select an interval of time, press the Shift
key on the keyboard and click on the first and the last cells of the desired area. If single cells of the table are to be selected, press the Ctrl
key on the keyboard and click on the cells to be selected.

To scroll through the timetable displaying the three different time zones of the bowling day (divided in 30 minute cells) use the two arrows
on the right. Here the earliest time displayed is the one set as Day Changing Time.

The last three columns of the table, called EX1, EX2, EX3, represent three extra holidays that each Center can define for its particular needs, by going to Setup > Calendar Setup.

Package Items

To choose the items that are going to be part of the package being created, click on the table or press the small calculator located straight above the table.

In the window that opens select the desired items and specify a Price
and a Quantity
for each one of them.

After choosing the items that make up the package, press OK
and, if the Center uses a scanning device, enter the package's Bar Code
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Packages > Package Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-521.html — 8.2  Open Choice Menus ===========

8.2 Open Choice Menus

8.2 Open Choice Menus

Packages can be composed of generic items to be specified afterwards (during or after sales, even after payment). For example, a package can include 1 "soft drink" without specifying the particular soft drink (the choice might be between "coke", "soda", "sparkling water", "lemonade", etc.).

In order to create an open choice menu, select the department, click on the Menu
tab and then press New
.

To choose the items that are going to be part of the menu being created, click on the table or press the small calculator located straight above the table.

In this window, add the items to the menu by selecting them on the left-hand side of the window and then press the green assign arrow. Similarly, to remove a previously chosen item, select it and press the red remove arrow.

After choosing the items that make up the menu, press OK
and select which item is to be proposed by default (Default Choice
).

Enable the Receive Payment without Specifying Package Items in Center Setup in order to allow payment without specifying each individual item in the package deal. So, when selling a generic soft drink with this option disabled, it should be necessary to specify the particular soft drink (e.g. "coke", "soda", "sparkling water", "lemonade", etc.).

ECONOMIC SETUP > Packages > Open Choice Menus

=========== Conqueror-2-522.html — 9.  Discounts ===========

9. Discounts

9. Discounts

The system allows to apply percentage discounts or fixed amount discounts during payment.

Discounts are applied automatically when a customer belongs to a category to which a fixed discount has been associated. For example, if a 15% discount is attributed to the Scouts
category, the discount is applied automatically when a scout's membership card is read.

Discounts can also be attributed manually at the discretion of the operator upon payment. For example, if the bill comes to $36 and the operator applies a discount of 10% to this bill, the discount is applied immediately and the total of the bill is reduced to $32.40.

As with price keys, discounts can also be dynamic, that is, applied at different rates in different time periods/days of the week (to promote bowling in the morning for example) or deactivated on certain days.

Discounts can be applied to specific price keys or to the entire bill (including taxes) at payment time, and in no case are discounts applied to transactions regarding deposits or when recharging the membership card.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Discounts

=========== Conqueror-2-523.html — 9.1  Discount Types ===========

9.1 Discount Types

9.1 Discount Types

Automatic Discounts

Automatic discounts are applied to members that belong to a Category with the right to a fixed discount; when the member's card is read at the cash register, the appropriate discount is automatically applied to that customer's bill.

When one bill is for more than one customer and some of these customers are members of different categories with differing discounts, while others are not members, the appropriate discounts will be applied only to customers belonging to categories with the right to a discount.

For example, a family bill comprises the grandfather (15% pensioner discount), the father (no discount), two children (10% scouts discount) and the mother (a regular valued customer with a super discount of 30%). The children's food, drinks and games will be discounted by 10%, the grandfather's game by 15%, the father's food and games must be paid in full, while the mother's games, food and special shoes are all discounted by 30%.

The above is true only if a member of the family doesn't have the option Apply Discount also to Other Bowlers assigned to their category, whereby the discount applies to everyone included the bill except those with a higher discount.

For example, if the father had a discount of 20% with this option checked, the whole family would benefit from the 20% discount except the mother, because she has a 30% discount.

Manual Discounts

Manual discounts can be applied to an entire bill (including taxes) or to individual price keys, by selecting them before choosing the discount.

When a discount is applied manually to a member with a regular discount, the higher of the two discounts applies. Example: if an operator decides to apply a 20% discount to the aforementioned family's total bill, the 20% discount will apply to all family members except the mother, whose 30% discount still applies as it is higher than the other discount.

Fixed discounts are always applied manually.

Click on Discount
in the Payment window, whereby the available discounts that can be applied within that particular time zone are displayed. Click on one of the discounts and press OK
to apply that discount to the bill.

The relevant privilege is required to perform manual discounts with the Authorization Required option.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Discounts > Discount Types

=========== Conqueror-2-524.html — 9.2  Discount Setup ===========

9.2 Discount Setup

9.2 Discount Setup

Setup > Price Setup > Discounts

To create a new type of discount, press New
, enter the Name
(e.g. school children) and set the following options:

Rate/Fixed Amount

Define the discount Rate
or Fixed Amount
.

In both cases different discount rates can be applied to different days/times in the dynamic table.

If the tab is not linked to a reservation, to a lane or to a time game, the discount can be inserted into the tab before payment time and then be saved in the tab.

It is also possible to define a 100% discount, allowing to "sell" single items or an entire bill for free.

When creating a price key, insert the Price
that has to be assigned to it.

Clicking in the relevant field, the Define Dynamic Value for Discount
window opens containing a table in which it is possible to specify whether the price key is to have the same price every day and at any time of the day, or different prices at different times or days, thus creating a Dynamic
price key.

If the same price is to be applied to the whole table, insert the Price
and press Apply
or Enter
on the keyboard.

In order to assign the price to a specific time zone, the whole table must be deselected by clicking on All
. Then, select the area of the table (day of the week or specific time by clicking at the beginning of a row or at the top of a column) to which the price has to be to applied and press Apply
. If the item is not to be sold in a particular time zone, select it and press Deactivate
and the area concerned is cleared.

To select an interval of time, press the Shift
key on the keyboard and click on the first and the last cells of the desired area. If single cells of the table are to be selected, press the Ctrl
key on the keyboard and click on the cells to be selected.

To scroll through the timetable displaying the three different time zones of the bowling day (divided in 30 minute cells) use the two arrows
on the right. Here the earliest time displayed is the one set as Day Changing Time.

The last three columns of the table, called EX1, EX2, EX3, represent three extra holidays that each Center can define for its particular needs, by going to Setup > Calendar Setup.

Authorization Required

This option limits the use of the manual discount only to operators with the Apply Authorization Required Discounts
privilege.

Apply Discount also to Other Bowlers

Option available only for percentage discounts

This option extends the discount of a member to all their fellow bowlers.

Therefore, if a member belongs to a category with a discount containing this option checked, their discount will be applied to the whole bill (e.g. all family members or friends, etc.) and not to the individual customer alone (except in cases where the individual customer already has the right to a higher discount).

For example, if the father had a discount of 20% with this option checked, the whole family would benefit from the 20% discount except the mother, because she has a 30% discount.

Member Category Only

Option available only for percentage discounts

Set the Member Category Only
option to send the current discount to the FBT Setup, where the discount can be linked to one or more Member Categories.

Note that this discount cannot be applied manually in the Payment window; it can only be associated to a category of members and be applied automatically.

External Key

If working with a third party program, set the relevant External Key
.

Italian and Romanian Market

Fixed discounts on single price keys are managed for both the Italian and the Romanian markets.

For a technical limit of WinTic on rounding and subdivisions of the account part of SIAE, in the Italian market it is necessary to avoid using percentage discount on the single price keys.

For a technical limit of WinTic on rounding, it is necessary to avoid using discounts on the transaction total (for both markets).

ECONOMIC SETUP > Discounts > Discount Setup

=========== Conqueror-2-525.html — 9.3  Linking Discounts to Member Categories ===========

9.3 Linking Discounts to Member Categories

9.3 Linking Discounts to Member Categories

The discounts created previously can then be associated to categories such as pensioners, children and regulars, by going to Back Office > FBT > FBT Setup and selecting the appropriate discount to apply to that category.

Next, the appropriate customers must be put in that particular category through Front Desk > Frequent Bowlers by choosing the category from the scroll down menu in the upper left-hand section of the Account
window.

The customer is given the appropriate discount automatically from hereon.

Fixed discounts can only be applied manually; they can't be linked to Member Categories
.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Discounts > Linking Discounts to Member Categories

=========== Conqueror-2-526.html — 10.  Denominations ===========

10. Denominations

10. Denominations

The system allows to set and identify the denominations that a Center deals with. This is useful for the operator at the end of a Shift, when checking collected money (advanced Shift management), enabling the operator to specify the exact amount of every single denomination (for example: 9 ten-dollar notes = 90 dollars, plus 10 five-dollar notes = 50 dollars and so on) and the system will then calculate the total collected.

It is possible to identify the Denominations
of currencies accepted in the Bowling Center and any other possible Payment Modes
(credit card, QCash, etc.).

ECONOMIC SETUP > Denominations

=========== Conqueror-2-527.html — 10.1  Settings ===========

10.1 Settings

10.1 Settings

Setup > Price Setup > Denominations

Here Notes
, Coins 
and Special Payments 
(such as credit cards, vouchers, cheques, etc.) can be inserted.

The window opens with the main currency of the Bowling Center selected.

To enter a coin, click in the Coins
column, press New
and enter the denomination Description
(e.g. "quarter of a Dollar") and its Value
("$ 0.25"). Repeat this operation for all denominations.

After putting in all the denominations of the main currency, press the Currency
button, select a different currency in the list and enter a new set of Notes
, Coins
and Special Payments
.

To modify a denomination already present, select it in the columns and press Modify
.

Special Payment Settings

To enter a Special Payment
(such as QCash, Credit Card, Prepaid Cards, etc.), click in the third column, press New
and:

enter the payment mode Description
(that is a free choice)
if working with a third party program, set the relevant External Key

select the related Payment Type
(QCash, Credit Card, Prepaid Card, Debit Card, Member Account, etc.)
enable the Authorization Required
option to limit the use of the current payment mode only to operators with the required Access 'Authorization Required' payment modes
privilege.
if enabling the Display Total in Money Summary
, the total related to the Special Payment is automatically calculated and displayed when closing shift, in the Money Received Summary
.

For Special Payments
set as Undefined
, it is also possible to predefine more that 1 default value, in order to make the payment procedure quicker. In case of coupons, for example, it is possible to set coupons of 5$, 10$ and 15$. In the Payment window, when choosing the Coupon
payment mode, a little window opens displaying the 3 different values allowing to quickly enter the quantity of coupons received. To set the payment mode default values, click on Default Values
field, enter the first value, press + 
, then enter the second value, etc.

The order of the Special Payments in the column is also the display order in the Payment window; use the arrows to customize it.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Denominations > Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-528.html — 11.  Fast Sale Items ===========

11. Fast Sale Items

11. Fast Sale Items

Fast Sale Items make the selling of price keys easier because price keys set as fast sale items are immediately ready to be sold.

When opening a lane from Lane Status or from the Booking System, these items appear allowing quick selection/sale of the relative price key.

It is possible to set maximum 3 fast sale items per resource.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Fast Sale Items

=========== Conqueror-2-529.html — 11.1  Settings ===========

11.1 Settings

11.1 Settings

Setup > Price Setup > Fast Sale Items

Here it is possible to create the fast sale items to be used in the Bowling Center.

Select the Resource Type 
on the right-hand side of the screen that the fast sale item is to be applied to. Note that a label such as Resource Type: 1/4
means that the first among four resources available in the Center is being selected.

Then, on the left-hand side of the screen, select the Department
containing the price keys to be made into fast sale items. Depending on the resource selected on the right, the department structure will only display the departments available.

On the left-hand side of the Fast Sale Items
screen is a list of the price keys belonging to the department selected that can be assigned as fast sale items on the right.

Now select a price key and press Assign
to move it to the Resource Fast Sale Items
window.

This operation cannot be executed for certain price keys, such as Members only, Authorization required and Sale price.

As the fast sale items are set, a Fast Sale
check appears for each item. Select those to be displayed when opening a lane as default items to be sold. When opening a lane, deselect items not to be sold.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Fast Sale Items > Settings

=========== Conqueror-2-530.html — 12.  Lane Orders ===========

12. Lane Orders

12. Lane Orders

BES X, BES, Bowland and Bowland-X Only

Customers can make bar orders from their lane monitors, choose items in special menus and specify the amount they will tender so that the operator can easily prepare the necessary change.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Lane Orders

=========== Conqueror-2-531.html — 12.1  How Does it Work with BES X? ===========

12.1 How Does it Work with BES X?

12.1 How Does it Work with BES X?

Bowler Side

When tapping on the Food & Drink Orders menu item on the SuperTouch console, a window opens, and the bowler can browse and choose what to put in their order, displayed on the right-hand side.

To modify a price key quantity, the bowler selects the row in the order and adjusts it using the + and - buttons.

When pressing the Confirm button, a window appears prompting for a confirmation and allowing specifying the amount the customer will tender so that the operator can easily prepare the necessary change (if enabled).

Operator Side

When an order is sent from the bowler console, the new Urgent Tasks Quick Button blinks in order to warn the operator that an order is waiting for review.

Clicking on it, a window appears displaying all the pending lane orders; the operator clicks on the order they want to process and the POS module opens displaying the items of the lane tab and the items of the order. Here the operator checks the order, modifies it if necessary and confirms it, saving the tab and sending the order to the bar receipt printer and to the kitchen printers (if configured).

In case the order comes from a lane without a tab (e.g. tournament and league lanes), a new tab is created.

In the case the order printout has to be printed again, go to the new plugin under POS > Reprint > Reprint Orders. Here the order and the printer can be chosen.

If the Lane Orders have been chosen as urgent tasks for that terminal, the Quick Button
starts blinking when there is a pending lane order to be checked. By clicking on it, a window opens, displaying all the urgent tasks that the terminal is subscribed to receive. The operator can click on the item they want to manage. When a task is already managed by another terminal, it is specified in the window.

When a terminal is in Safe Mode, the Tasks Requiring Immediate Attention
window is displayed but it is read-only, so that the operator can see it but has to log in in order to process them.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Lane Orders > How Does it Work with BES X?

=========== Conqueror-2-532.html — 12.2  Settings ===========

12.2 Settings

12.2 Settings

Setup > Price Setup > Lane Order Menus

A maximum of 9 different price key groups can be created, and each Center can manage them in the way that best suits its needs.

Press Add Group
, enter the name of the group to be created, select a Picture for it and press OK.

Then Select the Price Keys
that will available in that group: select a Department
and move the relevant price keys to the right-hand side of the screen by clicking on it and pressing on the assign arrow. Price keys coming from a different Department
can be chosen; to do this just select another Department
and move the relevant price keys.

The Department
structure displays only POS departments, because only items belonging to the POS can be ordered directly from the lanes.

Menu Mode

Choose one of the following Lane Order
options:

Complete Menu
- normal mode, for which the price list is utilized on the lanes.

Off
- deactivates Lane Orders for all the lanes, for example when the time of bar or kitchen closure is getting near.

Blinking Bar
- by pressing the Bar
button on the lane keyboard, the upper section of the players' grid flashes and SOS
is displayed in the corresponding lane icon in Lane Status advising the operator of the call (for those not utilizing the price list).

With Bowland it is possible to deactivate the Lane Orders and the relative Bar
key on the lanes through the Lane Options.

Pictures

Prepare a set of pictures in the common formats png, bmp, jpg for categories and price keys; category pictures should be 284x224 pixels; price key pictures should be 284x172 pixels - If the pictures are smaller or bigger, they will be automatically adjusted to be displayed in the best way.

In the case of a price key or a category without any picture, name and price are displayed in the center of the related box.

Display Without Taxes Notice on Console

Select this option if prices contained in the current lane order menu don't include taxes.

If taxes are not included, a Without Taxes
label is displayed near the total price, making customers aware that taxes are to be added.

Skip Amount Tendered

When customers finish making their bar orders they have to specify the amount they will tender, so that the operator can easily prepare the necessary change. Enable this option to skip this step.

Automatically Save in Tab and Print

Enable this option to directly send the order to the kitchen or to the bar skipping the operator review through the Urgent Tasks
window.

In this case, a tab is automatically created upon lane ordering.

Customized Font

Click on the Font
icon in order to customize the layout of lane order receipts.

To remove the customization and go back to the default, disable the Custom Font
option.

Quick Button for Urgent Tasks

Setup > Terminal Setup > Quick Buttons

Here the Lane Orders Urgent Tasks
Quick Button
can be enabled.

ECONOMIC SETUP > Lane Orders > Settings
